# Robes MVP — Claude Code Reference

## ⚠ THIS BRANCH: `beta` — the working branch for beta.byrobes.com
Renamed from `claude/beta-deployment-setup-wdvxb6` (2026-07-12); this is the branch all beta work happens on going forward. It duplicates `signup-flow` and layers the **P0 simplification** (tech handoff 2026-07-11: wardrobe-first utility engine) on top, for live testing on beta.byrobes.com (point the Railway staging service's source branch at `beta`; point it back at `signup-flow` to restore the pre-P0 beta). Deltas vs `signup-flow`:
- **Prompt box is a bare text entry** — pills stripped, and the old photo/"From wardrobe" attach row is gone (beta feedback 2026-07-11); the bundle's typewriter rotating placeholder (its examples in `dashboard-assets/144529f6….js` `PROMPT_EXAMPLES`, now incl. a weekly line) is the sole intent guide. Attachment lives in the **+ menu** (`#cb-addmenu`): Upload / Take a picture / **From wardrobe** (`#cb-addopt-wa` → `__cbWardrobePick`). The bundle photo path feeds `_cbPhotoData` via a document-capture `change` listener on `#cb-file` (capture because `Dash.onPhoto` clears the input) + `_rbDownscale`; a wardrobe pick renders the bundle's `#cb-attached` chip and downscales via the shared `_rbUrlToDataUrl`. `window.__cbClearPhoto` clears state + chip. A photo attachment ALWAYS routes to the 'style' track (`_cbDetectIntent` early-returns on `hasPhoto` — no other track consumes the photo). `_cbSetIntent` survives as the concierge-card scaffold injector (Daily / Weekly cards); a card-armed intent is only trusted while the prompt still starts with the scaffold's static prefix (`_cbSeedPrefix`), otherwise detection re-runs.
- **Weekly Plan track is live and plan-first** (extension of the Daily Look, workflow borrowed from the Travel Edit): the weekly intent always opens the **week planner modal** (`__wkOpen`/`_wkPlanPaint`, `#wk-plan-modal`) — a Mon–Sun calendar (`_wkWeekDays`: upcoming Monday, labels "Monday · 14 Jul") where she assigns activities per day (input + smart chips), leaves a day blank (Robes plans it) or taps **"Leave free"** (`null` in `dayPlan` — NO outfit), with **"+ Plan next week too"** extending to 14 days and "Skip — let Robes plan the days" as the escape. `__wkPlanGo` → `_wkGenerate(brief, dayPlan, weekDays)` → **`POST /api/weekly`** (`WEEKLY_SCHEMA`, gemini-2.5-flash, thinking 0, 12000 tokens; owned pieces render their wardrobe photos, unowned pieces get generated stills since 2026-07-22 — see the Weekly imagery delta): the calendar is injected as an authoritative itinerary (her plan per day = authoritative, free days omitted and re-stamped positionally server-side as `{rest: true}`; `user_activity` carried per day). The result (`__wkRenderResult`, `#wk-result-page`) is the day strip `#wk-strip` (weekday + date, "· your plan" marker, dimmed left-free cards) + a **Daily-parity day console** in `#wk-day` (`_wkPaintConsole`): LEFT "The Look" dark stylist panel (slot-labelled tiles, first wide, hover flick arrows `.wk-tnav`, Anchored badges, "N of M from your wardrobe"), RIGHT "The Rack" (slot eyebrow, provenance, description, **flick-through** cluster + **Anchor** toggle per card). Flicking reuses the Daily helpers (`_dlOptions`/`_dlOptIndex`/`_dlApplyOption`) — option set = served original + owned same-category pieces (travel precedent, no AI alternates in the weekly schema). **"↻ Restyle this day"** and **"✎ The real plan"** / free-day **"✎ Dress this day"** (`#wk-day-modal`, incl. "Leave this day free") post **`POST /api/weekly/day`** (`WEEKLY_DAY_SCHEMA`, 2600 tokens) — a surgical single-day re-mix that holds ANCHORED pieces fixed and gets `weekSummary` for routing continuity; `_wkApplyDay` re-marks anchors on the fresh items. Every mutation (flick/anchor/restyle/free) persists via `_wkPatchSaved` into the saved lookbook entry (`_wkActiveSaveId`; `type: 'weekly-plan'`, `wkData`, label "Weekly plan", reopen via `__snOpenItem` with `{skipSave, savedId}`). `.rb-sfoot` footer: Back to dashboard / **Plan a new week** (reopens the planner with values kept — `_wkPlan` survives). The Weekly Planner card lost its "Coming soon" tag — CTA `_cbSetIntent('weekly')` injects "A weekly plan for [my upcoming work week]"; submit routes to the planner.
- **Intent routing is three-track** (daily / weekly / travel, + key-piece styling): `_cbDetectIntent` gained `weekly` (word-boundary `\bweek\b` never matches "weekend"), lost `moodboard`; precedence piece → travel → weekly → daily (a trip or week span outranks a piece packed inside it). The clarify loop offers the four options; an unroutable legacy intent falls back to clarify, never a blind render.
- **Moodboards fully hidden** behind `var _RB_MB_HIDDEN = true` (declared at the TOP of `__robes_personalize` — early code paths read it): no `#rb-mb` row, no avatar item (dashboard + stylenotes, Style notes slots after Lookbook), `/moodboards` + `/moodboard/:slug` deep links and popstate reopens gated. All moodboard code kept — flip the flag to restore.
- **"Your piece, styled" card is center stage + temporary** — mounted ABOVE the wardrobe tracker (prompt box → card → tracker), and **collapses off the DOM** (animated max-height, then `remove()`) the moment she interacts with it (Add next piece / See the full looks) or types into `#cb-ta`; the tracker snaps up into its slot. The result is already saved in the lookbook (`cardSaveId`). `mount()` guards on `collapsed` so a late paintReady can't resurrect it.
- **Post-add fork modal REMOVED (2026-08-21, Mary's user testing)** — `window.__rbAddFork` no longer exists. It shipped gated to ≥15 pieces, then to <5 (audit round 2, 2026-08-20), but user testing found it still read as an interruption on every early add ("threw a user back into an unhelpful loop of styling a look 3 ways during their first session"). The non-modal add toast (`WA.submit`) now carries the below-5 payoff on its own — see the 2026-08-21 entry below.
- **All four result pages hide their siblings on render** (`_rbHideResultPages(except)`) — they're equal-z-index (40) fixed overlays, so DOM order (not open order) decides stacking; without the mutual hide a stale later-appended page paints over the one just opened.
- **Rollback caveat**: weekly plans sync to `lookbook_items` as `type: 'weekly-plan'`. The `signup-flow` build can't render that type — after pointing Railway back, any weekly rows saved during the test show as "Key piece"-labelled cards that dead-end on tap (harmless; delete the rows or redeploy this branch to fix).

### 2026-09-09 — A day holds many looks: the day page (Annie's wireframe, structural guidance)
Annie's next step after the look clean-up: inside a diary day, a saved look is a THUMBNAIL (her model wearing it, name, piece count), the day holds any number of looks, a look is opened from the day, looks can be added to and taken off the day, and the day's title edits in place. The Day / Evening switcher is gone from the look. All in `dashboard-personalize.js`. Verified: `looks_harness` **351/351** (the switcher pin flipped + a new section 11 with 13 day-page checks at 1280/390; the harness's `planned_days` stub is now STATEFUL — rows the app upserts come back on the next read, as the server's would, because the rail and the day page refetch after every pin and an empty read erased the pin the test had just made), `diary_harness` **32/32** (+3: the card title and a past row open the day page), `ftue_harness` 174/174, `piece_page_smoke` 79/79, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `inspiration_smoke` 28/28. Screenshots at 1280/390 checked by eye.
- **The data: every look on a date coexists.** `_pdFreshest` and `_pdSupersede` exempt `look` and `daily` rows (`_pdMany`) — two looks pinned to one date used to be "the same plan re-planned" and the older one was dropped wholesale. **`_pdDayLooks(rows)` is the one reader of a day's looks**: one moment per source (pinned looks + generated daily looks whose lookbook entry still resolves; trips and the bare `day` name row are not looks), day slots before evening, then by when each was filed (`created_at`, else `updated_at` — an optimistically cached pin has no `created_at` and would otherwise sort ahead of everything). `_pdDayTitle(rows)` is the day's own name: the typed `day` row, else the name a look's row carries for the day (a pinned look's `activity` is the look's own name when unnamed — that is not a day title), else the trip's day title. `_pdMomentLabel(i, n)` derives the moment from the look's place in the day (nothing alone; Day · Evening; Morning · Midday · Evening; Morning · Midday · Afternoon · Evening; five or more carry no label) — there is no stored moment beyond `slot`, which the DB caps at day/evening. `_pdSlots` carries `looks` + `title` per date.
- **The day page (`__rbDayOpen(date, {seed})`, `_dyPg*`)** rides `#dl-result-page` (every closer, the nav sync and the mobile back pill already cover it) and stands every console guard down on open (`__lastDlData` null, the day baseline, the keep-ask). Header: the full date as the eyebrow, the day's title (serif; "Name the day" as a quiet door while unnamed) + the rename pencil, the weather pill (today reads the live `__rbCtx`; another date reads the forecast a generated look stored for it; else nothing), a "Part of *{trip}* — Open the travel edit →" line on a trip date. Then **LOOKS FILED TODAY / LOOKS FILED / LOOKS PLANNED** with "N looks · M pieces filed" on the rule, and the grid: `.dyp-card` = 3:4 image (`_lkHeroUrl` → the render, else her photograph; a generated day's anchor shot via the row's `img`; a kept day look's saved look; else the pieces as the shared mosaic — a pinned look with no frame yet asks `_avRenderKick` for one, "Creating her frame…" while it comes, the landing repaints via `__rbDayRefresh`), LOOK n left · the moment right (+ a Worn ✓ chip), the serif name, "N pieces"; hover ✕ (always shown on touch). The dashed **+ Add a look · another moment in the day** card → `__mvWear(date)` (the one picker; it now reads the rail cache when the Diary isn't open, is headed by `_pdDayTitle`, and the pick refreshes the page). Rows in hand paint first (rail cache ∪ the Diary's month rows via `window._rbMvRows`), then `_pdFetch(date, date)` repaints — the fetch replaces the date's cache except rows written optimistically in the last 15s (a pin whose upsert is still on its way).
- **Verbs**: a card → `__rbDayOpenLook` → `_rbOpenMoment(m)` (the look console, under the day's header, the day's title carried as the occasion); ✕ → `__rbDayRemove`: a pinned look comes off quietly (`_lkUnpin`, toast "taken off {date} — it stays in your Lookbook"), a generated day look goes through `_rbConfirmDelete` (the copy says whether a saved look survives it) → `snRemove`; either way the day's name is written to a `day` row first so it outlives the looks. **Rename is inline** (`__rbDayNameEdit/Key/Commit` — the h1 becomes an input; Enter/blur commit, Escape cancels) through **`_rbDayNameAll(date, rows, v)`, the day page's one write**: every pinned look re-pins carrying the name (empty = the look's own name), every generated look takes it as `occasion_label`, the trip's day title follows, `_pdDayName` keeps the `day` row. The peek's rename and the Diary card's rename now route through it too (a trip day still writes `dayTitles` alone).
- **Openers**: `_rbOpenPlannedDay(m)` opens **the day page** for the moment's date (a trip moment still opens inside its trip; `seed` = the moment, so a day opened for a row the cache hasn't caught up with still shows it); the old body is **`_rbOpenMoment(m)`** — the look itself (`__lkOpenAsDay` / `__snOpenItem`). The rail's Open, the peek's Open the day, the `?d=` deep link, the piece page's wear rows and the Diary's card title / past row all land on the day page; the Diary's **look rows open the look** (`__dyOpenLook`). **The look console lost the Day / Evening switcher** (`dlOccHtml` is empty; `__dlSetSlot`/`__dlDressSlot`/`_dlSibling` survive programmatically) and gained **`.dlm-dayback`** — "‹ Wed 9 Sep" above the eyebrow → `__dlToDay(date)` (through `_dlExitGuard`, so an unkept look or a standing change still asks).
- **Surfaces converged**: `_dcMoments` takes `o.looks` — the rail card's phrase names every look on the day (`d.lookCount`, the ☾ mark for 2+); the Diary card lists one row per look (`_dyLookRow(m, i, n)` with the moment label; a trip day keeps Day / Evening), its title is `_pdDayTitle` (never a look's own name) and opens the day page; the past row sums pieces across looks ("· N looks"); the peek lists "Look n" rows outside a trip and keeps the quick Unpin only for a single pinned look.
- **Review round (Annie, same day)**, `looks_harness` **352/352**, `diary_harness` **35/35**, the rest unchanged-green: (1) **A month cell opens the day page straight away** — `__mvCell` no longer opens the peek (the peek survives on the rail's body tap and `__lkSeeDay`). (2) **From the list you enter the day first**: every `.dy-look` row opens the DAY (`__dyOpen` → the day page, `from:'diary'`), the look is reached from the day page. (3) **The breadcrumb runs look → day → entry point**: the day page carries `.dyp-back` — **‹ Diary** or **‹ Home** (`_dyPg.from`: an explicit `from` from the opener — the rail passes `home`, the Diary's rows/cells/title pass `diary` — else the Diary when it was open; `__rbDayBack` → `__rbDiaryOpen()` / `/dashboard`); **a look opened from the day shows only the look**: a pinned look opens its **look page** (`__lkOpen(id, {from:{label: 'Thu 10 Sep', go → __rbDayOpen(date, {from})}})`), a generated look opens its console with a **reduced header** (`_dlFromDay` — set by `_rbOpenMoment(m, {fromDay})` via `_dlFromDayPending`, read once by the next non-rerender render: the back pill, the Your-daily-look eyebrow, the look's name + rename pencil, the vibe — no date, day title, day pencil or weather; those live on the day page). Elsewhere (`__lkFromDaily`, the piece page's wear rows) the console keeps the day header. (4) **Moment labels are off** (Day / Evening / Morning…): `_pdMomentLabel` survives unused; cards read LOOK n alone, Diary rows carry no prefix. (5) **Taking a look off a day asks first**: `_rbConfirmDelete` gained `opts {sub, yes}` — a pinned look confirms "Take “{name}” off Thu 10 Sep?" · "It stays in your Lookbook — only the day lets it go." · **Remove**; a generated look keeps the delete copy.
- **Gotchas**: the day page's `_dyPg` is nulled at the top of `__dlRenderResult` — a `__rbDayRefresh` after a render must never repaint the grid over a look; `_dyPgRows` lives in the daily module and the peek reads it (same function scope); under a stub that answers `[]` for `planned_days`, the rail's refetch 700ms after every pin wipes the date's cache — the looks harness stub echoes rows for that reason.

### 2026-09-08 — The look, named first: the look page + one composer with two moods (designs `Robes_Look_IA.dc.html` + `Robes_Create_Edit_Look_IA.dc.html`)
Annie's clean-up of the saved look in context (the Lookbook, a day, a travel edit, Home), with a new look unchanged bar the photograph door and a real edit mode for a saved look — every write path is the one that already existed; only where the information sits moved. All in `dashboard-personalize.js` (`_lkDetailHtml` rewritten; `_lkImgActsHtml`, `_lkLivesHtml`, the `_lkDy*` diary sheet, `__lkDRemove`/`__lkDAddPiece`/`__lkDAddOpen`, `__lkBackDoor`/`__lkFromTrip`; `_rbConsole` gained `cfg.boardExtraHtml`, `_lkModelPanelHtml` gained `o.canvasExtraHtml`). Verified: `looks_harness` **327/327** (sections 3/4/5/8 + the nothing-owned and card-Wear pins rewritten to the new anatomy; the 390px overflow pin now measures the detail column itself), `ftue_harness` 174/174, `piece_page_smoke` 79/79, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `diary_harness` 29/29, `inspiration_smoke` 28/28 (re-run on the rebased tree after the Diary review round). Screenshots at 1280/390 checked by eye.
- **The look page (reading)**: **back to the door she came through** — `.rb-lk-back` reads the label of `_lkFrom` (`__lkOpen(id, {from:{label, go}})`): the grid → "Lookbook" (`__lkBack`), the home rows → "Home" (`__lkCardOpen(id,'home')`), the daily view's "Look details →" → the day ("Tue 8 Sep", `__lkFromDaily` reopens the saved row, else `__lkOpenAsDay` on the pin), a trip's imported look → **"Travel edit"** (new `.tvm-lksrc` "Saved in your Lookbook — Look details →" on the trip stage, `__lkFromTrip` → `__snOpenItem(_tvActiveSaveId)`), the piece page → the piece's name. Supersedes the 2026-08-08 "no sub-sub-nav back line" pin. The header is eyebrow **Saved look** + name + pencil — **the wear verbs left the header** (`.rb-lk-actrow`/`.rb-lk-calbtn`/`.rb-lk-wearbtn` are gone): **the diary lives ON the image** (`.rb-lk-diarybtn`, white circle bottom-left, never on a look she owns nothing of) and **the camera bottom-right** (`.rb-lk-photobtn` — hover names it "Add your photograph"; once her photograph exists the slot is replace on the You view only, and the **You / Model switch moves INSIDE the card** as `.rb-lk-viewrow` with "Kept as the record of this look"; the "Wore this look? Add your photograph…" line sits under the card until then). The card is the standing panel: image · note · tags + Edit. **The rack reads** ("The rack · N pieces" + the outlined **Edit & resave** pill; rows carry the piece's wear count). **The wear record reads the way a wardrobe piece prints it, AFTER the rack** (`.rb-lk-worn`: "Worn ——— three times" + " · €350 a wear" when priced pieces exist, the dated rows as white cards — `_lkFmtDay` "Tue 8 Sep" · the `_lkWearDiff` line · chevron, each opening its day (`__lkSeeDay`, or the daily row a `source:'daily'` wear names) — then **Worn today** (the tap IS the wear; settles to "Worn today ✓ · Not this, actually") and **Add a date** (the retro row). **The stat ledger (Wears / Last worn / Pieces / Saved / Per wear) is retired** — the rule line and the rows carry it. Lineage + **Delete this look** close the page. The pinned reminder strip survives above the card.
- **The diary sheet (design Two)** — `__lkDiaryOpen` (`#rb-lkdy`, z-950; bottom sheet ≤767px): the look's thumb + name under "Put in the diary", a Monday-start month (‹ ›, `__lkDiaryNav` refetches via `_pdMonth`), **filed days carry a rose dot** (diary rows in cache + the fetched month, this look's pins and wears), any date one tap (`__lkDiaryPick`), the chosen day in words above the pill ("Today, Tuesday 8 September" / "… · something already filed" / "… · records it as worn"). **`__lkDiaryGo`: today/future = `_lkPin` (the reminder strip is the confirmation); a past date = `_lkAddWear({source:'retro'})`; already pinned = opens the day.** "I wore this today" = `__lkWearToday`. Replaces the Today/Tomorrow/date panel (`_lkActNote === 'pin'`); `__lkAct('pin')` and the card's Wear pill (`__lkWearAsk`) open the sheet.
- **Editing a saved look = the composer's frame (design Two)** — `_lkEditMode || dirty` renders `.rb-lk-composer.rb-lk-editing` under eyebrow **Lookbook / Editing**: the look as it stands (a DIRTY draft always draws the board — a photograph or render of the look as saved cannot show the change), the editable rack (flick / Swap / **✕ takes a piece out** — `__lkDRemove` on the draft — / the **+ on a role strip and the trailing + Add a piece** — `__lkDAddOpen` aims the A2 chooser at the draft via `_lkAddTarget = 'detail'`, `__lkDAddPiece` lands the pick, Snap a new piece included), **"Where it lives"** (`_lkLivesHtml`: The lookbook ✓ always, every upcoming pinned day ✓ → the day peek, every travel edit carrying it ✓ → the trip, and a dashed "+ Put it in the diary" → the sheet), **the change bar stands from the first second** (`.rb-lk-editbar`: "No changes yet. Swap, add or take a piece out and this line tells you what happens to its wear." → "One change to this look. Its 2 wears stay with it if you update." · Discard (rose) · Save as a new look (only once dirty) · **Update this look — the one ink fill**; `__lkResave` with no draft simply closes the editor), Delete centred at the foot. No image controls and no wear record while editing — they belong to the look page. **Flagged, not built**: the design's "This day only · sits in the diary, not the lookbook" destination (a day-only look has no data home — `looks` rows are the Lookbook) and "Let Robes pick" per role (Annie: a new look changes nothing but the photograph door); no Share on the look page (a `looks` entity has no share path — `__rbShare` publishes `lookbook_items`).
- **A new look changes one thing**: **"Add your photograph" is a pill ON the canvas** (`_lkImgActsHtml({camera:'add', label:true})` via `canvasExtraHtml`, bottom-right; hidden on the no-model prompt, which carries its own photograph door) — `_lkModelRowHtml` now renders only once a photograph exists (You / Model + "Kept as the record of this look") or while it uploads. **Her model still loads on a new look by default** (the avatar cell, untouched). `.rb-lkm-replace` moved to the design's 38px white circle bottom-right.
- **A day holds the SAVED VIEW of its look (Annie, same day: "the day still holds the old view — it should hold a saved view of the look while retaining the header")**: in `__dlRenderResult`, a day wearing a saved look UNTOUCHED (`dayLook && !dlLoose && !dayChg.n && !_dlDayEdit`) renders `.dlm-console.dlm-saved` from **`_dlSavedViewHtml`** — the look page's own panel via **`_lkLookPanelHtml`** (the ladder extracted from `_lkDetailHtml`, now the ONE renderer of a saved look as saved: her photograph → the render → her model on a zero-piece look → the board; `occHtml` under the head, `actionHtml` closing the panel + the mobile share badge) with the day's **Day/Evening switcher** and **Share** on it, and the look page's **reading rack** (`_lkDetailItems(l, {saved:true})` — never a draft left open on the look page — rows carrying wear counts, names opening the piece via `__dlLookPieceOpen`) under the day's verbs **✓ Wore it · ✎ Adjust this day · ↻ Restyle this day**. The header above is untouched (date eyebrow, the day's name + pencil, weather, "Wearing", the Look-details door, the vibe). **Adjust this day** (`__dlDayEdit`) opens the console exactly as before — flick / swap / anchor and the rules-05/06 local-change machinery are unchanged, and a standing change keeps the console on its own; **Reset to the saved look** lands back on the saved view. `_dlDayEdit`/`_dlLookView` (the You/Model switch) reset on any render that is not a `rerender` (`_dlRerender` passes `rerender:true`). `looks_harness` **331/331** (four saved-view pins in the rules-05 section; the weather pill is not pinned — it reads the live forecast, absent under the stub).
- **Cohesion pass (Annie's live review, same day — "one experience, adapting to where she views the look from")**, `looks_harness` **334/334**, `piece_page_smoke` 79/79 (its dress pin now reads the held card), the other four unchanged-green: (1) **the saved look is HELD, reading and editing** — `.rb-lk-held` (the composer's and the day console's exact dress, a distinct class because code queries `.rb-lk-composer` for the composer; `.rb-lk-held .rbc-panel` merges the panel into the card) wraps `.rb-lk-con` in both modes; the wear record sits inside the card after the rack, lineage + Delete under it. (2) **"Worn today / Not this, actually" is gone** — today's wear files from the diary on the image ("I wore this today"); "Add a date" is the one retro door under the record; `__lkWearToday`/`__lkUndoToday` survive programmatically. (3) **No standing confirmation banners** — `_lkDone` is never rendered; `__lkResave` ("Updated"), `__lkPromoteAsk` ("Saved as its own look"), `__lkRetroPick`/`__lkDiaryGo` ("Recorded — worn …") toast via `_waShowToast`. (4) **The pinned strip has a ✕** — one `.rb-lk-pinstrip` per pinned day, "Open the day →" + `button.x` → `__lkUnpinDay(iso)` → `_lkUnpin` (repaints; zero pins left deletes the planned_days source). (5) **The "Lookbook / Editing" eyebrow is gone** — editing keeps the Saved look eyebrow; the held card + the change bar say edit. (6) **The day's saved view is the look page's card**: the camera on the image (`__dlLookPhotoAdd` → `_lkPhotoPick` → `_lkPatch(look, {photo_url})`, kept on the LOOK), the You / Model row + "Wore this look? Add your photograph…" note, **the tags row edits the LOOK** (`__dlLookTagsEdit/Apply` through the shared `_lkTagsApplyTo(l, t, surface)` — `__lkTagsApply` is now a thin wrapper), no diary button (this IS the day); the rack head is the look page's register — "The rack · N pieces" + **✓ Wore it** (`#dl-wear-btn`) + ONE edit door **"Edit this day"** (↔ "Edit & resave"); Restyle lives inside the edit console's head with the rest of the change machinery. (7) **Share is off every look surface for now** — the day's saved view (`actionHtml: ''`), the day console (`lookActionHtml` only carries Save on an unkept loose look, `shareBadge:false`) and the travel console; `__rbShare` + the header `#rb-share-btn` survive. Look dates print months by table (`_lkFmt`/`_lkFmtLong`/`_lkFmtDay` → `_rbMon3` — newer ICU printed "Sept."). **Flagged, not changed**: the day's `__dlTagsEdit` (the DAY's tags in the edit console) still writes `data.look_tags` only — the saved view now edits the look, so a day's tags and its look's can drift once she adjusts the day.
- **Second review (Annie, 2026-09-09)**, `looks_harness` **338/338**, the other five unchanged-green: (1) **the pinned strip's ✕ closes the strip and nothing else** — `_lkStripHidden[id|date]` (session) via `__lkStripHide`; it had unpinned the day (the pin comes off in the day peek and Where it lives; `__lkUnpinDay` survives programmatically). (2) **A day is only a date — the day's change flow is the look page's** (supersedes rules 05/06's "this day only" model): the `.dlm-adjusted` banner is the look page's `.rb-lk-editbar.dlm-editbar` ("One change to *{look}*. Its N wears stay with it if you update." · **Discard** → `__dlDayReset` · **Save as a new look** → `__dlDayPromote` · **Update this look** — the ink fill → new `__dlDayUpdate`, which commits the day's owned pieces onto the SAVED look through **`_lkCommitPieces(l, pieces)`** — the ONE commit path `__lkResave` also uses — resets `_dlDayBase` and lands back on the saved view; a rack holding an unowned flick toasts why it cannot update). The rule-06 question (`_dlExitGuard`, from ✓ Wore it, the bar's Save as new, and leaving) is **Update this look · Save as a new look · a quiet third** — "Discard the changes" on a navigation exit (resets, then goes), "Cancel"/"Not now" otherwise (closes, the question returns); **"Just keep it on the day" is gone**. The changed row's chip reads "Changed". (3) **Editing dresses her model live, not the mosaic** — `_lkLookPanelHtml` takes `o.editing`: with a model on file the editor renders the composer's canvas (`_lkModelPanelHtml({saved:true})` over the draft ids, `_lkmSync`'s debounce + key cache), **seeded from the saved look's own frame** (`_lkmSeedFromLook` — `render_key === _lkmKey(saved ids)` → `_lkmRenders[key]`; a render already on its way from the detail open marks `_lkmBusy` so the editor waits for it, and `_avRenderKick`'s landing frame writes into `_lkmRenders` + repaints the stage — never two renders for one composition); `_lkCommitPieces` reuses the live frame (`_lkPatch({render_url, render_key})`) else kicks. The day's edit console does the same through **`_rbConsole` `cfg.boardHtml`** (the model canvas over the day's owned ids while every rack piece is hers; the board carries an unowned flick); `_lkModelEnsure`'s late repaint now also re-renders an open day. (4) **The count lives on the rack alone**: look head "The look" (the panel + the day's saved view), the day console head "The day"/"The evening"/"The look", racks "The rack · N pieces"; `.rb-lk-sec.rb-lk-rackhead` reads in `.rbc-lhead .lab`'s register (9px · .22em · ink-faint). (5) **A day wearing a saved look carries the look's tags in its edit console too** (`__dlLookTagsEdit`) — `__dlTagsEdit` only ever edits a generated day now. Look dates print months by table. **Flagged**: the travel console (Annie is taking it in upcoming work).
- **Gotchas**: the stacked `.rb-lk-con` track is `minmax(0,1fr)` (a bare `1fr` let the nowrap wear rows widen the column past 390px — the standing "never widen cells" rule); `__lkTitleCommit` is guarded on `_lkTitleEditing` (the repaint removes the focused input, whose blur re-enters the commit mid-innerHTML and threw); `.rb-lk-imgacts` overlays the `.rbc-board` (position:relative, like the share badge) with `pointer-events:none` on the layer and `auto` on the buttons — `.rb-lookv2 .rbc-tile{pointer-events:none}` still holds; the `.rb-lk-viewrow` rides the lookv2 panel's flex order at 9. `_lkFrom` is only ever set by a door — a lineage link (`__lkOpen(id)` bare) reads "Lookbook".

### 2026-09-08 — The Diary is the fourth tab: plans out of the Lookbook (design `Robes_Diary_IA.dc.html`, phased 1→4)
Annie's IA ask: a clean four-tab system separating the Diary (plans) from the Lookbook (looks), the travel edit relocated into the Diary, the Diary as a vertical list, and a trip header carrying dates · location · weather. Built in four pushed phases so each could be tested live on beta; the design was structure + copy guidance, every surface re-registered in the beta's own tokens. Look cards untouched (Lookbook: nav structure only). Home, Wardrobe and Inspiration untouched (placeholders in the design). Verified: `looks_harness` 319/319 (segment + travel-row pins rewritten), `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `ftue_harness` 174/174, new **`scripts/diary_harness.mjs` 29/29**, `inspiration_smoke` 28/28, `stylenotes_model_harness` 232, `addflow_harness` 105, `onboarding_harness` 103, `piece_page_smoke` green.
- **Phase 1 — the nav is Lookbook · Diary · Wardrobe · Inspiration on BOTH breakpoints** (`#rb-tn-diary`, `#rb-dock-diary`; Style notes' dock copy mirrors it). **The mobile dock keeps Home (Annie's call) and tightens to five tabs** — 20px glyphs, 9.5px labels, `.04em` tracking, 4px gutters, `white-space:nowrap`. `_rbNavSync` derives `diary` from `#sn-page` + `rb-cal-on`; a detail overlay lights **the list it was opened from** — `window._rbNavOrigin` (`'lookbook'` | `'diary'`), stamped by `__snOpen` / `__rbDiaryOpen` / `__tvRenderResult`, also read by the mobile back pill (`#rb-backpill-label` reads Diary or Lookbook) and `__rbNavGo('back')`. **The Looks | Diary segment inside the Lookbook is retired** (`#sn-viewseg` gone with its CSS and the zero-looks `.inert` rule — the Diary opens at any look count); the Diary keeps riding the `#sn-page` shell underneath so nothing remounts, with its own eyebrow (`#sn-eyebrow` flips Diary/Lookbook in `_mvSetView`) and crumb. `/diary` (+ `/calendar` alias), deep link and popstate were already there.
- **Phase 2 — travel edits left the Lookbook**: `_lkPaint` paints no `#rb-lk-hol` row (the div is gone from `#rb-lk-wrap`), `any = streamN` (a trip-only account meets the composer — intended), the stat line counts looks alone, and the `+ New ▾` split is replaced by ONE **`+ New look`** button (`__lkNewMenu` survives with its one item, unreached). **The Diary's month header carries the + menu** (`.rb-mv-add` → `__rbDiaryAddMenu(ev, date?)`, popover `#rb-dy-addmenu`: "Add a look · Lookbook · N looks" → `__mvWear(date)` / "Add a travel edit · Where are we packing for?" → `__lkNewHoliday`). **`__lkNewHoliday` no longer closes the page** — the intake opens OVER the Diary (cancel leaves her there; sub-line "It files itself into the diary, on those dates."), and **`__tvRenderResult` closes `#sn-page`/`#rb-insp-page` itself** (the standing higher-z rule) and stamps origin `diary`. Trip page: back reads **← Diary** (`__tvGoBack` → `__rbDiaryOpen`), and **Delete this trip lives on Edit details** (`#tv-ed-delete` → `__tvDeleteTrip` → the named `_rbConfirmDelete` → `snRemove` → back to the Diary) since the row's ✕ is gone. `_lkHolidayRowHtml`/`_lkHolCells`/`_lkHolMeta` + the `.rb-lk-hol*` CSS are dead code kept for one release. Home's Lookbook row still composes travel edits (Annie: ignore Home this pass).
- **Phase 3 — the LIST is the Diary's default** (`_dyMode` 'list' | 'month', localStorage `rb_diary_mode`; `.rb-mv-seg` icon toggle in the head; month view keeps the DayCard grid + caption "Month reads the shape of it. List is where you plan."). `_dyListHtml(g, rows, sources, today)` renders the month from **the SAME `_pdMonth` rows and `_pdWinner` winners the grid reads** (one data path, no freshness gap): **invitation rows for today + the six days after while empty** (`.dy-invite`: pencil + "Name the day" input + a dated `+` → the same menu), later empties fold into the tail ("Nothing filed for the rest of {Month}." + "{Next month} ›"); **a dressed/named day is a card** (`.dy-card`: her title `activity` + pencil, else "Name the day"; one `.dy-look` row per moment — thumb, name, ✓ "N pieces", "Evening ·" prefix — tapping opens the day through `_rbOpenPlannedDay`; `+ Add a look` → `__mvWear(date)`); **a travel edit is a block** (`.dy-trip`: header title · `_rbDateRange` · chevron → `__snOpenItem`, weather line "Lahinch, Ireland · 13–19°C · *passing showers*" from `tvData.weather`, then `.dy-tday` rows with a weekday/numeral/month gutter, her title italic (inline rename), look rows, `+ Add` on an undressed day → opens the trip on that day); **the past files quietly** (`.dy-past`: "Filed · N pieces" + a Worn chip). **Inline rename is `_rbDayRename`, the one write path** (`__dyRename(date, key)` → `#dy-name-in` → `__dyRenameCommit`; trip days write `dayTitles`, daily days `occasion_label`; soft repaint via `_rbDayRepaints`). **Naming a BARE day keeps the name IN PLACE** (Annie, same day, supersedes the "her words go to the prompt" rule for this surface: "it can be used to plan a week before adding outfits"): its data home is a `planned_days` row of its OWN source — `source_type: 'day'`, `source_id: 'day:<date>'`, one `day` slot, `activity` = her words, no migration (`source_type` is free text, `source_id` has no FK) — written by `_pdDayName(date, v)` (empty name → `_pdDeleteSource`), bottom tier in `_pdTier` so any plan landing on the date outranks it and the name resurfaces if the plan leaves. `__dyInviteKey` Enter writes the row, pushes it into `_mvRows` and soft-repaints — the invitation becomes a named card with + Add a look. `_rbDayRenameTarget` returns the day row when nothing outranks it; renaming a plan on a date that also holds a typed name keeps both in step (`window._rbDayNamed`). **The look she adds inherits the name**: `__mvWearPick` passes the picker's title into `_lkPin(id, date, label)`, `__mvRobes` lands it in the empty prompt as the brief; `_rbOpenPlannedDay` on a `day` row opens the picker; the rail tags it "Planned". `diary_harness` **28/28**. **Empty month → the design's empty state** (`.dy-empty`: "Nothing planned *yet.*", the line, **Plan a trip** — the one dark fill — then the week's invitations). Gutters and ranges use `_rbMon3`/`_rbDateRange` (3-letter months always — newer ICU prints "Sept"). ≤767px the head tightens (24px title, 28px controls) so the month stays on one line.
- **Phase 4 — the trip page's header and its Travel diary** (`__tvRenderResult`): `_tvMastMetaHtml` now renders **the design's fact card** (`.tvm-facts` in `#tv-mastmeta`, max 520px): **Destination** and **Dates · N days** rows open Edit details, a read-only **Forecast** row (temp · *condition*; "Reading the forecast…" until `_tvFetchTripWx` lands — `_tvPaintMastMeta` repaints it in place), then the vibe pill (`.tvm-tag`, → Edit details). The `.tvm-wx` pill is gone. **THE WEEK is the "Travel diary"** — `#tv-weekstrip.tvw-grid` is a **vertical list** (one bordered cream block, max 760px) of the SAME `.tvw-card`s (`__tvDayTap`, `.sel` ink accent, `__tvDayTitleEdit/Key/Commit`, `.tvw-pin` + Look, `.tvw-hint`, `.tvw-pinstage`, the spark all unchanged): `.tvw-g` gutter (weekday / numeral / month from `_tvDayInfo`), `.tvw-b` body with the italic title, then **each pinned look as a `.tvw-lk` row** (thumb via `_tvLookHero` → first entry's photo/still, name, ✓ "N pieces"; "worn again · N changes" note kept) — the names phrase + swatches retire from THIS surface only (the rail/month DayCards keep `_dcLookPhrase`). The Diary's trip block and the trip page share one anatomy. Looks grid, stage/console, capsule bar (its `n / N packed` stat was already the design's) untouched. Not built: the design's "+ Tags" pill (the vibe pill already opens the same modal) and the "Travel looks · packed, no day yet" list (the looks grid + Pin-to-a-day bar already carry it).
- **The prompt's + menu carries "Add a travel edit"** (Annie, same day; `#cb-addopt-tv`, after Add a look, sub-line "Where are we packing for?" → `__lkNewHoliday` — the intake, then the trip page which files itself into the Diary).
- **Live-pass review (Annie, same day)**: (1) **Lookbook header** — `+ New look` sits inline with the page eyebrow (`#sn-headact`, the right slot of `#sn-headrow`; `#rb-lk-bar` and the stat line are gone) and the count reads beside the section label ("All looks · *31 looks*", `.rb-lk-allcount`). (2) **Diary head hierarchy** — the `+` sits beside the DIARY eyebrow in the same `#sn-headact` slot (`_mvSetView` fills it per view), the month title row keeps ‹ ›, and the **List | Month switcher sits below the title** as the app's text segment (`.rb-mv-modebar .rb-mv-seg`, cream well / white active — the retired `#sn-viewseg` register). (3) **No chevrons on the list** (the trip block header and the tail's next-month link lost theirs). (4) **Cards are white on hairlines** — the platform's card register: `.dy-card`/`.dy-trip`/`.dy-past` and the trip page's `.tvw-grid` are `#fff`, look rows inside them `--cream-100` on a hairline, the trip block header `--cream-100`. (5) **The airplane is the travel glyph** (the lucide plane path — the Diary + menu, the prompt + menu, the trip block header). (6) **Trip page: ONE pen** — the title's rename pencil is gone and Edit details is the pen beside the title (`.rb-rename-tbtn.tvm-editbtn[aria-label="Edit details"]` → `__tvEditDetails`, which carries the title); fact values read in the page's serif (`.tvm-fact .fv`, 17px; day count italic); the fact card and the Travel diary run full width ≤767px.
- **Not built this pass (phase 5, optional)**: the design's day-rename sheet (Dinner / Work / Party / Away chips) and the assign-a-look sheet (Today / Tomorrow / date; Robes styles one / Build a new look / Assign an existing look) — the peek rename and `__mvWear` picker serve both today.
- **Gotchas**: the three regression harnesses reload `dashboard-personalize.js` on every boot — never edit it while a harness runs. Newer ICU returns "Sept" from `{month:'short'}` — format months by table wherever a width matters.

### 2026-09-07 — The piece is its own page: two doors, one editor (IA fix step 1, design `Robes_Piece_IA.dc.html`)
First step of the IA fix: a wardrobe piece drills down to its OWN page (`#rb-piece-page`, the `_pc*`/`__rbPiece*` module in `dashboard-personalize.js`, CSS `#rb-piece-style`), reached from a LOOK or from the WARDROBE, with the edit consistent between them. Verified: new **`scripts/piece_page_smoke.mjs` 71/71** (both doors, the editor, star, replace-photo, wear rows, rail, build tile, Style-three-ways prefill, the daily door, the wishlist, history back/forward, the `/piece/:id` cold load, 390px); regression-green `looks_harness` 319/319 (on the rebased tree, after the 2026-09-03 Look Builder commits), `ftue_harness` 174/174, `addflow_harness` 105/105, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `inspiration_smoke` 28/28. Run harnesses here with `CHROME_PATH=/opt/pw-browsers/chromium-1194/chrome-linux/chrome` (playwright's own download is not installed in the container).
- **Two doors, one address**: `window.__rbPieceOpen(id, {from:'look'|'wardrobe'|'wishlist', lookName, siblings, quiet})`. The page is a fixed overlay at **z-46** — over the Lookbook/Inspiration pages (45) and the result overlays (40), under the mobile dock (48) and the nav (50) — with its own sticky header (back pill + right cluster), a 640px reading column, and `/piece/:id` as its URL (server route + `_rbNav` push on open, restored to the previous path on close; popstate + a boot deep link reopen it as the wardrobe's record; boot polls until the wardrobe AND wishlist have loaded, then falls back to the wardrobe). `__rbPieceCtx()` tells `_rbNavSync` which tab to light (wardrobe/wishlist doors → Wardrobe; a look door leaves the surface underneath to decide) and stands the nav's mobile back pill down (the page carries its own). `__rbPieceHide()` rides `__rbCloseResultOverlays`, `_rbNavArch`'s `_closeOverlays` and popstate.
- **From the wardrobe = the full record** (design Three): back pill "Wardrobe", the Hero-Rack star in the header (`__waHeroToggle`), eyebrow IN YOUR WARDROBE, serif title + the pencil, italic brand, a white card (photo with a **replace-photo** button → `#rb-pc-photoin` → `_rbDownscale` → `/api/wardrobe/upload` → PATCH `image_url`; the italic note; tag chips = season band + wear-for labels via `_tgWearOf`/`_tgLabel`), then the **Worn rule** (count in words — `_pcTimes`: once/twice/three times…; `max(times_worn, wear rows)`), the three most recent wears as date + look rows (from `_lkLooks[].wears`, the wear's `piece_ids` SNAPSHOT deciding membership) with "View all N wears →" toggling the rest, **"In N looks"** (saved Looks first — meta "Worn {date}" / "Pinned to a plan" (planned_days cache, source_type look, future) / "Not yet worn" — then daily looks + travel edits from `snLoad()` whose `wardrobe_match`es hold the piece; tiles compose `window._rbLookTile` per the handoff rule, sized 126×168 in the rail) closed by the dashed **"A {sixth} look · Build it from this piece"** tile (`__rbPieceBuild` → `__lkNew()` + `__lkApplyNew(id)`), and the one full-width ink **Style it three ways** (`__rbPieceStyle` → opens Inspiration + `__inStyleNew({piece})`).
- **From a look = preview and company** (design One): the row's NAME (and its picture) on every shared rack is the door — `_rbcRow` renders `.rbc-name` as a `.rbc-namebtn` button whenever the surface passes `cfg.onPiece` and the item carries `pieceId` (owned rows only; `cfg.pieceCtx` rides as a second arg where a surface needs addressing). Wired: the saved Look detail (`__lkPieceOpen`, read AND edit mode), the composer (`__lkCPieceOpen`), the daily console (`__dlPieceOpen`, headline as the way back) and the travel consoles (`__tvPieceOpen(i, 'li:di')`). Back pill = the look's name; header pager "n of m" + ‹ › walks the look's OWNED pieces (`siblings`); the card leads with "{Category} · Worn {n} times"; no wear ledger, no build tile, no CTA — the rail then **"See the full record in your wardrobe →"** (`__rbPieceRecord` reopens the same page through the wardrobe door). Back simply hides the page — the look is still underneath. Textcontent of `.rbc-name` is byte-identical (the button holds the name alone), so name-based harness matches still hold.
- **The editor is ONE modal from either door** (design Two): the pencil → `_waOpenEdit(it)` — the existing wardrobe edit form unchanged (The piece / Brand / Category / Subcategory / Colour / Season / Wear it for / Notes / Update piece, "Styled in N looks →", "Remove from wardrobe"). `__rbPieceSync()` repaints the page in place (scroll kept) after `_waLoad` (a save), a hero toggle, a wishlist remove, or closes it when the piece is gone (a delete, a wishlist buy). `__wtrkEdit(id)` stays the programmatic edit-by-id hook (harnesses use it); the **wardrobe grid card now opens the piece page, not the editor**.
- **Style it three ways arrives filled** (design Four): `__inStyleNew({piece})` renders the key-piece card in the tile's place (photo, "Your key piece", name, brand · category · colour, Change → `__inStPieceClear`), fetches the wardrobe photo into `_inStPhoto` via `_rbUrlToDataUrl`, flips the placeholder to "The occasion, the weather, how you want to feel in it…" and the hint to "Robes already knows the {shirt} and how you have worn it. A few words is plenty." `__inStGo` leads the prompt with "Style my {label} three ways (worn N times)" + her words — a bare submit runs. The result saves as a key piece (Inspiration), never the diary.
- **The wishlist follows the same format** (no design frame — same header/card/foot): back pill "Wishlist", eyebrow ON YOUR WISHLIST (rose), no pencil and no replace-photo (a wishlist row has no editor yet — flagged, not guessed), provenance chip + price + category where the tags sit, the note, then **I bought this** (ink, → `__wlBought`; the close that follows lands on All pieces) and the quiet rose **Remove from wishlist** (→ `__wlRemove`'s confirm). The `.rb-wl-item` card is the door; its two buttons stop propagation.
- **Live-pass amendments (Annie's IA_Update, 2026-09-08)**: (1) **the photo is shown whole** — a centred 3:4 frame (`width:min(100% - 32px, 390px)`, the grid card's ratio) with `object-fit:contain`, never a landscape crop; (2) **the replace-photo button is the Look photograph's** (`_LKM_REFRESH_SVG` in the same 28px cream pill as `.rb-lkm-replace`); (3) **the wear rows read from EVERY place a wear is written** (`_pcWears`): the Look entity's wears, the daily looks she marked worn (`dlData.worn` + `anchor_date`, opening the day via `__snOpenItem`) and the diary's worn `planned_days` rows (cache, opening via `_rbOpenPlannedDay` through `__rbPieceDayOpen`), deduped by date + source, newest three by default — wears that only ever landed on `times_worn` (accrual declined) have no date, so a quiet line names them: "{N} wears counted before Robes kept the dates."; (4) **the "In N looks" rail holds SAVED Looks only** — a trip or a day is not a look (the day still reaches the page through its wear row). (5) **the card wears the Look panel's dress** (Annie, same day: "exact same as the look") — `.rbc-panel`'s container (`--rad-lg` corners, 0.5px `--rule-mid`, 18px pad), `.rbc-lhead`'s header register, the photo at `--rad-sm` corners, the note in the lookv2 `.rbc-quote` register (serif italic 300, 15px/1.62, ink-soft, NO rule above), then `.rbc-tags`' hairline and its filled `--secondary` pills — harness-pinned by computed style against the panel underneath. `piece_page_smoke` **79/79**.
- **Gotchas**: `_pcCtx` is a `var` (TDZ rule); an unkept fresh daily look's exit guard (`_dlExitGuard`) intercepts `__rbNavGo` from under the piece page as designed — the harness boots fresh for the wishlist section rather than answering it; Playwright `innerText` returns CSS-uppercased text ("1 OF 4") — assert case-insensitively.

### 2026-09-03 — Look Builder: dressing your model (designs `Look_Builder__dressing_your_model.dc.html` + `Look_Builder__no_model_yet.dc.html`)
The composer's left column is now **her model on a canvas**, not a mosaic board: she stands in beige basics from the first second, and picking from a rack slot dresses her immediately. Verified: `looks_harness` 319/319 (composer + 390px + empty sections rewritten against the photographed canvas — cell first, one render per settled composition, Save reuses the frame), `ftue_harness` 174/174 (Robes-door checks → no-model canvas checks), `stylenotes_model_harness` 232/232, walkthrough screenshots at 1280/390. All in `dashboard-personalize.js` unless noted.
- **The canvas (`_lkModelPanelHtml`, `.rb-lkm-canvas`, 4:5)** replaces the `_rbConsole` board and the dashed "The look, once you start." stand-in on the HAND-BUILT composer (a Robes build keeps its console — proposals and the stylist note live there). Three states in one frame: her photographed model (`.rb-lkm-stage`), her own photograph of the look (`.rb-lkm-photo`), or — with nothing on file — the invitation to build one (`.rb-lkm-prompt`: dashed outline, NO MODEL YET, "Build her once, and she'll model all your looks.", the one ink pill **Build your model**, "Or start from your own photograph"). The eyebrow counts what she has on ("The look · N pieces").
- **THE REAL MODEL, PHOTOGRAPHED — never a sketch** (Annie, same day: the design's figure was a stand-in; production renders the actual avatar). Empty rack = her avatar cell (`POST /api/avatar/cell`, her in the base layer — the beige basics — fetched the moment the composer opens, `_lkmCell`); every rack change re-renders her wearing what is on it through **`POST /api/avatar/render`** (`_lkmSync` → `_lkmRender`): **debounced `_LKM_DEBOUNCE` 1800ms** so flicking through options asks for one frame, **cached by the SAME render key `_avRenderKick` uses** (`_lkmKey` = avatar id | sorted piece ids, `_lkmRenders`), polled through `GET /api/images/:jobId` (`_lkmPoll`, first read at once then 4s, 180s ceiling). **The previous photograph stays up while the next one is coming** (`_lkmShown`) under a "Dressing her…" / "Creating your model…" chip (`.rb-lkm-busy`, pronoun-aware); a failed render (`false` in the cache) leaves it standing, no chip, no retry loop. Landed frames paint INTO the stage (`_lkmPaintStage`, every `.rb-lkm-stage[data-ids]`) — never a composer repaint, which would take the caret out of the name field. **A hidden canvas spends nothing**: `_lkmSync` defers a tick and asks only for a stage with client rects (the Lookbook paints its composer while hidden at boot and on the early empty-lookbook arming — that paint used to burn a real cell call against the server and cache the failure); a failed cell retries after a 30s cooldown (`_lkmCellFail`). The saved row carries the reused frame through `_lkPushCloud`'s `after` → `_lkPatchCloud({render_url, render_key})` (the migration-21 degrade covers it). **Save reuses the canvas frame**: `__lkSave` passes `render_url`/`render_key` into `_lkCreate` so `_avRenderKick` no-ops on the key match — she is never rendered twice for one look. Server: `/api/avatar/render` takes **1–12 pieces** (a single piece renders "worn over the plain fitted base layer"; was 2–12) at **12/min** (was 6). `_lkModel`: undefined = not asked (bare warm frame until `_avFetchId` answers), null = no model, else `{id, man}`. Cost note: one image call per settled composition — the debounce and the key cache are the levers.
- **The saved look carries the same door** (Annie, same day: it was missing on the detail): `_lkDetailHtml` draws the photograph row under the panel — **Add your photograph** (`__lkDetailPhotoAdd` → `_lkPhotoPick`, the shared downscale + `/api/wardrobe/upload` helper → `_lkPatch(id, {photo_url})`) or, once one exists, the **You / Model** switch (`__lkDetailPhotoView`, `_lkDetailView`, photo first; Model = the render, else her cell on a zero-piece look, else the mosaic). A Robes build with proposals gets no row — its `photo_url` is the lead still, not her photograph. The grid card keeps the render-first ladder.
- **A saved look on a trip shows her model too** (Annie, same day): `_tvLookHero(li, di)` resolves an imported trip look's `lookId` → `_lkFind` → `_lkHeroUrl` (render → her photograph), and BOTH the LOOKS card (`_tvPaintLooks`, the mosaic's `photo`) and the stage (`_rbConsole` gained `cfg.heroUrl`/`heroAlt` — ONE 4:5 photograph in place of the board, `.rbc-hero`; the rack beneath is untouched) draw it — only while the trip still wears the look exactly as saved (piece-id set match against the day's effective entries); a day-scoped swap or add hands the surface back to the pieces. `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **The photograph is a one-way door**: `__lkPhotoToggle` adds or REPLACES, never removes (the ↻ button on the photo replaces). Once one exists the row under the canvas becomes a **You / Model** switch (`__lkPhotoView`, `_lkShowPhoto`); the Model side with nothing built opens the builder. Row copy: "Add your photograph" + "Wore this look? Add your photograph and it is kept alongside her" → "Kept as the record of this look". Pronouns follow the model on file, else the profile (`_lkModelPro`: her/him/them).
- **No model → the rack stays open** under a hairline notice (`.rb-lkm-notice`: "Add pieces now if you like. They stay on the rack, and your model wears them the moment she exists."). **Build your model parks the draft** (`__lkBuildModel` → sessionStorage `rb_lk_draft` {rows, seq, name, tags, roles, photo, home} + `rb_model_return` 'lookbook'|'home') and lands on `/stylenotes`, where the pill reads **Back to your look** and the ‹ back pill returns to `/lookbook` (`stylenotes.html`); the dashboard boot restores the draft (`__lkDraftRestore` — on `/lookbook` after `__snOpen`, on home after the 1700ms beat, opening the Build-your-own row).
- **Save is gated on the NAME alone — the two-piece floor is GONE**: `__lkSave` has no piece floor (an empty rack saves), `_lkNewHtml`'s `canSave` is just `!_lkBuilding`, and `.rb-lk-save` is never disabled for pieces — it reads **cream (`.unnamed`) until the look is named, ink after**, with the note beside it (`#rb-lk-namegate`, `.rb-lk-savenote`: "Name your look and it is yours to keep." → "Filed under X."), both flipped in place by `__lkNewTitleInput`. The masthead name-gate note is retired. A saved zero-piece look opens honestly: "Nothing on it yet." panel, "Nothing hangs here yet." rack, and her cell photograph on the panel (`_lkModelPanelHtml({saved:true})`) — never the aspirational-build "Not yours yet" copy, which is now gated on proposals.
- **"Or let Robes create your look" / "Or let Robes build the first one" is GONE** from the composer footer (`.rb-lk-robesdoor`) — the home prompt box is where Robes builds, with more to go on. `__lkRobesBuild` survives for Try another / Build from mine only.
- Not touched (Annie is taking it separately): the `+ Add a piece` rows and chooser.

### 2026-09-01 — Your Model shows the PHOTOGRAPH + presence moves onto the page + the male catalog (avatar iteration, design `Your_Model__avatar_iteration.dc.html` + Annie's SKIMS reference)
Three founder asks in one pass: the model page shows the ACTUAL avatar (not the abstract SVG), gender selection moves from Account details onto the model page, and the male catalog goes live end to end. Verified: `stylenotes_model_harness` **204/204** (photo stage, presence row, male ids, by-hand path — sections rewritten in step), `ftue_harness` 174/174, `looks_harness` 316/316, `node --check` server + personalize.
- **The stage is the photograph** (`.mv-photo` in `#mv-figframe`): the rail renders the ACTUAL avatar catalog cell — fetched via the new **`POST /api/avatar/cell`** (`{avatarId}` → `{url}` when the cell exists, else `{jobId}` + lazy `avatarCellEnsure` generation polled through the standard `GET /api/images/:jobId`; rate 20/min; concurrent asks for one cell share a generation via `avatarCellInflight`). Client `mvPhotoSync/mvPhotoFetch` runs off every `renderModel`: **debounced 900ms** so flicking through swatches asks for one cell, sequence-guarded so a stale response never lands, 180s poll ceiling, "Photographing…" chip (`.mv-photo-busy`) while in flight. The SVG figure SURVIVES as the pre-photo ghost + instant-feedback underlay only — a failed fetch leaves it standing, never a broken image. Caption (`#mv-caption`) names the colouring in colour words only ("Sand · espresso hair" — `MV_SKIN_NAMES`/`MV_HAIR_NAMES`; the no-body-type-language rule holds).
- **The cell imagery is the SKIMS register** (`avatarCellGenerate`, art direction per Annie's reference): a seamless fitted base layer in one soft neutral tone close to the skin (woman: scoop-neck stretch tank + fitted mid-thigh shorts; man: plain fitted crew-neck tee + fitted knee-length shorts), **barefoot, hair worn down** (men: short hair), warm-grey studio. **Cell storage keys are versioned separately from avatar_id** — `avatarCellKey(id)` = `id + '@v2'` (`AVATAR_CELL_STYLE`): bumping the style constant invalidates every cached portrait while `avatar_id` stays byte-stable in profiles/render_key/the client mapper. Old cream-vest-and-jeans cells are dead keys, never served.
- **Presence lives with the model now** (supersedes the Account-details field, migration-13 column unchanged): the adjust sheet leads with a **Presence** row (Woman / Man / Prefer not to say → `profiles.gender_identity`) that **persists IMMEDIATELY on pick** (identity, not styling — skin/hair still commit on Save) and re-files a kept model under the new catalog. The Account details modal keeps a one-line pointer to Style notes; `__acctGender*` handlers are deleted, `_rbGender()` stays the one reader. All model copy is pronoun-aware (`mvP()` — Keep her/Keep him, Save her/Save him, "He's on file…"); 'unspecified' rides the woman catalog, mirroring the render surfaces.
- **The male catalog is live end to end**: `avatar_id` gains a gender prefix — `parseAvatarId` accepts `^[wm]-…` (all legacy ids are `w-`), `AVATAR_FIG_WORDS_M` + male descriptor/base-layer branches in the cell prompt, and the render prompt / `AVATAR_IDENTITY` went gender-neutral-or-parametrized ("the model… the SAME man/woman"). **Every man-blocks-the-avatar guard is gone**: `_avRenderKick`'s early return, `/api/daily`'s `g === 'man' ? null`, `/api/style`'s `wearer === 'woman'` gate — all three now call **`avatarRefForUser(avatarId, userId, gender)`**, whose new prefix gate is the one rule: the resolved cell's catalog must match the wearer (a menswear brief on a woman's profile — or a stale opposite-prefix id after a presence flip — falls through to the generic model until the next keep). `_avLocalId` (the pre-migration-20 mirror) prefixes from `prefs.gender` else `_rbGender()`.
- **Shaped by hand needs no photograph** (design 1a "Chosen by hand" — also the male path's only path, since the analyse pipeline is woman-tuned): the adjust door is ALWAYS open ("Shape your model by hand" until anything exists, then "Not quite her/him?"), any sheet pick marks `st.model.touched`, and a touched model is keepable with zero reads (status "Shaped by hand. Add a photograph whenever you like — the model updates."). `avatar_prefs` is now v2 (`{skin, hair, nudges, kept, gender, v:2}`); v1 prefs load cleanly (gender null → profile value).
- **Gotcha caught by the harness**: `.mv-keep`/`.mv2-ey` (and anything uppercase-transformed) return the RENDERED case from Playwright `innerText()` — assert labels case-insensitively.
- **Run migrations 20 + 21 to make this stick across devices/restarts**: pre-20 the kept model (and its gender'd id) lives in localStorage only; pre-21 cells live in the server's in-process cache, so every Railway deploy re-photographs the first model shown (~30s wait + ~10¢, cosmetic but visible).
- **Second pass (same day) — the page restructured to the design's 2a/1a anatomy** (Annie: "update the UX to be the same as the designs"; harness rewritten in step, **210/210**). The step-numbered layout and the side adjust sheet are GONE:

### 2026-09-01 — Onboarding step 01 is ARCHETYPE FIRST (design `Onboarding_Step_01__Archetype_first.dc.html`)
Step 01 restructured to the archetype-first design; the shared step chrome re-registered on BOTH steps so nothing deviates between them. All in `onboarding.html`. Verified: **`onboarding_harness` 101/101** (step-01 section rewritten to the new anatomy; both breakpoints).
- **The ten archetype cards are the step's first decision** (`ARCHETYPES` + `.ac-grid`, 3 cols desktop / 2 ≤560px; `renderIcons` is now `renderStyle`): eyebrow "Style", h1 "Where does your *style sit?*", step chip **"Step 01 · Style"**. A card prints its **full descriptor only once selected** (`.ac-desc` — never on an unselected card); selected = the app-wide `#F3EFE6`/`#C9BCA6` convention with a filled ✓ mark (`.ac-mark`), second tap clears. Multi-select, no floor.
- **Icons and brands demoted to a refinement** behind a hairline (`.ic-refine`, label "Icons and brands"): the one-field chips-in-field anatomy, helper row, and pool survive byte-for-byte in behaviour, but the old sub copy moved into the empty input's placeholder ("Add the people and brands whose style inspire you" → "Add another…"), the tally is empty at zero (the "Three is plenty to start" line is gone with the sub), and **the pool seeds from her picked archetypes** (`ARCH_SEED` — a Minimal pick surfaces The Row/Phoebe Philo/Lemaire ahead of the house list; the typeahead searches the seeded source too).
- **A skip never survives an answer — now on EITHER axis**: `syncSkip` withdraws `#ob-skip` while `st.archetypes.length || st.icons.length`, and it returns only when both empty (harness pins the cross-axis hold).
- **No ink fills on the step's chips (Annie, same day)**: a chosen token in the field wears the warm selected fill (`#F3EFE6`/`#C9BCA6`, `.chip-solid` — was ink-on-dark), the pool chip hover previews the same fill instead of going dark, and the × is ink-faint — **the single ink fill on the screen is Continue** (the app-wide black-fill-means-the-one-commitment rule, restated here so it can't regress; harness pins the token's computed fill).
- **Archetypes persist on `profiles.style_dna.style_archetypes`** (jsonb — no migration; merged, never replacing analysis fragments; only written when there is something to record or clear) and prefill on boot, like icons.
- **Shared chrome aligned to the mock, both steps**: the "01 / 02" count is gone (a spacer keeps the step chip centred), progress rail is 2px on `--cream-200`, eyebrow 400/10px, title `clamp(32px, 4.6vw, 42px)`, headline-block→content gap 28px, field/dropdown/cap-panel radii moved to `var(--rad-sm)`. **Step 02's header follows the pattern** — eyebrow **"Wardrobe"** (the step label's section word; supersedes the 2026-08-18 "Your first piece" eyebrow) and title **"Add your *first piece.*"** with the same serif-italic accent as step 01 (supersedes the roman-one-line call, which predates this direction). Sub copy, capture flow, skip discipline untouched.

### 2026-08-25 — Style Notes → Your Model: one entry page, Taste & budget split out (avatar phase 1, design `Style_Notes_to_Your_Model.dc.html`)
First build slice of the avatar work (`docs/avatar-render-proposal.md` + `docs/avatar-design-brief.md`; branch `claude/avatar-gemini-vision-o8wovd`). `/stylenotes` is restructured from three tabs to the designed single-entry page — **"Two photographs. One model."** — plus a live model rail; the render pipeline (catalog imagery, look renders) is NOT in this slice. Verified: new **`scripts/stylenotes_model_harness.mjs` 158/158** (both breakpoints: empty/colour/both/kept states, keep-on-colour-alone, adjust sheet, doors, taste entry, degrade, analyse flow, mobile order), `ftue_harness` 174/174.
- **The subnav tabs are gone; the page is two views on one URL**: `view-model` (default) and `view-taste` (`/stylenotes#taste` — Taste & budget's own entry point, reached from the avatar dropdown's new **Taste & budget** item on the dashboard AND this page's own menu; hashchange routes live). Legacy `#silhouette` deep links (the dashboard `_rbSilPrompt` card) land on the model page. Chrome (topbar, weather, dock, save-state, logout) untouched.
- **The model page**: left column = steps 01 (close-up) / 02 (full-length) — small 112×150 photo slots (same `headshot-slot`/`full-slot` ids, `wireSlot` unchanged, each step's dark "Add photo & read" button pokes the same input), guide copy → **reduced inline results** on read (colour: season + italic summary + 9-swatch strip + Undertone/Contrast facts; line: archetype + one italic sentence — the reduced-silhouette call). **Step 02 dims but never blocks** until the close-up reads (existing silhouette-only accounts still render). Right rail (sticky desktop; compact header card ABOVE the steps ≤767px) = **the model**: an abstract SVG figure (skin/hair/dress polygon — no imagery generated on this page, deliberately: the photoreal model arrives with the render pipeline), Harmony/Line facts, status line, **Keep her** + "Not quite her?".
- **DECIDED (Annie, design round 1)**: (1) **Keep her is live from the close-up ALONE** — colour read = keepable on a neutral figure; a later line read upgrades the figure silently (a kept model re-persists on every fresh read). Supersedes the mock's `canKeep = hasLine` gate. (2) **Two figure nudge pairs only** — Softer/Straighter + Fuller/Narrower; Longer/Shorter cut (it would multiply the avatar catalog matrix; second tap clears a nudge). (3) **The full chapters live behind quiet doors on the page** — "Her full colour notes →" / "Her full line notes →" toggle the existing chapter sections (18-shade palette/neutrals/best/avoid/metals; traits/dresses/necklines/tips) wholesale; nothing was deleted, and the analyse pipeline + profile writes are byte-identical.
- **The adjust sheet** (`#mv-sheet-wrap`, z-400; right sheet desktop, bottom sheet ≤767px): 8 skin swatches (warm→cool, deep→fair) + 5 hair swatches + the two nudge rows; picks repaint the figure live; **Save her** keeps + closes. Gotcha shipped-and-caught by the harness: the wrap's `display:flex` beat the `hidden` attribute (the documented inline-display-beats-hidden class) — `#mv-sheet-wrap[hidden]{display:none}` is the fix, keep it.
- **The mapper is deterministic, no LLM**: skin/hair = nearest catalog hex to `style_dna.color_harmony.extracted_values.skin_tone_hex`/`hair_color_hex` (fallbacks s3/h1), figure = `body_type` → internal keys `hg/pe/re/it/ap` (`nt` neutral) — **the archetype names never render**; her explicit picks (`avatar_prefs.skin/hair`) always beat the proposal.
- **Migration 20** (`supabase/avatar_migration.sql`, **NOT yet run on Robes_p0**): `profiles.avatar_id text` (the resolved cell, e.g. `w-s3-h1-hg-fr`) + `profiles.avatar_prefs jsonb` (`{skin, hair, nudges, kept, v}`). Until it runs, `mvPersist` flips `_mvAvatarCols` on the column-named error and the model lives in localStorage `rb_model__<uid>` only (single-device) — keep still works, one console warn.
- **Not in this slice** (next: the render pipeline per the proposal §3.4): the avatar catalog imagery + `scripts/gen_avatar_catalog.mjs`, `/api/avatar/render`, `looks.render_url` (migration 21), the validation harness renders. The old try-on backfill code CLAUDE.md's Style Notes section describes (`pollTryon`, `rb_tryon_*`) had already been retired from the page before this pass — `/api/stylenotes/tryon` remains server-side and unwired, and is the prompt scaffold for the render endpoint.
- **Second pass (same day) — the render pipeline (avatar phase 2)**: a saved Look renders as ONE photograph of her model wearing it. Verified: `looks_harness` 316/316 (display-ladder change is regression-free), `node --check` both files; live validation runs via `scripts/avatar_render_smoke.mjs` against beta (the keys live on Railway).
  - **Server `POST /api/avatar/render`** (rate 6/min): body `{avatarId, pieces[2..12: {name, category, color, brand, image_url|null}]}` → `{jobId}` immediately, then ONE `gemini-3.1-flash-image` call in the background — parts = **the avatar cell reference image + up to 9 Cloudinary garment photos + a text manifest** (IMAGE-numbered for photographed pieces, described for the rest; 1+9 stays inside the model's 14-reference budget), tryon's identity-lock register + `FULL_BODY_FRAME` + warm-grey studio. 60s race, one retry after 8s, Cloudinary-hosted, `logAI` feature `avatar_render`; polled via the standard `GET /api/images/:jobId`.
  - **Avatar cells generate LAZILY, once ever**: `avatarCellEnsure(id)` — parse `avatar_id` → prose descriptor (`AVATAR_SKIN/HAIR/FIG/NUDGE_WORDS`; **keep in sync with stylenotes' MV_* mapper**) → one text-to-image reference portrait (plain cream-vest base layer, front-facing) → Cloudinary → `avatar_cells` row via the service key (in-process Map cache when the table/key is missing — a restart just regenerates a cell, ~10¢). **v1 deviation, flagged: lazy cells are used without a curation pass** — the /admin approval queue arrives with scale; the reference image never renders in the UI, only the looks worn on it.
  - **Migration 21** (`supabase/avatar_render_migration.sql`, **NOT yet run on Robes_p0**): `avatar_cells` (authenticated-read RLS, service-key writes only) + `looks.render_url`/`render_key`. Client degrade: `_lkRenderCol` PGRST204 strip-and-retry in `_lkPatchCloud` — pre-migration the render lives in the local cache only.
  - **Client**: `_avRenderKick(l)` fires at `_lkCreate` and on any pieces-carrying `_lkPatch` — guards: ≥2 resolvable owned pieces, not already busy, `render_key` (= `avatar_id|sorted piece ids`) unchanged → never re-renders, `man` gender → mosaic stands. `_avFetchId` reads `profiles.avatar_id` lazily (pre-migration-20 fallback: localStorage `rb_model__<uid>` prefs + the mirrored `_avLocalId` mapper). Poll 4s/300s → `_lkPatch({render_url, render_key})` → repaint (`_lkPaint` unless the composer is open, `_lkHomeSync`). **Display ladder is `_lkHeroUrl(l)` = `render_url` → `photo_url` → mosaic**, applied at every surface that draws a saved look (grid card, detail hero, home first-look card, home Lookbook row, both `lt.mosaic` picker sites).
  - **Renders trigger at the commitment moment only** (save / edit-&-resave); existing looks render when next edited — no bulk backfill (cost decision; Batch API is the lever when one is wanted). *Amended same day*: a look-detail OPEN also kicks (bounded backfill — only looks she opens, `render_key`-cached); a Robes build's **proposals ride the render** with their generated stills (an aspirational "1 yours, 3 to find" look renders too; a true render outranks the build mosaic on the detail, but a zero-owned build's lead still stays IN the mosaic — `looks_harness` pins this); server retries are 3 with widening backoff (a Gemini demand spike returns prose instead of a frame — the prose now lands in Railway logs as `said:`), verified by `scripts/avatar_client_smoke.mjs` (11 checks, real-dashboard boot).
  - **Third slice (same day) — her model wears the generated frames too**: `avatarRefForUser(avatarId, userId)` (explicit body `avatarId` wins, else `profiles.avatar_id` via the service key — **migration 20 is load-bearing here**: a localStorage-only kept model never reaches these surfaces) + `AVATAR_IDENTITY` (same woman, new scene, reference backdrop explicitly ignored). Wired into **`/api/style`** (all three look frames — the same woman across all three occasions, scenes still vary; an attached key-piece photo becomes "the SECOND image") and **`/api/daily`** (the Anchor's full-look frame only; still-lifes stay garment-only). Resolution happens INSIDE the background image IIFE so a first-ever cell generation never delays the text response. `man` wearer → generic model as before. Travel hero + moodboard frames deliberately not yet converted.

### 2026-08-21 — Mary's user testing: fork modal removed, "Dress today" pill arms the prompt (UserBrain session 1)
First UserBrain testing session run against the 2026-08-20 build surfaced two frictions the code-only audit hadn't caught. Both in `dashboard-personalize.js`. Verified: all six harnesses green — `addflow_harness` 105/105, `ftue_harness` 174/174, `looks_harness` 316/316, `onboarding_harness` 71/71, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `inspiration_smoke` 28/28.
- **The post-add fork modal (`window.__rbAddFork`) is GONE, not just gated.** Mary: it "kept throwing a user back into an unhelpful loop of styling a look 3 ways during their first session." The <5-piece first-session gate from the 2026-08-20 pass narrowed *when* it fired but not the complaint — a modal offering "Style it 3 ways" / "Build a look around it" after every early add still reads as an interruption to someone who is mid-catalogue, not asking to be styled. The function and both call sites (a fresh wardrobe add, and the wishlist "I bought this" path) are deleted outright. `WA.submit`'s non-modal add toast now carries the payoff the fork used to own at every count below 15 (the old `nNow < 5` carve-out — "the fork IS the payoff there" — is gone): "Added — N piece(s) filed. Each one replaces a borrowed piece in your looks."
- **The home "Dress today" suggestion pill arms the prompt instead of generating blind.** `_ikPillsPaint` (`#rb-sugg`) used to call `__dlSubmit('An outfit for today')` directly on tap, so — Mary — "it automatically goes straight to styling a day with no prompt or guidance." It now calls `_cbSetIntent('dress-me')`, the same scaffold-inject path every other dress-me door already used (the Daily card CTA, the onboarding handoff, the rail's empty-day scope) — the prompt box fills with an editable template, caret on the first bracket, and she sends when ready. The `chip-dress` template itself is reworded to Mary's own suggested shape — `"I'm going to [the office] and want to feel [chic]"` (was `"An outfit for [Sunday brunch]"`) — giving a place AND a mood to edit; it's shared by every `_cbSetIntent('dress-me')` caller. `_cbSubmit`'s bracket-strip (`/\[([^\]]*)\]/g`) already handles multiple `[…]` pairs, so an unedited send still runs on the defaults per the standing "brackets run with their defaults" rule.

### 2026-08-20 — Audit round 2 (Annie's live re-test, Bugs__Issues.md + the "Add your pieces" mock)

- **Add step 1 IS the batch promise (3.3 mock)**: h "Add your pieces." / "One or twenty. Robes reads the colour, the cut and the label — and fills in the rest.", dropzone = stacked-photos glyph (`.rb-wf-glyph`) + serif "Drop in as many as you like" (desktop) / "Add as many as you like" (≤767px) + "Robes files them one after another." Desktop: dark **Choose photos** + quiet "or <u>take a photo</u>" link (`.rb-wf-orcam` → the `#wa-rb-cam` input); mobile stacks dark **Attach photos** over outlined **Take a photo**. The `.rb-wf-batch` chip is gone (the zone carries the promise now).
- **The fork modal is a FIRST-SESSION payoff (supersedes the ≥15 gate)**: `__rbAddFork` fires only while the wardrobe holds **fewer than 5 pieces**, and only on a direct catalogue (the `_waAfterAdd`-armed skip already covers swap/snap-mine) — Annie's screenshot showed it interrupting a 20-piece user mid-swap. The 3.1 payoff toast now covers 5–14 (below 5 the fork is the payoff, no toast doubling).
- **Wardrobe L1 tabs never wrap on mobile**: `.wg-filters .wg-tab` joined the `flex-shrink:0;white-space:nowrap` mobile rule (dashboard.html) — the row scrolls horizontally like the pills always did.
- **The swap suggestion is honest or absent**: the `aiAlt = _waItems[0]` fallback (pink barrel-leg jeans offered for a silver cuff bracelet) is gone — a cross-category "similar outline" only exists for **Outerwear↔Tops**; otherwise, when she owns nothing in the category, the modal shows a **From your wardrobe browse-all grid** ("Nothing filed under {category} yet — pick any piece, or snap the one you have in mind.", 8 + count note) so the wardrobe is always pullable (6.2/6.3 — her pick is deliberate).
- **"{A} meets {B}." names the FIRST Robes build only**: `_lkBuildTitle` skips the icons name once any saved look already carries it (case-insensitive) — subsequent builds take the occasion-shaped offer.
- **A filled role strip carries a quiet ⊕ (4.1)**: `_rbRackRolesHtml`'s strips gain `.rbc-stripadd` (SVG glyph — NEVER a text `＋`, strip textContent must stay byte-identical for every consumer that matches strips by text) whenever `cfg.onRoleAdd` exists and the role holds a piece — a second Canvas piece no longer needs drag-and-drop (web-only, undiscoverable). Pre-casts via the same `onRoleAdd(role)` convention as the empty definition rows.
- **The name gate answers at the CLICK (4.2, supersedes disabled-until-named)**: `canSave = enoughPieces` — Save is live once the pieces suffice, and `__lkSaveAsk` opens with a name guard: unnamed → scroll + focus `#rb-lk-newtitle`/`#rb-lk-hometitle`, toast "Name your look and it's yours to keep", nothing written (before any wishlist confirm can stack). The masthead namegate note + `.rb-lk-savegate` piece-floor note still render.
- **The keep-ask lands HOME (5.3 follow-up)**: `_dlKeepAskModal`'s quiet answer/backdrop closes the look and routes `/dashboard` (page hidden, crumb cleared, `_lkHomeSync`) — proceeding with the original tap landed on Inspiration or the wardrobe depending on which exit raised it.
- **A trip can be deleted from the Lookbook row**: `.rb-lk-holcard` is a div (button-in-button was invalid) carrying the grid tiles' hover-`.rb-lk-rmx` ✕ → `__snRemoveTrip` → `_rbConfirmDelete('Delete this trip? Its looks and packing list go with it.')` → `snRemove` (planned_days swept via the standing delete path).
- **An unpinned look can leave the trip**: the look-scoped stage head's slot that held nothing (pinned looks show Unpin) now offers **"Remove from this trip"** → `__tvRemoveLook(li)` — confirm names the look, splices `looks[li]` (per-look state rides the object, so the splice is safe), clears selection, `__tvPinSync` + stage repaint + `_tvPatchSaved`. Capsule pieces stay (they may serve other looks).
- Deliberately untouched this pass: image quality of Robes' suggestions beyond the category rule (model-side), the DnD affordance itself (the ⊕ sidesteps it).

### 2026-08-19 (second pass) — First-session audit fixes (docs/beta-ftue-ux-audit-2026-08-19.md, Annie's pick of the findings)
Seventeen fixes from the screenshot-driven first-session walkthrough, all in `dashboard-personalize.js` unless noted. Verified: `ftue_harness` 174/174 (band + weekly-card pins updated in step), `looks_harness` 315/315, `addflow_harness` 105/105 (link-door pins → hidden-door + batch-chip), `travel_console_smoke` 102/102 (stage pins → hidden-section), `inspiration_smoke` 28/28, `onboarding_harness` 71/71.
- **kp guide band copy is ownership-honest (2.1)**: "…build it around what's yours — Robes borrows the rest." (was "build it from your wardrobe", a lie at one piece).
- **kp Build-this-look generates imagery (2.2)** — `noImages: true` removed from `_kpBuildLookRun`'s `/api/daily` post (Annie: the image should always render; the blank tiles read as an LLM failure). Belt: every daily frame with no image and no live job renders the piece's **monogram tile**, never the broken-image glyph — `frameBits`' fallback + `_dlSettlePlaceholder` (name via `__dlCurrentItems` by `image_index`).
- **Add toast carries a payoff below 15 (3.1)**: non-batch, non-edit adds toast "Added — today's look is now N piece(s) yours, (4−N) borrowed." (<4) / "Added — N pieces filed. Each one replaces a borrowed piece in your looks." (4–14); ≥15 keeps the bare toast (the fork is the payoff there).
- **The From-a-link coming-soon door is GONE from add step 1 (3.2)** — `__waLinkSoon` survives doorless (the `__wgPack*` pattern); `.rb-wf-linkrow` CSS kept.
- **The batch promise is a chip (3.3)**: `.rb-wf-batch` — bordered pill with a stack glyph, "Add several at once — Robes files them one after another" (was a faint sub-line).
- **Composer role strips carry plain-word asides (4.1)**: `_RB_ROLE_HINTS` (Canvas · tops & basics / Anchor · the hero piece / Texture · a layer with depth / Exclamation Point · shoes, bag & finish) rendered as `.rbc-rolehint` serif-italic beside the strip name, ONLY where `cfg.roleHints` asks — the composer's `rowCfg` passes it; generated consoles are untouched (byte-identity for DayCard/rack elsewhere holds).
- **The disabled Save says why on screen (4.2)**: `.rb-lk-savegate` note beside the button ("Add two pieces and this look is yours to keep." / built variant) — the title attribute was invisible on touch. The name gate keeps its own masthead note.
- **The whole FTU row is a tap target (4.3)**: `__rbFtuRowTap` on `.rb-ftu-row` — toggles unless the click was inside `.rb-ftu-head` (already handled) or `.rb-ftu-body`.
