- **The vibe-read line shows once per user (5.1)**: `_dlVibreadOn` — localStorage `rb_vibread_seen__<uid>`; stable across rerenders of the same look (keyed on the data object); after that the vibe chip alone carries change-the-vibe (it was already the same button).
- **Leaving an unkept fresh look asks first (5.3, Annie's call — narrows the 2026-08-17 "nothing is lost by leaving" stance)**: `_dlExitGuard` gained a second branch — on NAVIGATION exits only (`opts.keepAsk`, passed by `__dlGoBack` + the nav/wordmark wrap; never the wear path or internal transitions), an unkept FRESH generation (`_dlKeepArmed === data`, armed on non-skipSave renders; `_dlNoLook` still true; asked once via `_dlKeepAsked`) gets ONE modal — "Keep this look?" with day copy ("The day is written in your diary, but the look isn't in your Lookbook yet") or loose copy ("It isn't saved yet — leaving lets it go"); Save → the offer's own `__dlSaveAsk` flow (wishlist confirm included), quiet answer proceeds. Reopened days never ask.
- **Swap-suggestion grammar (6.1)**: "You don't own any {category} yet, but your…" (was "a outerwear") — both the shared and moodboard modal sites.
- **Swap modal saves to the wishlist (6.2)** — the "Shop via Affiliate →" coming-soon dead end is replaced by **Save to wishlist** (`__rbSwapWishSave` → `_wlSaveFromItem` on the modal's `_rbSwapItem`; flips to "✓ Saved", already-wishlisted renders inert, owned pieces get no second button — Snap mine runs full-width). The rack card's own Save STAYS the primary (Annie's caveat answered: both share `it.wishlisted`, double-save no-ops; daily passes `afterWish:'__dlWishSync'` so the card syncs). The "Opens {retailer}" line reads "Sold at {retailer} · {price}" (no button opens it any more). `__rbAffiliateSoon` survives doorless.
- **Weekly planner card promises the day-chip reality (7.1)**: desc "Plan the week a day at a time — name tomorrow, Robes dresses it, and the diary keeps the week.", CTA **"Start with tomorrow →"**, note "Each day styled from your own wardrobe." (was "Your week mapped day by day… Seven looks in one pass. Nothing worn twice." — selling the ADR-001-deleted engine; the seven-looks copy returns only when a week-scale flow does).
- **The rail's invitation is one worked example, not six clones (7.2)**: `_dcCard` gained `opts.inviteSub` (undefined = canonical line, '' = title only, string = custom) — the rail gives the FIRST plannable empty day "name the day — try “dinner with mum”" and quiets the rest; other surfaces unchanged. Relative eyebrows stand alone ("TODAY" / "TOMORROW" / "YESTERDAY", no date — they ellipsized at seven columns and the neighbours carry dates).
- **"Pack me for…" runs the engine (8.1)**: the intake's travel commit detects a pack/style/dress verb in her prompt and auto-fires `__tvRobesStyleTrip()` 350ms after the canvas trip renders (its own overlay + already-styled guard hold); a bare "plan a trip" intent keeps the canvas-first landing.
- **The travel stage stands down while empty (8.3)**: `#tv-sec-stage` hides whenever nothing is selected (was: shown with a dashed "Nothing on the stage" box whenever looks existed) — the LOOKS eyebrow already carries the tap hint.
- **Trip-page fill discipline (8.4)**: `.tvm-addbtn` ("+ Look", "+ Add pieces") demoted to ghost pills — "✦ Robes styles the trip" keeps the one dark fill.
- **The typewriter demos her wardrobe (X.1)**: the bundle's typewriter reads `twList()` = `window.__rbPromptExamples || PROMPT_EXAMPLES` lazily each cycle (`dashboard-assets/144529f6….js`); `_waSyncCounts` feeds it her two most recent pieces ("Style my {label} three ways", "An outfit around my {label} for the weekend") + two generic lines once anything is filed (labels ≤34 chars).

### 2026-08-19 — FTUE follow-ups: the kp guide band, the keep lands home, the concierge from session one (Annie's beta testing + the 2B mock)
Three corrections from live first-time testing. Verified: `ftue_harness` **174/174** (concierge-gating sections rewritten + a new guide-band block), `looks_harness` **315/315** (new guided-landing check), `inspiration_smoke` 28/28, `onboarding_harness` 71/71, `addflow_harness` 105/105, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **The 2B guide band on "Your piece, worn three ways"** (mock "Alert band · Guidance without a gate"): the kp result's FIRST landing (style track only, never dress-me) carries `#kp-guide-band` between the nav and the page — band cream `#F2EEE7` / `#E1DACB` hairline so it reads as page furniture, not a warning. Anatomy: START HERE eyebrow · "Pick one of the three looks below and build it from your wardrobe." + serif-italic "Or head home to catalogue your wardrobe and start a look of your own." (app spelling, not the mock's "catalog") · dark **Build a look ↓** pill (scrolls to card 01 — whose per-card Build this look is THE one filled button on the page while the band is up, via `kp-guide-on`/`.kp-build-first` + `!important` over the inline outline styles) · underlined **Home dashboard** (→ `__kpGoBack`, does NOT dismiss) · ×. **Dismisses on × or on the first Build this look; never returns** (`rb_kp_guide_done__<uid>`, localStorage) — because it doesn't gate, it survives a reload within the first session, which a modal shouldn't.
- **Saving a LOOSE look lands her back on the dashboard** (`__dlSaveKeep`): the keep was the console's one commitment, so after the toast a 600ms beat closes the overlay via `__rbNavGo('home')` + `_lkHomeSync` — she lands on home (now wearing the O7 first-look card and the concierge band) instead of a page whose one action just completed. Scoped to `_dlLoose && !anchor_date` and never on the wear-first keep (`_dlWearAfterSave` — the wear still needs the console); a DAY look's save stays on its day (Share / Wore it belong there), and `__lkSave` keeps its documented grid landing (2026-07-30).
- **The Styling Concierge loads from the FIRST session** (`_rbGateConcierge` — supersedes both the 3-piece floor and the 2026-08-18 FTU-rows stand-down): `show = !#rb-styled && !_rbConciergeDone()`. The band is the menu of what Robes does, so it stands at any piece count, beneath the FTU index rows too (`_rbFtueOrder`'s rows branch appends `.services` after the rows, every mode) — the ONE thing that delays it is "Your piece, styled" holding the screen: the click through to **See the full looks** retires the card, which is what brings the band in; no piece filed in onboarding → no card → it loads straight away. The 2a retire condition (one of each live edit) is unchanged. At 0 pieces the filed row paints receipt-less (note + CTA only, harness-pinned).
The home look-refinement register (R1 headers, white cards on hairlines, quiet controls) carried onto the Lookbook page, which had drifted cluttered — and the Travel Edit row rebuilt to the mock's "one column width" card. Verified: `looks_harness` **308/308** (mosaic + travel-row checks rewritten), all five other harnesses unchanged-green.
- **`_ltMosaicHtml` is ADAPTIVE — no empty half, ever**: the shared LookTile mosaic sizes its grid to the LIVE cells — 1 piece fills the frame (`.rb-lk-mos.n1`), 2 split it vertically full-height (`.n2`), 3 give the first a 2-row span (`.n3`), 4+ keep the quartet. Empty filler cells only ever render on a genuinely piece-less look (the 0-piece fallback quartet). Every mosaic consumer inherits this (grid cards, travel cards, pickers) since it's the one primitive.
- **`.lt-card` re-dressed to the home card language**: white on the page, 1px `--rule`, 3px radius, the mosaic held at a SQUARE 1:1 window, info block `14px 18px 18px`, serif 22px/400 titles. Card eyebrows (`LOOK`) keep the small-caps register.
- **The Travel Edit row is the mock's 386px card** (`_lkHolidayRowHtml` rewritten + new `_lkHolCells(item)`): a 3×2 capsule mosaic (`.hmos` — piece imagery by `wardrobe_match.image_url` → `generatedImages[image_index]`, tone-tile fallbacks from a fixed cream/sage/mauve set; the trip's hero `img` leads the first cell when no piece imagery exists; sixth cell is a quiet serif `+N` overflow count), then serif 23px 2-line-clamped title + 11px meta ("12 pieces · 7 looks · 31 Jul – 4 Aug"). The row gets its own R1 header (`.rb-lk-shead`: TRAVEL EDIT eyebrow + right-aligned "All N" / "None packed yet" over the hairline) and **bleeds past the page gutters** (negative `--s6` margins + matching padding) so the next card peeks — the scroll affordance IS the peek. **The trailing dashed "+ New travel edit" card is retired** (the header + "+ New ▾" carry adding).
- **1b empty state**: the invite is a white card ("Plan a trip." / "Name the date and location. Robes will pack your trip." / rectangular ghost **START PLANNING →**) beside ONE labelled example card — tone tiles named on-cell (Sequin slip dress, White linen shirt, Black bikini, Raffia tote, Gold flat sandals, +9), a dark **ROBES EXAMPLE** badge on the lead cell, meta ending "**· not yours**". The old 60%-opacity dimming treatment is replaced by the badge + caption (legible, still unmistakably not hers); the example stays `pointer-events:none`.
- **Page chrome quieted to the R1 register**: `#sn-eyebrow` ink-faint 400 .24em (was rose 500), the stat line (`.rb-lk-statline`) serif italic 17px, ALL LOOKS gets the same eyebrow + hairline header with sort/Refine right-aligned on the rule, and `#rb-lk-grid`'s `.rb-add-card` matches the card family (`#F7F4EE`, 1px rule, 3px radius, min-height 340px instead of forcing the 3/4 aspect).

### 2026-08-18 — The concierge band, high fidelity (Claude Design handoff "Concierge + Learning", 4a is the build target)
Annie's Claude Design pass, rebuilt on the LIVE dashboard (the handoff targeted `main`'s stylesheet and believed the concierge home didn't exist — only 4a's design was taken, recreated with the dashboard's own tokens/classes). Verified: `ftue_harness` **161/161**, all five other harnesses unchanged-green.
- **The concierge is THE TINTED BAND** — `#F2EEE7` with `#E1DACB` as its on-tint hairline (both hardcoded in `#rb-svc-style`; cream-300 disappears against the tint), 1px `--rule` top/bottom, **full-bleed within the shell frame** via `--rb-bleed` negative gutters that mirror `.dash`'s own padding per breakpoint (`--s6` / 36px compact / 26px ≤920 / 20px ≤560) — the inner content stays on the page grid, no `vw` tricks, no overflow. **Exactly one band per screen.**
- **R1 — one header rule for every home section**: eyebrow left (Inter 400/10px/.24em, ink-faint; INK on the band), its one action right, a hairline under the pair. Applied to the band's `.sec-head`, the rail's `.rb-rail-head` and the Lookbook/Inspiration `.rb-sec-head`s (the rose 500 eyebrows went ink-faint 400 on home).
- **The header meter is one inline row** — eyebrow · 96×2px rail · "N pieces filed" — the 2a caption line is gone (the card captions carry the borrowed-piece claim). **R6: fill = `min(pieces/15, 1)`, LINEAR** — the milestone curve (`_msFillPct`) described gates and is retired here — **and the rail never animates in place** (no transition).
- **Cards per 4a**: white on the tint, 4px radius, 1px `--rule`, **160px photography** — the repo's own `public/images/looks/look1/2/3.jpg` replace the daily bundle photo AND the calendar/suitcase SVG illustrations (both giant inline-SVG constants deleted) — quiet serif ordinal (Cormorant 15px, translucent cream), serif 22px title, 12px desc, the `.rb-svc-note` caption now sits ABOVE its own hairline (`#EFE9DC`, border-bottom) with the text CTA (10px/.2em + 15px arrow) beneath. **R3: text CTA only, never a filled button — the prompt keeps the only filled dark button on the page** (harness-pinned: nothing filled inside the band).
- **Filed row per 4a**: 40×50 thumb at 2px radius with `#E1DACB` border, "N pieces in your wardrobe · See all →" behind a border-left divider, and the ghost CTA at **2px radius** (the handoff's flagged style-guide divergence resolved 4a's way — the one deliberately non-pill button in the app). ≤767px it stacks: receipt row, then the full-width 48px ghost button.
- **R5 sharpened — GENERATED counts, a save is not required**: the daily look defers its save, so `_rbSvcComplete('daily'|'travel')` flags per user (`rb_svc_done__<uid>`, localStorage) the moment a FRESH `__dlRenderResult`/`__tvRenderResult` runs (reopens pass `skipSave` and never count); `_rbConciergeDone` ORs the flags with the saved-row check (which alone crosses devices — the flag is single-device until a server-side home for it exists, flagged not guessed). Weekly stays a coming-soon promo (engine deleted, ADR-001): its CTA still reads "Coming soon", not 4a's "Generate the week", and it rejoins the retire condition when the track returns.
- **Deliberately NOT taken from the handoff**: 4b and the mobile row-cards (Annie: reference 4a only — ≤767px keeps stacked cards on the band with the meter under the eyebrow), the week-grid cell anatomy (the DayCard rail is the live system; it takes only the R1 header), and the prompt-box resizing (the live composer's own design stands).
- **Second pass (same day, Annie's live review)**: (1) **the cards are DAY DOORS** — Daily's card/CTA scopes the prompt to TODAY via `window._ikScopeDay(_pdLocalISO())` (day chip + focus; the dress-me scaffold survives as the `?diary=off` fallback), and **the Weekly planner is LIVE again as "Plan the week →" scoping TOMORROW** (week planning lives in the diary day chips — this is its door; the coming-soon dialog + `.svc-promo` are retired, `_snPaintWays`' skip-guard is dead code). (2) **Image window 260px** (was 160), crop pulled to the top (`object-position:50% 8%` inline — heads in, full look to mid-thigh); text block tightened: body `14px 16px` pad + 7px gap, desc line-height 1.5, note margin folded into the gap — the card grows ~50px net. (3) Day-card swatches showing "just black" were **investigated, not a bug**: the pipeline (`_dcSwatches`→`_ltToneOf`) verified green/red/black with fixture hexes — a day whose look holds ONE owned piece (the black waistcoat) correctly shows one near-black swatch; colour arrives with a fuller wardrobe.

### 2026-08-18 — "Robes is learning" merged INTO The Styling Concierge (Annie's 2a mock: "Nothing gated. Cataloguing buys quality, not access.")
The two modules were fighting for the same job (feature visibility + the reason to catalogue); they are ONE module now, in the concierge's live design language. Verified: `ftue_harness` **151/151** (the milestone-card sections rewritten against the merged module + a retire section), all five other harnesses unchanged-green.
- **The standalone `#wtrk` card NEVER renders again** — the section stays in the DOM only as `_rbApplyLayout`'s anchor (its internals keep painting, invisibly). Its jobs moved into the concierge: **a quiet header meter** (`#rb-svc-learn`, replacing the static "Specialised edits, on request" meta) — "Robes is learning · N piece(s) filed", a thin `_msFillPct` line, and ONE caption: "Every piece you file replaces a borrowed one in the looks below." (the only honest reason to catalogue when access isn't the reward; bare count, never a denominator) — and **the filed-piece row** (`#rb-svc-filed`, closing the module): a RECEIPT, not a wardrobe — one thumbnail always (most recent piece), caption "{Category} · Your first filed piece" → "Last filed" from the second, "N pieces in your wardrobe · See all →" beside it (→ wardrobe), then "One photo files four pieces." + the outlined **"Catalogue what you're wearing now"** pill (`_wtrkOpenAdd`). Fixed height at every count.
- **NOTHING GATED**: the Daily card's `.rb-lock-wrap` progress pill is deleted (`_rbUpdateDailyOutfitLock` now strips it); no card states a condition, no number is a threshold. Each card instead carries a one-line `.rb-svc-note` caption above its CTA — Daily's is count-fed ("Today's look: 2 pieces yours, 2 borrowed." → "your pieces first, gaps borrowed." at 4+ → "styled entirely from your closet." at 15+), Weekly "Seven looks in one pass. Nothing worn twice.", Travel "Tell Robes where and how long." (card descs already carried the 2a copy).
- **The module RETIRES**: once she has created one of each LIVE edit — a daily look AND a travel edit (`_rbConciergeDone`, snLoad types; the weekly planner is a coming-soon promo and rejoins the condition when the track returns) — the whole module falls away at once, meter and receipt row included, and nothing takes its place (the actions live permanently elsewhere; home gets shorter). Data-driven, no flag.
- **Visibility otherwise unchanged**: hidden while the FTU index rows hold home; shown from 3 pieces. **Position is a rule now**: `_rbFtueOrder` sequences `.services` explicitly after the prompt + rail (+styled), ahead of the Lookbook and Inspiration rows.

### 2026-08-18 — Dashboard FTU simplification: one goal per screen (Annie's W01/O1/O1b/O7 mocks)
Beta feedback: too many CTAs on the first-time dashboard. Until she has built her own look or planned an outfit, home is ONE goal at a time — everything else demotes to quiet hairline index rows that **unfurl in place** (nothing leaves the page). All decided in `_lkHomeSync` (still the one place), state machine keyed on Lookbook content. Verified: `ftue_harness` **178/178** (the two home-rack sections rewritten row-based + new "styled card" and "O7" sections; boot now seeds TWO looks — one look is the O7 state), `looks_harness` 307/307, `addflow_harness` 105/105, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `inspiration_smoke` 27/27, `onboarding_harness` **71/71**.
- **Second pass (same day, Annie's live confirmations on beta)**: (1) **rows she opens STAY open** — `_rbFtuOpen` is a set, an unfurl never closes a sibling (supersedes the O1b one-at-a-time note; a second tap folds only that row; a mode flip still resets the set so O7 lands hairlines-closed). (2) **A saved key piece surfaces in the home Inspiration row in the FTU states too** — `_rbRenderInspRow`'s guard narrowed to `#rb-ftu-rows && #rb-styled` (the row stands down only while the styled card IS that piece, hero-sized; the moment the card retires the row appears). (3) **The onboarding "Welcome in" done screen is retired** — `finishToDashboard()` in `onboarding.html` goes straight from the key-piece step (Continue CTA, and the deliberate double-skip) onto `/dashboard`: the per-user `rb_onboarded__<uid>` session flag lands synchronously (the dashboard gate reads it, so no bounce-back), the `onboarded_at` write gets a capped 1.5s beat before navigation, `renderDone` kept unreached like `renderPhotoStep`. The dashboard's "Your piece, styled" card is the arrival moment now. (4) **Zero looks WITHOUT the styled card, the prompt LEADS the page** (Annie: three closed hairlines alone read bare) — mode `zero-lead`: the concierge steps out of its row to sit in focus under "Good {part}, {name}. / What are you dressing for today?" with the Style-something eyebrow, and the rows shrink to BUILD YOUR OWN + THE WEEK AHEAD. The three-row `zero` anatomy now exists ONLY while the styled card is the hero; the card retiring (opening the looks, or a returning session) flips home to `zero-lead` — the row-open default and `_rbFtuTouched` are gone with it, and a mid-typing mode flip hands the caret back to `#cb-ta` after the move. (5) **A planned day EXPOSES The week ahead by default** — the rail's `paint` feeds `_rbFtuWeekAuto(slots)`: any today-or-future moment unfurls the week row once per row set (a fold she makes afterwards stands; a mode flip re-arms it) and the whisper follows the truth both ways ("One day planned." / "N days planned." ⇄ the mode's default). (6) **The composer's home fallback reverted to the Robes door** ("Or let Robes build the first one" / "create your look") — the first cut's "Or start from one of your three looks" kp-pointer is gone, Annie's call. (7) **"Robes is learning" demoted to the page's LAST module whenever visible** (`_rbFtueOrder` appends `#wtrk` at the end — supersedes progress-leads-below-3) and re-registered compact + off the sage wash: white card, rose eyebrow, smaller numeral/headline/tiles (`#rb-wtrk-style` overrides; harness pins ≤320px and the white tone).
- **Zero looks (W01/O1)**: the styled card is the hero with the SINGLE filled CTA — **"See the full looks →"** is now the dark pill in its header (it leads into the kp page's Build-this-look, where a generated look becomes her own); the "Add your next piece" dark button is DELETED and the piece count is a caption under the tiles (`#rb-styled-foot`: "One piece filed. Every look borrows the rest until you photograph your own."). Title reads the piece's own name ("Your acid green cropped jumper, *worn three ways.*"); masthead echo flips to "Your first piece is filed." Beneath it, **`#rb-ftu-rows`** — three hairline rows (BUILD YOUR OWN / STYLE SOMETHING / THE WEEK AHEAD) via `__rbFtuToggle`: each unfurls its module in place (arrow → flips ↑), ONE open at a time. **The prompt (`.concierge`) and the rail (`#rb-rail`) physically live inside their row bodies** (`#rb-ftu-body-style`/`-week`; the rail's own head hides there — the row titles it), so `_rbFtueOrder` gains a rows branch that sequences only [styled, rows] and never touches them; the rail's `mount()` targets the week body when the rows stand. **The rack (`#rb-lkhome`) is not rendered at all until she asks** (`_rbFtuRackSync` — W01 spec note: the first look should come from Robes, not an empty four-slot form); inside the build row it drops its eyebrow (the row titles it) and its fallback door reads **"Or start from one of your three looks"** (→ `__snOpenItem` on the kp entry) instead of the Robes-build door whenever a styled key piece exists. Opening any row compacts the styled card to its header line (`.rb-styled-compact`). With NO styled card (returning session, or the card retiring after she opens the looks — `collapse()` now re-syncs home), the Style-something row opens itself as the one door — never overriding a state she set (`_rbFtuTouched`). Services, tracker, Lookbook + Inspiration home rows all stand down while the rows stand (`_rbGateConcierge` gained the guard; `_rbRenderStyleNotes`/`_rbRenderInspRow` early-return). `_cbSetIntent` + `_ikScopeDay` call `_rbFtuRevealPrompt()` so nothing arms a prompt inside a closed drawer.
- **First look (O7 — hero card retired, prompt leads)**: condition = exactly ONE saved Look and no other artifact (`snLoad` holds no `look`/`daily-look`/`travel-edit` rows). The prompt moves OUT of its row to lead the page as a card under a "Style something" eyebrow (`#rb-conc-ey`) — the one filled button is STYLE ME — then **`#rb-firstlook` "Your looks"**: the look she owns (thumb via `photo_url` else the `_rbLookTile` mosaic, name, "N pieces yours · M borrowed"), **"Finish it"** when proposals remain (→ `__lkCardOpen`, the whole row is the target — the ONLY nudge back into cataloguing), and **wardrobe progress as a caption, never a CTA** (thin `_msFillPct` bar + "N pieces filed. At 15, Robes builds every look entirely from your own closet."; ≥15 flips to the head-to-toe line, no bar). The rows shrink to BUILD YOUR OWN + THE WEEK AHEAD ("One look, unplanned.") — **the week ahead stays a hairline until a second look exists**. The styled card retires to Inspiration (`__rbStyledCollapse` fires; the three ways stay browsable on the tab). Tracker, Lookbook row, services stay down. A second look, a daily look or a travel edit exits to the standard home (mode flip rebuilds the rows, handing the modules back to dash flow).
- **Gotcha hit in build**: `_lkLooks` is assigned far below the first `_waSyncCounts` call — a bare `_lkLooks.length` in `_lkHomeSync` was the documented hoisted-var boot killer (whole dashboard → error screen). Reads are defensive (`(_lkLooks || []).length`). Also the boot try/catch SWALLOWS the real error and the bundle's orphaned timers then throw misleading nulls — debug boot failures from the `Dashboard boot failed:` console line, not the pageerror.
- **Deliberately NOT built**: O7's mock suggestion chips ("Today · The office · + Something else", pre-filled from her style notes) — the existing state-derived `#rb-sugg` pills already ride the prompt; upgrading them to style-notes-fed chips is its own pass.

### 2026-08-18 — Onboarding step 01: one field, one pool · skip closes once she answers (Annie's beta feedback + the D mocks)
The Icons step restructured to the "D · Restructured" frames, plus the feedback call that a skip must not survive an answer. Verified with the new **`scripts/onboarding_harness.mjs`** (**64 checks** at 1280px + 390px: copy, the inline-chip field, the meta row, pool shape, the restored hairline, skip visibility in both directions on BOTH steps, the unreadable-piece recovery, no page errors, no horizontal overflow).
- **The chosen icons live INSIDE the field.** The old anatomy was a bare input, then a second "Your icons" shelf with its own label + tally, then a divider, then the pool — four blocks for one question. Now: one bordered field holding the chips (`#ic-chips`, `display:contents` so the chips are flex children of `.v1-field` — the input is never rebuilt, so focus and caret survive a paint) followed by the input, whose placeholder flips **"Type a name — person or brand" → "Add another…"** once anything is in. The `.v1-selected-head` / `.v1-empty` / `.v1-divider` blocks are deleted.
- **One helper row under the field** (`.ic-meta`): how-to on the left ("Press Enter to add"), where-she-is on the right (`.ic-tally`, italic serif — **"Three is plenty to start"** at zero, then "N added"). The hint no longer hides ≤560px — the mobile mocks carry both halves.
- **The pool is the only other thing on the screen**: label **"Or tap a popular one"** (was "Popular among stylists"), ten chips (was twelve), each led by a quiet `+` (`.chip-plus`). Chips carry `data-v` so the `+` can't end up inside the committed name.
- **Skip closes the moment she answers** (`paintSelected` owns it): `#ob-skip` is hidden while `st.icons.length`, and returns if she empties the field — Continue is then the only way out. Copy: title **"Who do you *dress like?*"**, sub "Add the people and brands whose style inspire you. The more you add, the faster Robes gets to know you."
- **Affordances the inline field owes her**: tapping the field's padding focuses the input (the chips are usually what's under the finger), and Backspace on an empty input takes the last chip back.
- **THE HAIRLINES WERE NEVER RENDERING — `--cream-300` / `--cream-400` were self-referential** (`--cream-300: var(--cream-300)` in onboarding.html's `:root`, over the same names tokens.css already defines). A custom property that cycles is invalid at computed-value time, so all 13 rules reading them were IACVT and fell back to unset — **`border-style` resolved to `none` on every hairline in the flow**: the field, the ghost chips, the typeahead dropdown, the `kbd`, the progress track, the capture panel and the Add-photo pill. The mock's bordered field literally could not render until this was fixed. The two lines are gone; tokens.css supplies them. **Rule (tokens.css says it too): never re-alias a name tokens.css already defines.**
- **Focus moved onto the field** with the borders back: the page's `:focus-visible` a11y rule was drawing a 2px ink box just inside the field's own border (a doubled ring). `.v1-field:focus-within` now carries the indicator (ink border + 1px inset shadow) and `.v1-input` suppresses its own — one target, still clearly focused.
- **A skip never survives an answer — the rule is BOTH steps** (Annie, same day): step 02 withdraws `#ob-skip` the moment a photo is attached (`syncSkip`, called from `paint()` so every state change re-decides it), not merely once the piece is filed — she has answered as soon as she has attached. It comes back **disarmed** if the answer empties: Remove, or an unreadable piece clearing its own photo. The two-tap "Skip anyway" confirm survives for the never-attached case, and is now only ever reachable there, so the handler's old `P.tag ||` branch is gone. **`skipArmed` is `var` at the top of `renderKeyPiece`** — `paint()` calls `syncSkip()` and runs BEFORE the skip wiring at the foot of the function, so a `let` would sit in the temporal dead zone on that first call (the documented trap that once broke the whole dashboard).
- **Step 02 is WARDROBE, and it says what it wants** (same day, Annie's screenshot): step chip **"Step 02 · Wardrobe"** (was "First piece"), eyebrow **"Your first piece"** (was "Your wardrobe" — the two had been the wrong way round), h1 **"Add your first piece"** (roman, one line, no trailing stop — the `<br><em>key piece.</em>` clause is gone), and the step gains a `sub` it never had: "Give Robes one piece you love — from your wardrobe, or you might be wearing it right now. This is where your wardrobe starts." — the anchor line that says WHICH piece, since "your first piece" alone left her choosing in the dark. The capture panel's own `.cap-label` still reads "Your key piece" (deliberate: key piece is load-bearing product vocabulary — the `/api/style` track, "Style a key piece", the kp result pages).
- **Not fixed here, flagged: `public/board.html` carries the same bug** (`--ink:var(--ink); --cream:var(--cream)`), so the PUBLIC share page renders white-with-black text instead of cream/ink — 8 rules dead. Same one-line fix, but it's a different surface.

### 2026-08-17 — Inspiration CTA + "Build this look" from a key piece styled (Annie's Inspiration/Key-Piece-to-Look mocks)
The kp result's per-look dead end is closed and Inspiration gains its front door — the deferred follow-up to the 2026-08-10 "save-as-look excluded" note, served through the existing console rather than new surfaces. Verified: `inspiration_smoke` 24/24 (new harness — the modal journey end to end), `looks_harness` 307/307, `ftue_harness` 155/155, `travel_console_smoke` 102, `addflow_harness` 105/105.
- **Inspiration header CTA → the two-step styling modal** (a deliberate exception to every-route-lands-on-the-prompt, Annie's call — same class as `__lkNewHoliday`'s intake): the black "Style a key piece" pill (and the empty state's card) opens `__inStyleNew()`'s modal (`#rb-inst-wrap`, z-950) — step 1 per the mock: "Style a key piece, *three ways.*", dashed snap/attach tile (`#rb-inst-file`, accept image, NO capture; downscaled via `_rbDownscale`, preview + "Change photo") beside the brief textarea ("A photo makes it sharper — but a few words is plenty"; photo OR words is enough) → step 2 is the scan state (dark panel, piece photo, scan line + corner brackets, `_IN_SCAN_STAGES` cycling headline/sub/caption at 2.4s, "This takes about twenty seconds. Cancel"). `__inStGo` posts `/api/style` with `_cbStyleSubmit`'s exact contract (90s abort; Cancel/× aborts silently; an error returns to step 1 with her inputs kept) and lands on `__kpRenderResult` (closing the modal + the Inspiration page — kp is z-40 under insp's 45).
- **"Build this look" on every kp look card lands in EDIT MODE — the LOOSE console** (`__kpBuildLook(i)` → `_kpBuildLookRun`; third pass 2026-08-19, reconciling two parallel sessions — Annie: "should expose the look in EDIT mode, not as a saved look until initiated by the user"; supersedes both the first cut's dated daily look AND the second's straight-to-`_lkCreate` mint): footer row under the Outfit/Details/Accessories stack — microcopy + outlined pill. The daily engine itemises the way's prose **composition-only** (`POST /api/daily` with **`noImages: true`** — no image job started; the response is otherwise identical), and the result renders through `__dlRenderResult` as a **loose look** (1a): editable console (flick owned-only / swap / anchor live), eyebrow "Your look", **the way's title as the headline** (Robes' name offer, rule 01), NOTHING written, **Save this look** the one commitment. **The keep (`__dlSaveKeep`) carries the richness**: owned matches → `look_pieces` (+roles), every unowned item → a migration-19 **proposal** (opts = item + its alternates, `_dlSlot` chip, category cats — built generically in `__dlSaveKeep` now, so ANY kept look with unowned pieces reopens whole), `photo_url` = the way's ORIGINAL kp frame (`data._dlPhotoUrl`), and **her uploaded product photo lands on the key piece's own proposal card** (word-overlap match `_kpBuildToks` stamps `it._kpPhotoUrl`). Unowned pieces wishlist on keep (the standing `__dlSaveKeep` behaviour). Tracks `kp_build_look`.
- **Legacy dl-origin framing kept for existing rows**: the first cut's `origin:'key-piece'`/`kpSourceId` plumbing through `__dlSubmit`/`__dlRenderResult` (crumb "Your look", "Built from … Back to the three ways →" via `__dlBackToKp`, build overlay copy) survives so daily-look rows saved during the first cut's window still render with their framing — no new flow reaches it.

### 2026-08-17 — Look rules: named, then saved · the day is not the Lookbook (spec `Look_Rules__Design.dc.html`)
The six rules the whole looks model now answers to, plus the vibe/occasion split. **A look is generated (by her or by Robes) → it gets a NAME → she saves it → from that point it gathers wear data.** A look made FOR A DAY is pinned to that date and saving it to the Lookbook is *offered, never forced*; adjustments on a pinned day are LOCAL to the day, and Robes asks once whether they are a new look. Verified: `looks_harness` **314/314** (rewritten across nine sections + a new rules-05/06 section), `ftue_harness` 155/155, `addflow_harness` 105/105, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **Rules 01/02 — the name is the gate on Save** (`_lkNewHtml`, `__lkSave`): who generated it decides who names it. A Robes build arrives named (`_lkBuildTitle` fills the field 420ms after the pieces settle, marked "Robes' name for it. Yours to change."); a look SHE built keeps `Save this look` **disabled until the field has a value**, with `.rb-lk-namenote#rb-lk-namegate` ("Name it, and it's yours to keep.") saying so where the answer goes — a title attribute is invisible on touch. `__lkNewTitleInput` flips the button and the note **in place**, never by repainting (a repaint takes the caret out of the field). `__lkSave` re-checks and, if unnamed, focuses the input instead of refusing silently. **The offered-name fallback is gone for hand-built looks** — `name_provisional` now only ever means "Robes named it and she left it", which is reachable only on a build or a day-look keep.
- **Rule 03 — no look-level wear without a saved look** (`__lkAccrue`, rewritten): passive accrual used to MINT a Look from any unrecognised piece set the moment a wear was confirmed, which filled the Lookbook with looks she never named and made rule 02 unenforceable from the other end. It now only records a wear against a look that **already exists** — `o.lookId` addresses one directly (a day knows which look it is wearing, so an adjusted day still counts), else the exact piece set has to BE a saved look. **Declining is a supported answer**: every caller already falls back to patching `times_worn` itself, which is the pieces counting their wears exactly as the rule says. `_lkAddWear` gained `o.pieceIds` — the wear snapshots **what was on the body**, not the look as saved, which is what lets the worn log name the difference.
- **Rule 04 — the day is written; the Lookbook save is an OFFER** (`__dlRenderResult`, `__dlSaveKeep`): supersedes the 2026-08-13 deferral, which withheld *both* writes and lost the day. Two different things, two different writes — the `lookbook_items` daily-look row + its `planned_days` index land automatically on generation (that row **is** the day: date, weather, what she was dressed in), and `.dlm-offer` ("Save to your Lookbook") mints a named `looks` entity, which is the thing that gathers wear data. `data._dlNoLook` is the offer's state (the retired `_dlDeferred` flag is deleted off reopened blobs). **Robes generated it, so Robes names it** — the headline, `name_provisional: true`, hers to change from the detail; unowned pieces still go to the Wishlist and she is told so first. Share stays the day's action on the panel (date, weather and share all describe a day, not a look).
- **Rules 05/06 — adjust the day, asked once**: `_dlDayBase` holds the saved look's composition as the baseline the moment a day opens wearing one (set on a FIRST render only — re-baselining on a flick would erase the change before it). `_dlDayChanges()` measures against it; `.dlm-adjusted` is the one quiet line ("One change on this day only. *The Thursday one* in your Lookbook is unchanged.") with **Reset to the saved look** (`__dlDayReset`, rebuilt from the look) and **Save as a new look** (`__dlDayPromote`). Changed rows carry the `Today only` chip + "Swapped in for the {x} · undo" (`__dlDayUndo`, `.dl-localrow`). **`_dlExitGuard` is repurposed into THE one question** — same modal from the banner, from `✓ Wore it`, and on leaving the day (`__dlGoBack`, the nav wrap, the wordmark): "You changed N pieces. Save it as a new look?" with a suggested name (`_dlDaySuggestName`) + **Save as a new look** / **Just keep it on the day**. `_dlAsked` latches it to once per day. Keeping it on the day sends the wear to the ORIGINAL look carrying the adjusted `pieceIds`; saving as new writes a `source:'variant'` look with `origin_look_id` and the wear follows it. **The `beforeunload` alarm and the "this look isn't saved" exit modal are gone** — nothing is lost by leaving now.
- **1c — the saved look detail is the look and its history** (chrome per the C1/C2 frames, second beta pass same day): eyebrow reads **Saved look**; the masthead's action pair is a **circled calendar** (`.rb-lk-calbtn` → the which-day panel) + an **outlined "Wear today" pill** (`.rb-lk-wearbtn`) — the three-button `.rb-lk-acts` row and **Pack it are gone** (the mock carries no third action; packing lives in the travel intake, which imports saved looks; `_lkPackIt` survives door-less). The pin is a **subtle reminder strip** (`.rb-lk-pinstrip`, sage `--secondary`, "Pinned for Sunday 16 Aug." + Open the day →) and IS the pin confirmation — `__lkPinTo` sets no `_lkDone`. **One ledger card** (`.rb-lk-ledger`): stat cells in a row — Wears / Last worn / Pieces / Saved (+ per-wear) — over the worn log ("Worn · N times" + quiet **Add a date**); worn rows carry no chips, the difference is named in the line via **`_lkWearDiff`** ("White sandals instead of the jacket — *adjusted on the day*"). **Lineage** (`.rb-lk-lin`) names both directions; Delete rides the same bottom row. The rack **READS by default** (each row carries the piece's own wear count via `.rbc-wears`); **Edit & resave turns on LIVE editing** — flick and swap land on **`_lkDraft`** and the mosaic + rack repaint in real time, the SAVED look untouched, with the quiet **`.rb-lk-editbar`** strip (D1's register: "One change to this look. Its 2 wears stay with it if you update.") offering **Discard / Update this look / Save as a new look**. Save-as-new opens the **D2-anatomy modal** (`__lkPromoteAsk`: "You changed N pieces. Save it as a new look?" + Name-the-new-look input with a suggested name; the quiet exit is "Just update this look") — rule 02 holds, the variant carries `origin_look_id`. **The ask-before-apply promotion gate is retired** (`_lkPending` unused); `__lkResave`/`__lkDraftDiscard`/`__lkPromoteAsk` replaced `__lkUpdate`/`__lkPromote`/`__lkCancelPromote`. The Edit & resave toggle hides while changes stand — the strip is the only way out, so half-made edits can't be silently stranded.
- **1e — vibe and occasion are two axes, not one.** **Occasion belongs to the DAY and titles it** (E3): the daily masthead is eyebrow = full date → h1 = the occasion → weather pill + "Wearing *{look name}*" + the vibe chip. A day with **no** occasion titles itself by the date and **the eyebrow drops away**. The day's ✎ names the OCCASION (through `_rbDayRename`, the one write path every day surface shares — `__dlDayRename`); the pencil beside "Wearing" renames the look. `__lkOpenAsDay` no longer writes the look's name into `occasion_label` (the 2B Quiet conflation, in another costume). **Vibe belongs to the LOOK**, is read from her prompt, and **there is exactly one** — stacking them makes the wear data unreadable. The ten ship as seeds (`_TG_VIBE_SEEDS`: Powerhouse · Chic · Undone · Polished · Easy · Romantic · Sharp · Quiet · Statement · Off-duty) with "+ Add a vibe" for her own; `__rbTagPick`/`__rbTagCommit` REPLACE rather than append on the vibe axis, `normLookTags` caps it to 1 server-side, and `_rbVibeOf`/`_rbVibeLabel` read the first of any legacy multi-vibe blob (an edit collapses the rest). `.dlm-vibread` is E1's "Robes read **Powerhouse** as the vibe. Not quite? Change the vibe". Server: `LOOK_TAG_VIBE_SEEDS` + `vibeVocabLine(vibes)` — `/api/daily` and `/api/lookbuild/note` take a `vibes` array (the client sends `_rbVibeVocab()`), so "power CEO", "powerhouse" and "boss" all land on the one tag she already has.
- **E2 — the Lookbook holds SAVED LOOKS ONLY; vibe filters inside Refine.** `_lkShelfItems()` no longer returns `daily-look` rows: a day is a DAY, it lives in the Diary and is opened from a day cell (home strip, travel strip, month view), **never from the Lookbook** (legacy `type:'look'` items stay — they have nowhere else to live). **Vibe is a Refine axis under Season and Wear it for — one filtering system, one place** (Annie's beta pass, same day, corrected a first cut that gave vibe a standing `.rb-lkvibe` row + insight card on the grid: "it should not appear on the lookbook independently"; the row, `__lkVibePick`/`__lkVibeClear` and `_lkVibeInsight` are deleted). Cards carry `.lt-vibe` and read "3 wears · 4 pieces · last 13 Aug" (wears lead; zero wears reads "Not yet worn").
- **C1 routing — a Lookbook card opens the SAVED LOOK DETAIL, never the day** (Annie's beta pass, same day: a saved look opened from the grid was wearing a day's chrome — date, weather, share): `__lkCardOpen` goes straight to `__lkOpen`, superseding the 2026-08-08 "a look card opens as it is worn" routing. `__lkOpenAsDay` survives as the DIARY CELLS' opener only (`_rbOpenPlannedDay`). **The day console reads as the DAY** (D1 anatomy): panel head "The day · N pieces" (evening variant "The evening"), the `Robes` credit slot flips to a sage **Adjusted** while the day differs from its saved look. **Day title ladder (E3 + D1 reconciled)**: occasion titles the day (date as eyebrow) → no occasion but a saved look wearing titles "Wearing *{look}*" (date as eyebrow; the meta "Wearing" line drops — never twice) → neither titles by the date, eyebrow dropped.
- **Made LOOSE (third beta pass, same day — Annie: a look built from a key piece "should expose the look in EDIT mode, not as a saved look")**: the rules map's 1a·02 split is now real code — a look built AROUND A PIECE is not for a day. The post-add fork's second door reads **"Build a look around it"** and passes `loose: true` into `__dlSubmit`; a loose look takes **no `anchor_date`** (everything date-shaped keys off it: no day title ladder, no Day/Evening switcher, no day-rename pencil, no diary sync), **writes NOTHING on render** (no `lookbook_items` row, no `planned_days`), and the console is its builder — flick/swap/anchor live, eyebrow **"Your look"**, headline as title, **Save this look** as the panel's one commitment (`actionKeep`, no share badge). Keeping it mints the `looks` entity alone; Share returns once it exists. `✓ Wore it` on an unkept loose look keeps it first (`_dlWearAfterSave`); `↻ Restyle it` re-mixes loose (`__dlRestyle` carries the flag). Leaving an unkept loose look discards it — 1a's "it exists nowhere until it is saved" is the model, not a bug. The day baseline (rule 05) never arms on a loose page.
- **The flick cluster is reserved for HER WARDROBE (same pass)**: `_dlOptions` returns the served piece + her same-L1-category pieces ONLY, uncapped (was capped at 4 with AI alternates in the cycle) — an owned top flicks through all her other tops; a Robes suggestion is **never flicked into another suggestion**, only swapped for a piece she owns, and with no owned peers it has no flick at all (`_rbcRow` hides the cluster at `count.len === 1`). Saved-build proposals lose their suggestion-cycling flick too (`__lkPropFlip` unreached; Swap + Save stay). The `alternates` data still rides `/api/daily`'s schema and saved blobs untouched — nothing consumes it in the flick any more (`_rbFetchAlternates`/`_rbPrefetchAlternates` are dead code), and `/api/alternates` still powers the composer's shop proposals.
- **Knock-on, deliberate**: the home builder module and the learning card gate on `_lkHomeZero()`, which now means *no saved looks* — an account with only generated days meets the composer, which is correct (she has not saved a look yet). The home Lookbook row (`_rbRenderStyleNotes`) drops days for the same reason. `_rbcRow` gained a `cfg.onSwap` guard (Swap rendered unconditionally and would have emitted `window.undefined(...)` on the read-only rack — same bug class as the flick cluster in the 2026-08-12 fifth pass).
- **Deliberately NOT built**: the spec's D3/D4 mobile sheets are the existing modals at 390px, not new components; no migration (vibe still rides `tag_looks` / the flat `looks.tags`, the day still rides `lookbook_items` + `planned_days`).

### 2026-08-14 — DayCard v3: two axes, the compose sequence, swatches everywhere (spec `DayCard_Spec_v3.dc.html` + Annie's F-calls)
The three day surfaces (home rail · trip strip · diary) re-converge on the v3 model. Verified: `travel_console_smoke` 102/102, `ftue_harness` 154/154, `looks_harness` 287/287, `addflow_harness` 105/105.
- **Two axes, not five states** (`_dcMoments`): `when` (past/today/future; the diary's out-of-month 'void' is the fourth when, numeral only) × `stage` (`empty`/`named`/`dressed`; `naming` is a transient UI stage the surface arms via `opts.naming`). **'free' folds into 'empty'** — an unfilled day always offers the invitation and KEEPS the plan's chip (membership follows the DATE, not the stage; the chip renders on every stage, always below the second line, never the foot — empty invites flow in the title position via `.dc-empty.top`). **`named` is the cell v2 had no state for** — a titled look-less day renders its title + a dashed **`+ Look`** control (`opts.lookAdd`); past+named stays quiet (the look was never pinned). `d.state` survives as the legacy composite for CSS/callers; the `?daycard=off` legacy renders are untouched.
- **The compose sequence, every surface**: empty → invitation ("add plans…" / "name the day and add a look" — the trip strip's copy, verbatim, everywhere; "Left free. Tap to dress it." and "Nothing yet." are gone) → naming (inline input IN the card) → named (+ Look) → dressed. **Rail naming** (`__rbRailName/Key/Commit`, `.dc-name-in`): a trip-member day names inline and the title writes to that trip's `tvData.dayTitles[day_index]` (the same write the strip's editor makes) + `_pdSyncSaved`; a bare day's invitation tap keeps scoping the prompt (naming a bare day IS typing the plan into the prompt — the standing rule). The **ring/sparkle rides every non-past full card** (v2 showed it on four of seven with no rule) and always scopes the prompt on home; empty-past cards stay inert.
- **Swatches everywhere** (`_DC_THUMB_MODE = 'swatch'`, settled): the rail's palette whisper is the rule on all day surfaces — `.dc-sw` hex dots from the day's owned pieces, no denominator (**counts belong to the console — F3: the "{n} pieces · {n} yours" slot stays dead, do not resurrect**). `'image'` restores v2 thumbnails.
- **Title rule (F2, Annie)**: authorship decides — **her words render VERBATIM** (`_dcTitleOf` no longer sentence-cases user activity; "dinner with mum" stays as typed), a **Robes-authored title is a NAME, not a sentence** (trailing full stops stripped at render; server prompt rule in `/api/travel` + `/api/travel/looks`: look `title` = 2–4 words Title Case, never a sentence/trailing stop). **The trio treatment is "2B Quiet" (same day, Annie's production catch — the day's title and the looks' occasions were CONFLATED on the card: "Sunset drinks", the first look's occasion, rendered where the day's facts belonged)**: a day is title / day look / evening look. The trip-strip card renders the title, then the look NAMES as ONE serif phrase, both names, no labels (`.lknames` — "Golden hour glow · Cozy and sophisticated"), in the weight the occasion line used to hold; **occasions never render on the day card**. An un-named dressed day titles as its day look's NAME (which then never repeats in the phrase); looks beyond the two named ride the swatch row as `+N` (`.swplus`). Data-side, **`_pdRowsTv` keeps `activity` for HER words only** (the day title; the occasion no longer rides the user slot) and the look's name goes on `headline`. **The phrase is ONE shared rule — `_dcLookPhrase`** (second pass, same day, Annie: the rail and diary had kept the old "Evening · {activity}" line while the trip card got the phrase — "don't let these diverge again"): `_dcMoments` builds `d.second` from the moments' `headline`s (never `activity` — stale rows carried occasions there) and `_tvPaintWeek` feeds the same helper from the blob, so all three surfaces render the identical serif-italic phrase (`.dc-eve` restyled to the serif register; no "Evening ·" label anywhere on a day card — the compact ☾ mark alone carries the two-moment signal). Old planned_days rows self-correct on each source's next sync. **The trio holds in the day peek too** (third pass, same day, Annie's bug: the peek's Day slot was named "Exploring City" where "Golden hour glow" belonged): the slot lines read the LOOKS' names (`headline`-first), and the day's own title heads the modal.
- **Day rename reaches every surface** (same day, Annie: the trip strip edits plans inline, the rail/Diary didn't because they open modals — "on the modal itself, & if no looks have been assigned, allow on the card"): **`_rbDayRename` is the ONE write path** — a trip day writes the trip's `dayTitles` (the strip's own write), a daily look its `occasion_label`, a pinned Look re-pins with the label (`_lkPin`'s name-carrying rebuild means an empty commit can't clear a look-day's name). Where the edit lives follows the stage: an undressed (empty/named) rail card edits INLINE on the card (`__rbRailName`, trip-member days); a dressed day edits **on the peek** (`.dpk-ttl-row` — her title or "add plans…" + the `✎` pencil → in-place input, `__rbDayPeekRename/Key/Commit`); the Diary edits **on the add-a-look picker's headline** (`__mvWearRename`, pencil rendered whenever `_rbDayRenameTarget` resolves — free travel rows count, so an in-trip empty day names from the Diary too). Commits repaint SOFTLY from rows in hand (`_rbDayRepaints` → `_rbRailRepaint` + new `window._rbMvRepaint`) — a refetch would race the debounced planned_days upsert and revert the fresh name.
- **Trip strip converged** (`_tvPaintWeek`): the **pencil retires** — the title is its own rename affordance (dotted underline on hover); cards match the homepage height (`min-height:176px`, was 128); the strip **gains the sparkle** on dressed days → `__tvDayPick` — one destination, double duty (+ Look dresses, the sparkle restyles). **`__tvDayPick` leads with the two make-doors** (From your lookbook / ✦ Robes styles one) over "Or one of this trip's looks" — twelve near-identical phrases are the least likely place the answer sits.
- **Diary converged**: ONE empty treatment — future/today empties carry the invitation (`.dc-invite` at cell scale) and open the picker, named cells carry `+ Look`, past empties stay quiet, void stays a bare numeral; worn renders in the month grid. **`__mvWear` is now the shared add-a-look picker** (eyebrow "Add a look · {date}", the day's own title heads it, **"✦ Robes styles one"** → `__mvRobes` closes the page and scopes the prompt to the date, then a **"Your looks"** grid; the zero-looks Lookbook door survives) — the rail's named days open it too (`__rbRailLook`). Eyebrow truncation fixed (`.dc-ey` 9px/.14em + 30px ring reserve).
- **F1 — no fourth surface**: the spec's "Weekly Plan strip" does not exist — the weekly track was deleted 2026-08-08 (ADR-001); the audit line was stale. Nothing converged, nothing built.
- **F4 — ghost bands**: `_pdWrite` gained a **date-range sweep** — after every sync, rows of the source outside the blob's current date span are deleted (a MOVED trip can never leave its old dates banded, however the orphans got there); `comingUp` regained supersession (its select fetches `updated_at` again and runs `_pdSupersede` — a rescheduled plan's stale twin can't win the Coming-up slot).

### 2026-08-13 (wardrobe UX pass) — top-ten tab row + one-door empty state (Annie)
Two de-clutter calls on the wardrobe page, verified `ftue_harness` **154/154** (empty-state section rewritten), `addflow_harness` **105/105** (3 new checks), `looks_harness` 287/287, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102:
- **The category row holds the top TEN sheet L1s by piece count** (`_waTabsPaint`, extracted from `_waBuildFilters` and re-ranked on every `_waRender`): membership by count (ties break in sheet order), **display in the sheet's canonical order**, All leading, and the remainder behind ONE **"More ▾"** tab (`__waMoreTap` → `#rb-wg-morepop`, a fixed-position popover listing the hidden categories with their counts; outside-click dismisses) — the row never wraps to a second line. **An active category never hides**: picked from More it surfaces into the row as the active tab (so the cascade has its anchor). The repainter stands down while a cascade is open (`#rb-wg-cascade` lives inside `#wg-filters` — repainting under it would orphan the flyout); the tap that closes the cascade repaints on its way through.
- **The empty wardrobe is ONE invitation**: at zero pieces the hero card ("Every look starts with a photograph.", two doors) is the whole page — the milestone bar ("What more pieces bring" — home's learning meter already carries that promise) and the add-card grid + `.rb-ghost-card` tiles are gone (the duplication read as three asks for the same act). **The `.rb-add-card` returns with the first piece** and stays from then on, per the filled-grid convention.

### 2026-08-13 — Looks convergence: the build speaks, the aspirational daily waits to be kept (Annie's discrepancy report)
The two zero-wardrobe doors had drifted into two products: "Let Robes build" filled the rack but arrived MUTE (no stylist note, no colour/texture/tags, bare shop cards), while the prompt's `/api/daily` spoke beautifully but AUTO-SAVED a look she owns nothing of straight into the Lookbook with no CTA. Both now share one register and one rule — **a look made of proposals is kept only when she keeps it, and keeping it moves the proposals to the Wishlist**. Verified: `looks_harness` **287/287** (31 new checks across the three passes: build note/tags/fabrics, the shared proposal row, saved-build reopen with modal swap + flick + owned adoption, zero-owned detail, two daily deferred-save sections, the exit ask), `ftue_harness` 156/156, `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **The build gets its words — `POST /api/lookbuild/note`** (flash, thinking 0, 450 tokens — the "new lightweight endpoint" the fifth-pass decision flagged; the PICK stays deterministic and client-side): given the assembled pieces (name/role/owned), it returns the look's PANEL NOTE (`PANEL_NOTE_RULE` + banned constructions), `look_tags` (via `normLookTags`) and a 2–4-hex `palette`. Client `_lkBuildNoteFetch` fires AFTER the pieces settle (seq-guarded `_lkBuildSeq` so a stale response can't write over a newer build), patches the panel quote + tag row + palette in place, and never blocks the 900ms beat — a failed fetch leaves the build exactly as before. **The note saves onto `looks.note`** (`__lkSave` passes it into `_lkCreate`) so the saved Look detail reads it back; the tags ride the existing `lookTags` path. Fabric chips (`_rbcFabricsHtml`) render under the built mosaic; the palette row falls back to the fetched hexes when no owned tones exist (a 0-owned build previously showed nothing). The tag row's 2-piece floor now counts proposals on a built look.
- **A proposal IS a rack card** (second pass, same day — Annie: "a template following the exact same rules as the prompt generated"): the bespoke `.rb-lk-shop` card is retired; every proposal renders through **`_rbcRow`** via `_lkPropRowHtml` — same height rules, the slot label on the frame (`vslot`), `_rbcProvenance` (brand · retailer · price), the `how` ROW NOTE, and the same action cluster: Swap **with the swap icon** + `.rbc-act.save` Save (→ "✓ Saved"). Only two build-specific states survive in CSS: `.rb-lk-shopph` (the labelled breathing block while a still-life is coming) and `.rbc-row.rb-lk-busy` (dim while a fresh suggestion fetches). The masthead credit reads plain **"Robes"** everywhere ("Robes' build" retired). **The escape leak is fixed at every generator** (third pass — a daily rack card read "€110", a brand "Polène"): `deEscDeep` runs over the parsed payload of `/api/style`, `/api/daily`, `/api/travel`, `/api/travel/looks`, `/api/lookbuild/note`, `/api/wardrobe/analyse` and the per-field pass in `/api/alternates`; client-side `_rbDeEsc` cleans rows saved BEFORE the fix at display time (`_rbcPrice`, `_rbcRetailPrice`, `_rbcProvenance` brand, the swap modal's header/footer).
- **A saved build reopens WHOLE — `looks.proposals` (migration 19, `supabase/look_proposals_migration.sql`, NOT yet run on Robes_p0)**: `__lkSave` snapshots the shop rows (`{role, chip, cats, opts, oi, saved, image_url}`) onto the look row, so the Look detail draws the SAME mosaic (owned photos + proposal stills via `boardOnlyItems`) and hangs every proposal as a full `_rbcRow` on the rack — head "N yours, M to find", note, tags, ownership line, exactly as built (Annie: "both looks should appear exactly the same after being saved"). Saved proposals keep their verbs, split the daily way (third pass, same day — Annie: "hitting swap should open the modal"): the **flick cluster** (`__lkPropFlip`) cycles the stored suggestions — non-destructive, `img_oi` remembers which one the still was shot for so flicking back recovers it — while **Swap opens the shared `_rbSwapModal`** (`__lkPropSwap`/`__lkPropSwapApply`/`__lkPropSnap`): wardrobe candidates by category, Snap mine, the affiliate door. **Picking an owned piece is the moment the look becomes hers** — the proposal comes off the rack and the piece joins `look_pieces` carrying the proposal's role (the wear verbs and stats return with it). **`__lkPropSave`** wishlist-saves; everything persists through `_lkPatch`. Degrade until migration 19 runs: `_lkPushCloud`/`_lkPatchCloud` strip the column on a PGRST204 naming it (`_lkPropCol`), and `_lkLoad`'s merge carries local-cache proposals onto cloud rows that couldn't hold them — single-device persistence until the column exists.
- **A saved look she owns nothing of stops pretending** (`_lkDetailHtml`, `ownedNone`): Wear it today / Wear on a day / Pack it — verbs that operate on owned pieces — are replaced by a "Not yours yet." panel with **Open your wishlist** (`__lkOpenWishlist`, the /wishlist deep-link open pattern); the stats ledger and the Worn log wait for a piece. (The bare "nothing hangs here" rack line now only renders for legacy zero-piece saves with no stored proposals.)
- **EVERY fresh daily look defers its save** (`__dlRenderResult`; shipped as zero-owned-only, widened the same day — Annie: "make the user save every look — the commitment action is important"): the fresh render stamps `data._dlDeferred` (riding the data object so flick/swap rerenders — skipSave with no savedId — keep the state; a savedId clears it), writes NOTHING (no `snAdd`, no `_pdSync` — the look isn't in the Lookbook or on the rail until kept), and the Look panel's action is **Save this look** instead of Share — `__dlSaveAsk` interposes the composer's exact wishlist confirm ONLY when the look holds unowned pieces ("N pieces aren't yours yet…"; an all-owned look saves on the tap), `__dlSaveKeep` then writes the lookbook row + diary index + every unowned piece to the wishlist in one keep, toasts, and rerenders (Save flips back to Share, late frames persist via the now-set `_dlActiveSaveId`). **Wearing an unkept look keeps it first** — `__dlWear` routes through `__dlSaveKeep` (or through the confirm via `_dlWearAfterSave` when unowned pieces exist; Cancel cancels both) before logging the wear. Reopens and restyles of SAVED rows (`savedId`) keep the evolve-in-place behaviour — the deferral is only ever about the first keep. "✓ Wore it" hides at 0 owned (it logs wears on owned pieces — a button that did nothing). `_rbConsole` gained `cfg.actionKeep` (the action stays visible ≤767px — `.rbc-action.keep`) and `cfg.shareBadge:false` (no mobile share circle — sharing an unkept look would save it as a side effect, skipping the confirm). **Abandonment is real now: closing the page without saving discards the look** — that is the commitment model, not a bug.
- **The daily console is held in the composer's container** (`.dlm-console`): white card, hairline `--rule-mid`, `--rad-lg`, whisper shadow, and the Look panel's own chrome strips inside it (no frame in a frame) — the prompt-built look and the composer's build now sit in the same held card.
- **Leaving an unkept look asks first** (same day, Annie: "if a user closes a page, it should get an alert to save, or close"): `_dlExitGuard` interposes ONE modal — "This look isn't saved." with the wishlist line folded into the same sentence when unowned pieces exist — offering **Save the look** (→ `__dlSaveKeep`, then the exit proceeds) or **Let it go** (drops the deferral flag and leaves); backdrop tap stays. Guards `__dlGoBack`, and a late-boot wrap (1600ms setTimeout — the app's own late-wiring pattern) covers `__rbNavGo` + the wordmark onclick; a `beforeunload` listener covers the tab itself. Because the exit modal already says where unowned pieces go, its Save calls `__dlSaveKeep` directly — no second confirm stacks.

### 2026-08-12 (fifth pass) — "Let Robes build the first one" fills the SAME rack (frames 04b/04c)
ONE RULE: it returns to the same rack, filled, and **saves nothing until she does**. A head start, never a separate feature and never a black box. Supersedes the door's previous behaviour (`__snWay('dress-me')`, which closed the page and armed the home prompt). Verified: `looks_harness` **243/243** (two new sections, 16 checks), `ftue_harness` 156/156, `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **In place, no new screen** (`__lkRobesBuild`): the slots become flat cream blocks (`.rb-lk-fill`, a 1.5s opacity breath, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`) for a **minimum 900ms beat** so the fill reads as a build rather than a flash. No spinner, no full-screen overlay, no navigation — harness-pinned on all four. **The name lands LAST** (a further 420ms), so the look assembles then earns its title, and only if she hasn't typed one.
- **The pick is client-side and deterministic** — `_LK_BUILD_ROLES` maps each formula role to its categories, and `_lkBuildCandidates` takes the hero first, then the least-worn. **Robes never hangs a piece she hasn't seen a photograph of** (`_pdHttp(image_url)` is a hard filter), which is what makes a photograph-less wardrobe fall through to the aspirational variant. *Decision: no LLM in this path — the 1–3s budget rules out `/api/daily`, and "never a black box" argues for a legible pick. Styling intelligence here would need a new lightweight endpoint.*
- **Gaps decide the variant**: ≤1 gap → **the owned build (04b)**, her pieces plus at most one shop suggestion; ≥2 gaps → **the aspirational build (04c)** (`_lkAspirational`), where every gap is proposed. A gap with nothing to propose renders "Nothing in your wardrobe fits here yet." + its own way in.
- **Proposals come from `/api/alternates`** — the app's light, fast suggestion endpoint (flash, thinking 0, 500 tokens), asked for the slot rather than for a replacement. It returns TWO, so **Swap is instant** and only re-fetches once both are spent. Each renders as a full piece card (`.rb-lk-shop`: category chip, serif name, brand · retailer · price, "Not yours yet") with **exactly two actions — Swap and Save**.
- **No Anchor and no image carousel until the look is saved** — both belong to the saved card. `_lkConItems` collapses `count` to `{cur:0,len:1}` while `_lkBuilt`, and the built `rowCfg` drops `onFlip`/`onRemove`. **`_rbcRow`'s flick cluster is now gated on `cfg.onFlip`** (it rendered unconditionally and would have emitted `window.undefined(...)`); every other caller passes one, so nothing else moved.
- **The title comes from her ICONS, not the pieces** (`_lkBuildTitle`): two saved icons → `"{A} meets {B}."`. Fewer than two → the offered occasion-shaped name; with neither icons nor owned pieces it takes the lead proposal's word. The masthead gains `.rb-lk-namenote` — "Robes' name for it. Yours to change." — since the name is an offer.
- **The footer changes hands after a build**: Save still leads, then **Try another** and — only when the look is not aspirational — **Wear it today** (`__lkSaveAndWear`: save, then pin to today). The aspirational build offers **Build from mine only** instead (`__lkBuildMineOnly` → re-run with `mineOnly`), because she cannot wear what she does not own. Note the rule is `_lkAspirational`, NOT "any proposal exists" — 04b's one suggestion still leaves a wearable look.
- **DATA-MODEL LIMIT, flagged not guessed**: `look_pieces` references `wardrobe_items`, so **a Look cannot hold a piece she doesn't own**. On save, an aspirational build stores the look with her owned pieces and pushes every proposal into `wishlist_items` (`_wlSaveFromItem`), so nothing Robes offered is lost. The two-piece floor relaxes to ONE for a build carrying proposals. Holding unowned pieces on the look itself needs a migration.
- **A proposed piece has no photograph, so Robes shoots one** (same-day fix, Annie: "no images are populating for thumbnails or look"): **`POST /api/lookbuild/images`** runs the travel still-life job for up to 4 proposals (3s stagger, 50s race, Cloudinary-hosted, `logAI` feature `lookbuild`) and the client polls the existing `GET /api/images/:jobId` (`_lkShopPoll`, 4s tick, 300s ceiling), patching every frame in place. Until one lands the slot is a labelled breathing block, never a blank. Re-fired on Swap, cleared on save/reset.
- **The board carries the proposals too** — `_rbConsole` gained **`cfg.boardOnlyItems`**, so the LOOK panel shows the whole look while the rack keeps drawing proposals as `.rb-lk-shop` cards. `.rbc-yours` now counts that same set, or a four-piece look read "1 of 1 from your wardrobe".
- **A look made ENTIRELY of proposals still draws** (same-day fix): `_lkNewHtml`'s `!items.length` branch sent a zero-owned build to the "The look, once you start." placeholder, so the mosaic never painted however many stills landed — it now falls through whenever `_lkShop.length`, and `.rbc-yours` reads "0 of 4 from your wardrobe".
- **Save is open on any build carrying proposals, and says where they go first** (Annie, 2026-08-12): `__lkSaveAsk` puts a confirm in front of `__lkSave` whenever the look holds unowned pieces — "N pieces aren't yours yet. The look is saved to your Lookbook, and those pieces go to your Wishlist…". The floor drops to ZERO owned for such a build. A look with no owned pieces would otherwise save with no imagery at all, so **its lead generated still becomes the look's `photo_url`** rather than leaving a blank Lookbook card.
- Harness: `boot({pics:N})` decides how many fixture pieces carry a photograph — the build's whole behaviour turns on it (`pics:6` → 04b, `pics:1` → 04c).

### 2026-08-12 (fourth pass) — naming settled, black off the Look detail, key pieces off the Lookbook row
Three founder calls closing out the FTUE series. Verified: `ftue_harness` **157/157** (5 new checks), `looks_harness` **222/222** (2 new), `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **"Travel edit" is the noun EVERYWHERE** (Annie, 2026-08-12 — settles the naming flagged in the second pass): the strip label, the stat line (`N travel edits`), the card fallback title and the `+ New ▾` menu item ("New travel edit"). "Holiday" survives ONLY in internal identifiers — `__lkNewHoliday`, `_lkHolidayItems`, `.rb-lk-holcard` — and in her own saved trip titles, which are her words. Harness-pinned on the chrome alone, never on user data.
- **Black comes off the Look detail's action row**: `.rb-lk-act.primary` is white with a firmer hairline and ink 500-weight type (`rgba(32,32,33,0.28)`, cream-100 on hover). **The reservation is now app-wide — a black fill means the one real commitment on the screen, and only Save this look / Start packing carry it.**
- **A styled key piece rides its OWN home row** (`#rb-insp-row`, `_rbRenderInspRow`, mounted after `#rb-sn`): eyebrow **Inspiration**, "View all" → `__rbInspOpen()`, cards labelled "Key piece" opening the kp result. Key pieces moved to Inspiration in the 2026-08-10 IA but were still appearing in home's Lookbook row; the third pass stopped showing them there, and this gives them their proper home. `#rb-styled` (the onboarding handoff) is untouched and keeps its separate job.
- **A key-piece result lights Inspiration in the nav, not Lookbook** (`_rbNavSync`: `kpOpen` is now its own branch above the `snOpen || detail` fallthrough) — the beta screenshot showed `/inspiration` with a kp result open and the Lookbook tab lit, a leftover from when key pieces lived there.

### 2026-08-12 (third pass) — FTUE step 3: the look builder is INLINE ON HOME at zero looks
The rack replaces the learning card and the Lookbook row at zero looks, and the "Style a piece" card moves off home onto Inspiration. Verified: `ftue_harness` **152/152** (13 new checks over two new sections), `looks_harness` 220/220, `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **`#rb-lkhome` — the builder on home** (`_lkHomeSync`/`_lkHomePaint`, section eyebrow "Build your first look" + a right-aligned "N of 4 on the rack"): mounted whenever **the Lookbook holds nothing** (`_lkHomeZero()` — the same content test §2 uses) and the Lookbook page is closed. `_rbFtueOrder` gained a branch — while the module exists the sequence is `[styled, lkhome, conc, rail]`, so the rack leads the prompt directly under "Your piece, styled".
- **It renders through `_lkNewHtml({home:true})` — the SAME component and the SAME draft.** The home variant differs three ways: the name sits INSIDE the card (the section eyebrow is the masthead, so `#rb-lk-hometitle` leads the rack rather than a `.rb-lk-mast`); the rack is split by the new **`cfg.roles`** subset argument to `_rbRackRolesHtml` (Canvas + Anchor, then a `.rb-lkh-more` block behind `.rb-lkh-showmore` "Texture · finish / Two more slots, optional. — Show"); and every empty slot routes to **`__lkHomeSnap`** rather than the A2 chooser.
- **ONE composer in the DOM, ONE draft.** `_lkPaint`'s detail branch leaves `#rb-lk-body` EMPTY while the home module owns the draft, and `_lkHomeSync` removes the home module while the page is open — otherwise both surfaces answer a bare `.rb-lk-composer` query and duplicate the ids inside it. **Two bugs the harness caught here**: `__snOpen` resets `_lkView` to `'grid'`, so `_lkPaint`'s zero-content arming used to `_lkResetComposer()` and **wipe a half-built home draft** the moment she opened the Lookbook — it now only resets when nothing is placed (`if (!_lkUsed().length)`), else it just re-enters `'new'`.
- **The camera IS the slot** (`__lkHomeSnap(role)`): arms `_waAfterAdd` to pre-cast the role and land the piece, then pokes **`#wa-rb-cam` when it exists, else `#wa-rb-file`** — the dedicated camera input, never `capture` on the picker (the standing rule). Cataloguing is a by-product of building, so **home carries no "catalogue your wardrobe" CTA at all** (the learning card and its add CTAs are hidden).
- **Collapse and preview are the small screen's affordances** (≤767px only — both mocks agree, and the web frame's own note says the two columns are what let the preview show while the slots fill): `.rb-lkh-collapsed` hides texture + finish behind Show, and `.rb-lkh-composer .rb-lk-con > div:first-child` (the look preview + "Add a photo") is `display:none!important` — that override is needed because `stretchLeft` writes an inline `display:flex`. A piece cast into either late role **force-expands** or it would hide on mobile. Expansion is `_lkHomeMoreOn`, session-only. Module height: 534px at 390px, 587px at 1280px.
- **`_lkHomeSync` is the ONE place that decides** the learning card's and the Lookbook row's visibility (`#wtrk` display, `_rbRenderStyleNotes()`, `_rbFtueOrder`) — saving the first look retires the module and hands both back in the same call, so they cannot drift.
- **The home Lookbook row mirrors what the Lookbook holds**: `_rbRenderStyleNotes` no longer reads raw `snLoad()` (which carried key pieces — Inspiration's, not the Lookbook's). It composes **her Looks** (newest by `created_at`, opening via `__lkCardOpen`) with daily looks and travel edits, and **hides the section entirely when empty** — home never shows an empty grid. Without the Looks half the row stayed blank after her very first save.
- **"Style a piece" moved to Inspiration** (`_rbStylePieceCardHtml`, shared): home's `#rb-sn` empty state ("A little gift to begin") is gone, and Inspiration's empty state now carries the full card instead of its bare button — a piece styled three ways lives there, so the invitation to make one does too. **`#rb-styled` (the onboarding "Your first piece, worn three ways" handoff) is deliberately untouched** — founder call, it is working.
- **Gating is on LOOK count, not piece count**: a 10-piece / 0-look account gets the rack and no learning card. `ftue_harness`'s `boot()` therefore seeds one saved look by default (`{looks:false}` for the zero state), or every milestone assertion would be testing a hidden card.

### 2026-08-12 (second pass) — FTUE step 2: the Lookbook empty state is ONE door (Annie's four Lookbook frames)
The empty Lookbook offered three doors and no primary action — the "Ways to fill it" concierge clone shelf, a holiday-edit CTA and the + New menu. It is now the composer itself, and the first saved look is what unlocks the rest of the page. Verified: `looks_harness` **219/219**, `ftue_harness` **135/135** (its two ways-block sections rewritten), `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **Regression fixed first**: the composer's trailing **`+ Add a piece` now ALWAYS closes the rack**. It was gated on `allRolesInked`, which took the generic door away from the state that most needs it (the empty look) and implied a look caps at four pieces.
- **The whole dashed slot row is the tap target** (`.rbc-rghost.tap`, min-height 52px, `role="button"`): the `+ Add a piece` pill is the affordance, the row is the hit area. The pill deliberately carries **no handler of its own** — its click bubbles to the row, since two handlers would open the chooser twice.
- **ONE DOOR** — `_lkPaint` arms the composer whenever the Lookbook holds nothing (`!any && _lkView !== 'new'` → `_lkResetComposer()` + `look_compose_opened {door:'empty-lookbook'}`). Guarding in the painter rather than at each caller is what makes it a rule instead of a path — the seg's Looks tab, a bridge, and a delete that empties the shelf all land there. `snRenderPage` no longer branches to `_snPaintWays` (the function survives, unreached from here), and `_lkEmptyHtml` is likewise now unreachable. **`_lkNew`'s state reset is split out as `_lkResetComposer()`** so snRenderPage/_lkPaint can arm it without re-entering `_lkShelfOpen` (which opens the page, which paints it, which would arm it again).
- **The Diary is visible but inert at zero** (`#sn-viewseg button.inert`, toggled in `snRenderPage` off the same content check): tapping it leaves her on Looks rather than opening an empty month.
- **The first look populates the page**: the travel strip now renders **whenever the stream does**, edits or not — its zero state is the **invitation** (`.rb-lk-holcard.invite`: "Plan a trip." + the one black `Start packing →`) followed by **one dimmed, labelled Robes example** (`.rb-lk-holcard.example`, 60% opacity, `pointer-events:none`, "A trip to Ibiza / Robes' example") so it can never be mistaken for her own trip. Section label follows the mocks: **"Travel edit"** (was "Holiday edits" — note the `+ New ▾` menu still says "New holiday edit"; naming to settle). Stat line always speaks both halves: **"1 look · no edits yet"**.
- **Sort and Refine RENDER at every count and sit inert below four** (`.rb-lk-sort[disabled]`), superseding "withheld until a Look exists" — a control that appears from nowhere is harder to learn than one that is visibly not yet needed. **The threshold counts `streamN`, not `_lkLooks.length`** — the stat line calls the whole stream "N looks" and artifacts filter on the same axes (ADR-002 [C10]), so the control is gated on what it actually controls. A delete that drops the count back under four **also drops any live filter** (`refActive`), or looks would hide behind an inert control.
- **Softening across the four frames**: `#sn-viewseg` is a **cream well with the active segment lifting to white** (was a black fill), `+ New ▾` is a **hairline ghost pill** (lost `.primary`). **Black is reserved for the one real commitment on each screen — Save this look, Start packing.** (The Look detail's `.rb-lk-act.primary` — Wear it today, Update this look — is a different screen and was left alone.) The New look tile's hint reads "Built on the rack".

### 2026-08-12 — FTUE step 1: the look composer is ONE held container (Annie's web + mobile mocks)
First of a series of first-time-experience passes. The composer's parts were sitting loose on the page — the name buried in the rack header, the panel and the rack as two unframed columns, Save appearing out of nowhere at the second piece. It is now one card with the name leading it from outside, at both breakpoints. Verified: `looks_harness` **212/212** (composer + 390px sections rewritten, 5 new checks), `ftue_harness` 139/139, `addflow_harness` 102/102, `travel_console_smoke` 102/102.
- **One card**: `_lkNewHtml` wraps the whole `.rb-lk-con` grid in **`.rb-lk-composer`** (white, hairline `--rule-mid`, `--rad-lg`, 26px pad, whisper shadow). **No frame inside a frame** — the composer scope strips the Look panel's own border/background/padding (`.rb-lk-composer .rbc-panel`) so the dashed "The look, once you start." box sits directly on the card, and the ghost role rows take a `--cream-100` fill so they read recessed against it.
- **The name leads it from outside**: the title input moved out of `.rbc-rackhead` into a `.rb-lk-mast.rb-lk-newmast` masthead above the card (serif, `Name your Look` placeholder, 560px cap, no eyebrow — the page already reads Lookbook). **The rack head is gone from the composer entirely** — the four formula strips name themselves, so there is no second header; `_rbRackRolesHtml` is unchanged and the Look detail keeps its rackhead. (Harness rule: don't assert `.rbc-rackhead .ey` on the composer — probe `.rbc-rolestrip span` for the shared-stylesheet check instead.)
- **Save stands on screen, withheld** (`.rb-lk-saverow`, the last child of the right column, over a hairline): `.rb-lk-save` is always rendered and `disabled` below the two-piece floor (`--cream-400` fill, title "Add two pieces and this look is yours to keep") rather than absent — a missing button read as a broken container.
