It comes live at two pieces. **Save still closes the look out into the Lookbook** — `__lkSave` is untouched (create → `_lkView = 'grid'` → toast), no interstitial.
- **One alternative door beside it, worded for the account** — `window.__lkRobesBuild` → `__snWay('dress-me')` (close the page, arm the home prompt; the standing every-route-lands-on-the-prompt rule), fires `look_robes_door {first}`. Copy: **zero looks → "Or let Robes build the first one"**, any looks → **"Or let Robes create your look"** (`_lkLooks.length` is the switch).
- **Mobile ≤767px**: card pads to 18/16 at `--rad-card`, the save row stacks (`flex-direction:column`) so **Save runs full width at ≥52px** with the Robes door centred beneath it. The rest of the mobile spec note already held (no holiday strip / All-looks header / sort / Refine at zero — `_lkPaint` hides them in detail view).

### 2026-08-10 (second pass) — Travel Edit: one-scroll holiday edit (hifi `Holiday_edit_detail__hifi.dc.html`)
Supersedes the travel-surface parts of the 2026-07-30 looks-first restructure: the **three-tab result page and the step-2 build page are gone** — the Travel Edit is ONE scrolling page, and a trip opens the moment it's named (no generation gate). Verified: `travel_console_smoke` **78/78** (rewritten for the new anatomy + a 390px section), `looks_harness` 203/203, `ftue_harness` 139/139, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **Intake is where/when/vibe, then straight in.** The `#tv-brief-modal` is Destination / Dates / **Vibe** (the brief textarea is gone — "no long text boxes"); `__tvSubmit` → **`_tvCreateTrip()`** builds a canvas `tvData` (`{destination, dateFrom/To, dateLine, vibe, brief, plans, dayTitles: {}, capsule: [], looks: [], tripDays}`) and renders immediately. The Diary unfurl's travel commit does the same (`_ikCommit` → `_tvCreateTrip`; classifier `day_intents` with dates land as **day titles**, its CTA reads "Open the travel edit →"). **`__lkNewHoliday` opens the modal directly** (deliberate exception to every-route-lands-on-the-prompt). Seeds still ride in: a Look's Pack-it imports the look whole, wardrobe pack-a-trip picks + moodboard pieces land in the capsule. The build page (`_tvShowBuildPage`, plan chips, plan→look mapping, key-piece browser) is **deleted**; `/api/travel` survives behind **`__tvRobesStyleTrip`** — the "✦ Robes styles the trip" door on the empty-looks state (plans = day titles + `data.plans`; owned capsule → `shortlistIds`; a look styled for a named day comes back pinned to it).
- **The page** (`__tvRenderResult`, one scroll): masthead ("← Lookbook" back → `__snOpen`, title + rename pencil, weather pill, **vibe pill**, **"Edit details"** button) → **THE WEEK** (`.tvw-card` day cards — NOT `_dcCard`; the hifi's own anatomy) + day console → **LOOKS** grid (`.tvm-lookgrid`, `_rbLookTile` mosaics, pin-status meta "Fri 31 · Sat 1"/"unpinned", "+ Look" menu + trailing "+ New look" card) + look console → **THE CAPSULE** (collapsible drawer `#tv-capbody`; bar carries "The capsule · N pieces", the **`n / N packed` stat** and "+ Add pieces" → `__rbcAddMenu('__tvCapAddApply')`; Keep / Worth-adding cards with the **pack checkbox on every packable piece** — the masthead pack progress bar is gone, the stat lives with the pieces). Consoles unfold on tap and FOLD on a second tap (`__tvDayTap`/`__tvLookTap`); `_tvSelDayI`/`_tvSelLookI` are null until she taps. `__tvSetTab` survives as a scroll shim ('days'/'outfits' → week, 'rack'/'edit' → opens the capsule) so `_rbOpenPlannedDay` keeps working. Hero mood tile + on-page `stylist_summary` no longer render (data kept).
- **Day-plan titles** (the "new update" that reaches every calendar surface): `tvData.dayTitles[di]` — the day card's serif header is an inline editor (`__tvDayTitleEdit/Key/Commit`, "add plans…" invitation, ✎ on hover of a set title). **`_pdRowsTv` prefers the day title as the row's `activity`** and a titled look-less day is `planned`, never `free` — so her words surface on the home rail and the Diary through the existing planned_days pipeline (display; inline editing on those surfaces is a follow-up). The looks-first `_pdRowsTv` branch now also takes `looks: []` trips (fresh canvas trips band on the calendar; legacy `trip_day_plan` still honoured).
- **"Name the day, then pin a look"**: a bare day card invites naming (tap → title editor); a **named** day earns the "+ Look" door (`__tvDayPick` sheet: this trip's unpinned looks / "From your lookbook" / "✦ Robes styles one" pre-loaded with the day's title as the occasion).
- **Looks are added, and their pieces follow**: "+ Look" menu = **Add a look** (saved-look picker `__tvAddSavedLook` → `_tvImportLookInto` — the look travels whole and `_tvMigrate` resolves its pieces into capsule entries, so **pieces are added by default**) or **Create a new one** (`__tvStyleLooks` → `/api/travel/looks`). **`__tvMoreGo` now styles from a POOL**: the capsule first, and while the case holds <8 pieces her wardrobe rides behind it (cap 24) — returned `item_index` values resolve through the pool, wardrobe picks materialise via `_tvCapsuleIndexFor` — so a fresh canvas trip can style looks before anything is packed.
- **"Edit details" modal** (`__tvEditDetails`): Title / Destination / Dates / Vibe on the page. Shorter dates **clamp everything day-indexed** (pins, overrides, dayAdds/Drops, dayTitles); a moved destination/dates re-fetches weather. `_tvPatchSaved` now persists the identity fields too (`destination/dateFrom/dateTo/dateLine/vibe/plans/dayTitles/weather/headline`).
- **Weather is client-fetched** (`_tvFetchTripWx` — the old build-page Open-Meteo pair + `weathercode` → condition word, filling `data.weather` in the server's shape) since a canvas trip never calls `/api/travel`; an engine-styled trip keeps the server's echo. The masthead pills repaint in place (`_tvPaintMastMeta`, `#tv-mastmeta`).
- **Rollback caveat**: canvas-trip rows (`looks: []`, `dayTitles`) render on the 2026-07-30 build as an invite-only travel page (its `__tvRenderResult` guard rejects an empty capsule → "Could not build this trip" toast on open). Old saves are untouched — `_tvMigrate` is unchanged for day-blob and looks-first rows.
- **Live-pass polish (Annie's beta test, same day)**: (1) **the prompt's Style Me CTA hides while the unfurl is attached** (`.concierge-box.rb-attached .cb-send{display:none}`) — the panel's own CTA is the one action; (2) **the intake date inputs AUTO-SAVE on change** (`_ikDatesSync`: a start date alone lands with the track's default span suggested back into the To input; both dates run the full `_ikDatesDone` re-derive) and `_ikCommit` captures a still-open editor first — hitting the CTA can never reset a half-entered range; (3) **the week strip is ONE line at every width** (`.tvw-grid` flex + overflow-x; cards `flex:1 0 200px` capped 280px, 140px fixed ≤767px); (4) **the looks grid collapses to one row by default** (`_tvLooksOpen`, row size 4/3/2 by breakpoint; "Show all N looks ▾" toggle in `#tv-looks-more`; a selected look past the fold auto-expands; collapsing clears the selection) and the trailing **"+ New look" tile is gone** — the header's "+ Look" is the only add door; (5) the day console always carries the **Day / Evening switcher**: first pinned look = Day, second = Evening, exactly one = an "Evening · free" tab → `__tvDayEveAdd` (the `__tvDayPick` sheet) so dressing the evening is one tap.
- **ONE STAGE (same day, Annie's `Holiday_edit_detail__one_stage.dc.html` — fixes the two-consoles-at-once bug)**: the day console and the look console are ONE surface — `#tv-stage` in `#tv-sec-stage`, sitting between the week rail and the LOOKS grid; the per-section hosts are gone (`_tvPaintDayConsole`/`_tvPaintLookConsole`/`_tvPaintDetail` survive as aliases of **`_tvPaintStage`**). Rules: **a pinned look card opens AS ITS DAY** (`__tvSelectLook` resolves the first pin → day-scoped stage, `_tvSelDayI`+`_tvDayLookIdx`; the rail outlines the day; day-vary semantics + badges as before); **an unpinned look opens look-scoped** led by the **Pin-to-a-day bar** (`.tvm-pinbar` day chips — free days dashed, taken days name their look; `__tvStagePin` pins and flips the stage to that day) while every look-less day card in the rail reads *"pin {name} here"* (`.tvw-pinstage` → `__tvStagePin`) — pin from either place. Selection is mutually exclusive (`_tvSelDayI` XOR `_tvSelLookI`); a look-card tap scrolls the stage into view (desktop); the section is labelled **THE LOOK** with a quiet "Clear" (`__tvStageClose`) that empties it back to the dashed placeholder; the staged card carries the ink border alone (the "↑ on the stage" marker was cut — highlighting is enough); the stage section hides entirely on a trip with no looks and nothing selected. **The Pin-to-a-day bar is the ONE pin door on an unpinned look** — the console's "📌 Pin to days" head button and the old multi-day pin sheet (`__tvPinOpen`/`_tvPinPaint`) are deleted as duplicates (`__tvPinToggle` stays the write path; multi-day pinning happens day by day via the bar, the rail invites or a day's `__tvDayPick` sheet). **≤767px the open stage is a SHEET** (`#tv-sec-stage.on`: fixed scrim `#tv-stage-scrim` + `#tv-stage` fixed top-44px rounded sheet with a sticky `.tv-sheethead` — grab bar, title, ✕) — it covers the list, never pushes it; closing lands her exactly where she was; the inline section is display:none on mobile while closed. Look-scope editing (add-reaches-every-day etc.) now only exists for unpinned looks — a pinned look is always edited through its day (the swap sheet's "All pinned days" covers cross-day changes). `travel_console_smoke` **102/102**.

### 2026-08-10 — IA refinement: Inspiration · Looks | Diary · Holiday edits (Annie's wireframes)
Supersedes parts of the 2026-08-08 IA delta below: the top-level **Calendar tab is retired** (the third nav slot is now **Inspiration**), the Diary lives INSIDE the Lookbook, and the Lookbook's type shelves are gone. Verified: `looks_harness` **201/201** (rewritten sections + 8 new checks), `ftue_harness` 139/139, `travel_console_smoke` 44/44, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **Nav is Wardrobe · Lookbook · Inspiration** (`#rb-tn-inspiration`/`#rb-dock-inspiration` replaced the calendar entries; *superseded 2026-09-08 — Lookbook · Diary · Wardrobe · Inspiration, the Diary its own tab*). **Inspiration** (`#rb-insp-page`, `window.__rbInspOpen`, path `/inspiration` — server route + deep link + popstate) is the undated shelf: **key pieces styled live there, out of the Lookbook** ("Key pieces, styled" cards: lead image + two look thumbs, title, "Styled three ways by Robes"; card opens the kp result page via `__snOpenItem`, which now also closes the inspiration page). Card action is **Restyle** only — it lands on the home prompt with the ask re-armed ("Style my {title} three ways" unless the title already reads like a prompt). **Save-as-look is deliberately EXCLUDED this iteration** (Annie, 2026-08-10 — to be addressed separately).
- **The Lookbook is Looks | Diary** (`#sn-viewseg` returns in `#sn-headrow` with new labels; **Calendar renamed Diary everywhere**): `window.__rbDiaryOpen` (legacy alias `__rbCalOpen`) opens the month view inside the Lookbook — path **`/diary`** (`/calendar` survives as an alias route + popstate/deep-link), crumb `Lookbook / Diary`, the Lookbook nav tab stays lit (`_rbNavSync` no longer has a calendar destination). Seg-Looks tap resets to the landing grid (no sub-sub-nav). Copy: "it's in your diary", the weekly promo's "moving into the diary". The Diary still creates nothing (`__mvWear` unchanged).
- **The type shelves are retired** (`#sn-tabs`, `_SN_FILTERS`, `_snSetFilter` deleted; `_snFilter` survives as a constant `'looks'` for the looks module's guards; `#sn-grid` is a hidden legacy element). The Lookbook IS the looks view: **Holiday edits ride a pinned row** (`#rb-lk-hol`, `_lkHolidayItems` = travel-edit items, `_lkHolidayRowHtml` — horizontal cards with hero image, title, "12 pieces · 6 looks · 7–14 Aug" from `tvData` via `_lkHolMeta`/`_lkHolDates`, trailing dashed **+ New holiday edit** card) above the **All looks stream** (`_lkShelfItems` = looks + daily-look items only; **"daily look" is not a type** — its card eyebrow reads Look and its meta carries the date as STATUS via `_lkItemMeta`: "Worn 5 Aug" / "Planned · Sat" / the date). **Key pieces never enter the Lookbook** — a kp-only account still shows the ways-to-fill cold start (`snRenderPage`'s content check counts looks + daily + holiday only).
- **"+ New ▾" is one button, split two ways** (`__lkNewMenu` popover: New Look → `__lkNew`; New holiday edit → `__lkNewHoliday` → closes the page and arms the travel intent on the home prompt — the standing every-route-lands-on-the-prompt rule).
- **Controls sit with their section (same day, Annie's mock)**: the top bar row is the collection stat (`.rb-lk-statline`, "6 looks · 4 holiday edits") + "+ New ▾"; **sort (Last worn) and Refine moved down into the All looks section header** (`#rb-lk-allhead`, `.rb-lk-allrow` — label left, controls right, the Refine drawer opens beneath the row). Sort/Refine still render only when a Look entity exists; the stat counts stream (looks + daily) and holiday edits.
- Wear stays the one verb on a Look (today / a future day / the retro "I wore this — add a date") — no change needed; the refinement's Look definition was already live.
- **Beta generation outage fixed (same day, Annie's admin screenshot)**: every `/api/daily` + travel generation was 400ing at Gemini request validation — `LOOK_TAGS_SCHEMA.vibe`'s enum carried an empty string (`LOOK_TAG_VIBES.concat([''])`, the spec-F "else ''" escape) and Gemini rejects empty enum values (`INVALID_ARGUMENT: enum[7] cannot be empty`). `/api/style` was unaffected (no look_tags). Fix: `''` removed from the enum, `vibe` dropped from `required` (no-vibe = omit the field; `normLookTags` already returns `''` for a missing vibe so clients see the same shape). **Rule: never put an empty string in a Gemini response_schema enum — express "none" by making the field optional.** The admin Generation health modal (prompt/response/detail per call) is what found this in one screenshot — use it before suspecting quota.
- **Live bug fixed (same day, Annie: "can't open a Look or save a new look")**: `__lkOpenAsDay` rendered the daily console (z-40) UNDER the still-open Lookbook page (z-45) — a look card's tap looked dead, and the just-saved look's dead card read as a failed save. The fix closes `#sn-page`/`#rb-insp-page` inside `__lkOpenAsDay` AFTER the resolvability check (a false return → detail fallback must never blank the page she's on); harness-pinned via elementFromPoint ("the day view actually lands ON TOP"). `__lkSave`'s `_lkBusy` latch is now try/finally-guarded so a one-off throw can never permanently mute the Save button. **Rule: any opener that renders one fixed overlay from inside another must close the higher-z page first — display-checks alone pass while the user sees nothing.**

### 2026-08-08 — IA: looks-first navigation (design handoff "Three tabs, three tenses")
One home per object — **Wardrobe (what you own) · Lookbook (how you wear it) · Calendar (when you wear it)** — from the `IA__Looksfirst_navigation.dc.html` recommendation. Verified: `looks_harness` **188/188** (rewritten for the new IA + 17 new checks), `ftue_harness` 139/139, `travel_console_smoke` 44/44, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **Looks moved OUT of the wardrobe into the Lookbook.** `#rb-wsub` is All pieces | Wishlist again; the whole `#rb-lk-wrap` surface (grid bar / Refine / detail / composer — internals untouched) now mounts inside `#sn-page` as the Lookbook's leading shelf. The lookbook tabs (`#sn-tabs`, `_SN_FILTERS`) are **All looks | Key pieces styled | Daily looks | Travel edits** — the mixed "All" items shelf is gone, `_snFilter` defaults to `'looks'` and `__snOpen` always reopens there on the landing grid (`_lkView` reset; tapping the All looks shelf tab from a detail/composer also resets — no sub-sub-nav). `_lkPaint` guards on `_snFilter === 'looks'`; `_lkShelfOn`/`_lkShelfOpen` are the one open path (`__lkOpen`/`__lkNew`/`__lkGo` route through it, closing the wardrobe panel when it's on top). **`__waSetView('looks')` survives as a redirect** to `__lkGo()` (legacy callers + the `/looks` deep link, which now serves the Lookbook shelf). Crumb is plain `Lookbook`; path `/lookbook`.
- **Calendar is a top-level tab**, not a toggle: `#rb-topnav` + the mobile dock gained Calendar (`#rb-tn-calendar`/`#rb-dock-calendar`), `#sn-viewseg` is deleted, and **`window.__rbCalOpen()`** opens the same `#sn-page` shell with `rb-cal-on` (which now also hides `#rb-lk-wrap`), eyebrow + crumb "Calendar" (`#sn-eyebrow` id added), path **`/calendar`** (server route + deep link + popstate). `_rbNavSync` derives `calendar` from `snOpen && rb-cal-on`; `__rbNavGo('calendar')` is the tap path. `_mvSetView` survives unchanged for programmatic use (`window._mvSetView`).
- **"Wear" is the one scheduling verb** (rule 02): the Look detail's action row reads Wear it today · **Wear on a day** · Pack it (the pin mechanics are unchanged — `_lkPin`/`planned_days`; only the vocabulary moved: "Wearing it {date} — it's on your calendar", pins panel "Wearing it X and Y"). Every look card gained a hover **Wear** pill (`.rb-lk-wearx` → `__lkWearAsk` — opens the detail with the which-day panel armed; always visible on touch).
- **The Calendar creates nothing**: an empty in-month day at state `empty` (today/future) is wired to **`__mvWear(date)`** — a look-picker modal composing `window._rbLookTile` (never a second look card); picking pins via `_lkPin` (`planned_days` source_type `look`). **Zero looks → the door hands her to the Lookbook** ("looks are made there, then worn here" → `__lkNew`). Past-empty days stay inert; the legacy non-DayCard cell path is untouched (`?daycard=off` parity).
- **Bridges, not duplicates**: the piece edit form's footer shows a quiet **"Styled in N looks →"** (counts `_lkLooks` holding the piece; closes the form → `__lkGo`). Rack rows already bridge look → piece.
- **Empty-lookbook cold start layered**: a truly empty account (no looks, nothing saved) keeps the page-level "Ways to fill it" state (snRenderPage falls through — the concierge cards answer the page); once ANYTHING exists the All looks shelf paints normally and the looks module's own empty state serves. `_lkPaint` re-claims the shelf on a direct paint from the ways state (composer opened from the zero-looks calendar door, first look landing) — without that it painted into a hidden wrap.
- Copy sweep: `__cbLookPick`'s zero-looks toast now says "build one in the Lookbook first".
- Migration map (per the handoff §5): Wardrobe › Looks → Lookbook › All looks; grid/calendar toggle → Calendar tab; Travel edits + Wishlist stay; the removed weekly plan is not replaced — "Wear on a day" covers it.
- **Cohesion pass (same day, Annie: "the experience on The Lookbook is not cohesive")** — one card language, one stream, looks open as they're worn. `looks_harness` **193/193** (7 new checks), other three harnesses unchanged-green.
  - **The All looks shelf shows EVERYTHING the Lookbook holds**: her Looks and every saved result (key pieces styled / daily looks / travel edits; moodboards stay P0-hidden) interleave by recency in ONE grid — look ts = last wear (never-worn to the end descending, unchanged semantics), item ts = its save-epoch id. The type shelves are pure filters of the same presentation. Refine still speaks the look-tag axes, so live filters narrow to looks alone; sort/Refine render only when a Look exists.
  - **One card**: `_ltTile` gained `d.eyebrow` + the `lt-card` dress (white card, 3/4 image zone, small-caps type eyebrow, serif title, quiet meta — the item shelves' chrome). `_lkLookCard` (mosaic/photo, ✕ + Wear pill) and `_lkItemCard` (generated image, ✕, swipe attrs) both compose it, and **`snRenderPage`'s type shelves now render through `_lkItemCard` too** — all four tabs are one family. `#rb-lk-grid` matches `#sn-grid`'s 3/2/1 rhythm. `_lkEmptyHtml` is now unreachable (ways state covers truly-empty; any content fills the shelf) but kept as a safety.
  - **A look card opens as it is worn** (`__lkCardOpen`): assigned to a calendar day → THAT day's daily view (next upcoming pin, else the latest); generic → today's daily view — both through `__lkOpenAsDay` (nothing minted, "Wore it" accrues to the look). The **Look detail survives one tap away**: the daily view's masthead carries a quiet "From your look "{name}" — Look details →" door (`.dlm-lksrc` → `__lkFromDaily`, rendered whenever `dlData.look_id` resolves — so calendar-opened look days get it too); the detail also stays the fallback when pieces can't resolve, and the card's Wear pill still opens it with the which-day panel armed.

### 2026-08-08 — Day-chip planning, first slice: "Add a look" in the prompt + menu (design A.2, prompt-first)
The prompt is the sheet — no new surfaces, no verbs on the day card. First shippable slice of the A.2 handoff (the rest of the day-chip brief is still to come):
- **`#cb-addmenu` gains one peer row**: `#cb-addopt-lk` "Add a look" (injected beside `#cb-addopt-wa` in the same 1000ms setTimeout; count sub-line `#cb-addopt-lk-n` "Lookbook · N looks" refreshes on every `#cb-add-btn` tap since looks load async). Tap → `__cbLookPick()` — a `cb-lk-pick` modal of her Looks composing `window._rbLookTile.mosaic/cells` (handoff rule: never a second look card; the look's own photo rides `l.photo_url`). Zero looks → toast, never a dead picker.
- **Attach**: `__cbLookApply(id)` arms `_cbLookId`, renders the bundle's `#cb-attached` chip (look photo/first piece thumb + name), and replaces any photo/wardrobe attachment (`_cbClearPhoto` now also clears `_cbLookId`; the chip × and `_cbReset` clear both).
- **Style me pins, never generates**: a `_cbLookId` branch at the top of `_cbSubmit` (before the diary dispatch, so it covers `?diary=off` too) pins the look to the scoped day — `_ikScope.kind === 'day'` date, else today — via `_lkPin`, clears the day chip and resets the prompt. **The prompt text becomes the day's name**: `_lkPin(id, date, label)` grew an optional third arg that lands in the row's `activity` (which `_dcTitleOf` prefers), and the rebuild now carries earlier pins' names forward (`_pdRowsLk` alone would reset every date to the look's name). Nothing attached → the existing routing (day scope → `__dlSubmit`) is unchanged — that's A.2's zero-decision default, already live.
- **The pinned look belongs to the day card** (follow-up, Annie, same day — the gaps pass): the day peek is look-aware — the slot line carries the look's NAME beside the day's name when they differ ("Day · Silk midi look / Office dinner"), and a quiet **"Unpin the look"** (`__rbDayPeekUnpin` → new `_lkUnpin(id, date)`) gives the day back without a trip to the Look detail — the same verb travel's day console has.
- **A pinned-look day OPENS AS THE DAY** (second follow-up, Annie, same day — production feedback: "Open the look" bounced her to the wardrobe's Look detail, out of the calendar): `_rbOpenPlannedDay`'s look branch now routes through **`window.__lkOpenAsDay(m)`** — a synthesized `dlData` (no generation call: pieces resolved from the wardrobe with `wardrobe_match`, roles via `_rbRoleNorm(p.role)` else the rack heuristic, `headline`/`occasion_label` = the day's name, `anchor_date` = the pinned date, `context` snapshotted from `__rbCtx`, `look_tags` via `_rbTagsParse`, `look_id` carried) rendered through **`__dlRenderResult(..., { skipSave: true })`** — the full Daily console (date masthead, weather pill, Day/Evening switcher, The Look + The Rack, "✓ Wore it" / "↻ Restyle this day") with nothing minted. "Wore it" lands the wear on THIS look automatically (`__dlWear` → `__lkAccrue` matches the exact piece set); **"Restyle this day" anchors to the day on screen** — `__dlRestyle` now passes `anchorDate` when there's no saved row, else the fresh generation would anchor to today. The wardrobe Look detail survives only as the fallback when the look's pieces can't be resolved; the peek CTA reads "Open the day →" again. `_lkUnpin` is rebuild-and-sweep like `_lkPin` (names carried forward; zero pins left → `_pdDeleteSource`), and repaints the Look detail's pins line (`_lkPaint`) since "See the day" opens the same peek. **"Wore it ✓" on a look day now persists**: `_dcMarkWorn` re-runs `_lkPin` for look moments after accrual (a look has no lookbook blob to stamp — without the re-pin the row's status reverted to un-worn on the next fresh fetch).
- Deliberately NOT in this slice: any day-card verbs or new calendar chrome (the card never grows verbs), travel's in-console look picker, and the daily-path day-name flip (`_pdRowsDl` still prefers `occasion_label` over the prompt — decide in the day-chip brief).
- Verified: `ftue_harness` 139/139, `looks_harness` 171/171 (pin path), plus an end-to-end boot smoke (attach → Style me pins with the prompt as the day's name → peek shows look name + Open-the-look + Unpin → unpin returns the day to empty; no page errors).

### 2026-08-08 — Weekly track REMOVED (day-chip planning takes over; ADR-001)
Week planning moves to calendar day chips (separate brief) — this pass is the clean removal + safe landing only. **Supersedes every "Weekly Plan track" reference below**: the weekly artifact/console/engine surface is gone. Decision record: `architecture-decisions.md` ADR-001 (option A — sweep).
- **Deleted server-side**: `POST /api/weekly` + `POST /api/weekly/day`, `WEEKLY_SCHEMA`/`WEEKLY_DAY_SCHEMA`, the weekly-only helpers (`weeklyClosetBlocks`, `weeklyNormaliseItem`, `weeklyCategorySlot`, `weeklyTruncateItems`, `weeklyCoverageGaps`, `WEEKLY_ITEM_RULES`) and the weekly still-life image job; `publicSharePayload`'s `weekly-plan` branch. **`heroMark`/`heroDirective` survive** (daily + travel consume them) — they now live where `weeklyClosetBlocks` used to. `FORMULA_ROLES` survives (`TRAVEL_ROLES` aliases it). `/api/intent` lost the `weekly` enum value — its prompt now files a home-week plan as `unclear`, so the app asks instead of acting.
- **Deleted client-side** (dashboard-personalize.js): the entire `_wk*`/`__wk*` module (planner modal `#wk-plan-modal`, day modal `#wk-day-modal`, `#wk-result-page`, generate/imagery/console/evening/restyle/persist — ~1,000 lines), `_pdRowsWk` + the weekly `_pdSync`/`_pdSyncSaved` branches, `wkResultPage` in every overlay closer, the Lookbook "Weekly plans" tab + `__snOpenItem`/`_snTypeLabel`/`_shareBuild`/`__rbRename`/`_shareActiveEntry` weekly branches, the "Plan my week" chip/clarify options/suggestion pill, `_cbDetectIntent`'s weekly block (a week-shaped prompt now lands in the clarify loop), the intake's weekly commit + `_ikRestylePlan` + the whole plan-scope machinery (`_ikComputePlanScope`/`_ikAutoScope` — only the day chip exists now), DayCard/rail/month weekly branches (week chip, sage dot, "Open the week →", weekly bands). The bundle typewriter lost its weekly line (`PROMPT_EXAMPLES` in `144529f6….js`).
- **Precedence retiered**: `_pdTier` is now `look 3 > daily 2 > travel 1` (ties to latest `updated_at`, unchanged). `_pdSupersede` needed no change — it was always type-generic. `_rbTrackCfg`'s unknown-kind fallback moved weekly → **daily**. `_MS_UNLOCKS` dropped the 5-piece "Plans your week" milestone — the ladder is 3/10/15 (ticks 15/50/78%, fill interpolates through them; ftue_harness updated).
- **Existing data (ADR-001 option A — sweep)**: `_lbCloudPull` archives any `weekly-plan` row it pulls (`type` → `'weekly-plan-archived'`, blob kept recoverable in `data`) and deletes its `planned_days` rows via `_pdDeleteSource`; `snLoad()` filters `weekly-plan*` types so a cached row can never render or re-push. **Run `supabase/weekly_plan_sweep.sql` once** to settle dormant accounts in one shot. The backfill tools (`scripts/backfill_planned_days.mjs`, `supabase/planned_days_backfill.sql`) no longer emit weekly rows — re-running them can't resurrect swept data. `/admin` keeps both type labels for forensics.
- **Type set**: `lookbook_items.type` is now `key-piece · daily-look · travel-edit · moodboard` (+ the read-only `weekly-plan-archived`). No migration — the column is unconstrained text.
- **The concierge Weekly Planner card SURVIVES as a promo** (same-day follow-up, Annie: the marketing content stays relevant): cards are Daily(01) / Weekly Planner(02, `.svc-promo`) / Travel(03) — the promo keeps the calendar illustration + copy, its CTA reads "Coming soon" and the tap opens `KP.comingSoon` (week planning is moving into the calendar). `.svc-promo` cards are skipped by the empty-Lookbook clone loop (`_snPaintWays`) — a coming-soon door is not a way to fill the lookbook (harness compares clones against `.svc:not(.svc-promo)`).

### 2026-08-08 — Spec E: mobile parity for the shared console (Look Template)
Same skeleton, one column, nothing dropped — a CSS block in `_RBC_CSS` (`@media(max-width:767px)`, the "E · Mobile parity" section) plus two markup hooks in the shared console. Verified: `looks_harness` **171/171** (4 new E checks at 390px), `travel_console_smoke` 44/44, `ftue_harness` 139/139, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **01 One header**: the rack's duplicate `.rbc-rackhead .ey` eyebrow and `h2` title hide ≤767px — the look head (and each page's masthead) carries eyebrow + name; the rack's `.rbc-hbtn` action chips stay as a wrapping row at ≥44px. The composer's name INPUT lives in the rackhead but is neither `.ey` nor `h2`, so it survives.
- **02 Single-line rows**: `.rbc-mslot` (new span in `_rbcRow`, hidden on desktop) prefixes the slot into `.rbc-sub`, which reorders ABOVE the name (`.rbc-body>div:first-child` goes flex; sub `order:-1`) — slot · status share the eyebrow. The image `vslot`/`vcount` pills and `.rbc-hownote` yield; thumb narrows to a 56px column; `.rbc-arrow` 44px, `.rbc-act` min-height 44px.
- **03 Share badge**: `_rbConsole` appends `.rbc-share-m` (34px circle, check-badge register, → `__rbShare`) as the board's last child whenever `cfg.lookActionHtml` exists — appended AFTER the tiles so the `data-n` nth-child scale rules never shift. Hidden on desktop; ≤767px it shows and the full-width `.rbc-action` hides, leaving the footer to the dock. The Look detail passes no `lookActionHtml` (Wear/Pin/Pack are its actions) and correctly gets no badge.

### 2026-08-07 (second pass) — Looks Refine, custom tags, ghost strips, drag-cast roles (Look Template follow-ups, Annie)
Four amendments on top of the A3 + F tranche below. Verified: `looks_harness` **162/162** (11 new checks — bar/Refine, "+ tag" door + vibe: prefix write, draggable rows + re-cast + look_pieces.role persistence, ghost strips + ink-in), `travel_console_smoke` 44/44, `ftue_harness` 139/139, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **Looks tab bar: + New look & Refine-by-tags.** The grid bar (`#rb-lk-bar`) now carries the sort toggle, a **Refine** pill (ink `hot` state while filters are live) and a primary **+ New look**. The Refine drawer (`.rb-lk-refwrap`) filters on the four look-tag axes — each axis lists its vocabulary plus any custom tags her looks carry (`_lkRefineOpts`); a look's read is its stored `tags`, else the inherited overlap of its pieces (`_lkTagsOf` — the same read the detail shows, so legacy looks filter too). Within an axis picks OR, across axes AND (`_lkMatchRefine`); nothing-matches renders a named state with a Clear link, never a bare empty grid. State `_lkRefine {climate[], light[], wear[], vibe[]}` + `_lkRefineOpen`.
- **F2 "+ tag" — Wear and Vibe are open axes.** The tag sheet's Wear-it-for and Vibe groups close with a dashed "+ tag" chip → inline input (`__rbTagAdd`/`__rbTagCommit`; Enter/blur commits, Escape cancels, 28-char cap). A typed value matching ANY axis's vocabulary (case-insensitive) selects that option instead — the disjointness guard. **Flat-array round-trip: a custom Vibe stores as `vibe:Foo`; an unknown plain string reads as a custom Wear tag** (`_rbTagsParse`/`_rbTagsFlat`). **HANDOFF: Climate and Light stay vocabulary-only — never open those axes**, they have no prefix and customs there would corrupt the recovery.
- **B1 amendment — ghosted formula strips are the composer's education layer.** ~~Empty rows under forecast strips~~ **superseded same day (Annie): a slot must NEVER forecast a role — her trousers can anchor, her top can be the exclamation.** The composer renders NO empty slot rows at all: the four canonical strips always render (`.rbc-rolestrip.ghost` until a piece lands in the group — education AND standing drop targets), and the ONE dashed **+ Add a piece** is the way in, opening the **A2 chooser** (`__lkAddOpen`/`_lkAddPaint`, `#rb-lkadd-sheet`): step 1 "What kind of piece?" — **Still open in this look** (the unfilled preset slots, shown ONLY once the look already holds a piece; an empty look goes straight to the categories) then **All categories** (the sheet's fifteen) — step 2 the wardrobe picker (slot pick keeps the slot's legacy-cat filter via `_lkRowOptions`; category pick filters at sheet level via `_waSheetCatOf`) + **Snap a new piece** (arms `_waAfterAdd` → `__lkApplyNew`, or `__lkRowSnap` for a slot pick). A ✕ on a filled row removes it — the slot returns to the chooser's Still-open list, never a bound placeholder. `_LK_START_ROWS`/`_lkRows` survive as internal placement state (presets fill first; `__lkRowOpen`/`_lkPickerHtml` inline pickers no longer render in the rack).
- **Drag & drop role casting on every rack.** `_rbRackRolesHtml` gained `cfg.onRoleDrop` (+ `cfg.roleCtx` for surfaces needing addressing) — rows wrap in `.rbc-dragrow[draggable]`, strips become drop targets, one delegated runtime (`_rbcDndInit`, HTML5 DnD — pointer devices; touch stays read-only for now). A drop on a row re-casts to that row's group. Handlers per surface: composer `__lkCRoleDrop` (draft `_lkNewRoles`, saved at `__lkSave`), Look detail `__lkDRoleDrop` (silent apply — presentational like a rename, no promotion gate), Daily `__dlRoleDrop` / Weekly `__wkRoleDrop` (set `it.role` on the blob item, existing patch persists), Travel `__tvRoleDrop` (sets the FORMULA entry's role so the cast carries the look everywhere it's pinned; a day-scoped add takes it on the capsule item). All casts validate through `_rbRoleNorm` and fire `role_cast {surface}`.
- **Migration 16** (`supabase/look_roles_migration.sql`, **NOT yet run on Robes_p0**): nullable `look_pieces.role`. Until it runs, `_lkPiecesCloud` strips the column on a PGRST204 naming it and retries (`_lkRoleCol`) — casts on saved Looks then live in the localStorage cache only. Null = heuristic decides at render; no backfill.

### 2026-08-07 — Formula roles + Look tags (Claude Design "The Look Template" handoff, spec A3 + F)
First tranche of the Look Template spec (`The Look Template.dc.html`, design chats 2026-08-06/07): the four dressing-formula roles surfaced as Rack strips, and the look-level tag intelligence layer. Verified: `looks_harness` **151/151** (7 new role/tag checks — strips lead rows, Canvas-before-Anchor order, tag row, sheet axes, PATCH shape), `travel_console_smoke` 44/44, `ftue_harness` 139/139, `addflow_harness` 102/102.
- **Formula roles are display-level casting, sourced from the generators** (spec A3: roles attach to PIECES, Robes casts them, she never files them): canonical set `_RB_ROLES` = The Canvas / The Anchor / The Texture / The Exclamation Point (display order per the spec's examples — Canvas leads). Sources by authority: Daily's step titles (stamped onto items at flatten; "The Accents" folds into "The Exclamation Point" via `_rbRoleNorm`), Travel's `formula[].role`, Weekly's new per-item `role` (schema enum `FORMULA_ROLES` — `TRAVEL_ROLES` now aliases it — emitted by /api/weekly + /api/weekly/day, validated in `weeklyNormaliseItem`, budgets 12000→13000 / 2600→2800), then the `_rbRoleGuess` slot/name heuristic for hand-built looks and legacy saves (a cap/scarf reads Texture, jewellery Exclamation). The Anchor ROLE and the Anchor ACTION stay one idea (founder call 2026-08-07, spec A3 note 03).
- **`_rbRackRolesHtml(items, cfg)` is the one rack painter**: regroups rows by role (stable within a group), heads each group with a `.rbc-rolestrip` hairline — used by `_rbConsole`, the Look detail rack and the composer (filled rows lead with strips; empty dashed rows follow UNLABELLED — roles are cast as pieces land, spec B1). Handlers ride `it.idx`, so regrouping never breaks a callback — but **rack row DOM positions are no longer stable; never assert nth-child on `.rbc-rack`** (the looks-harness flick check was rewritten name-based for exactly this).
- **Look tags — four axes on the LOOK, not the piece** (spec F2: thermal Climate ×1 / Light ×1 / Wear-it-for ≤2 / optional Vibe, `_RB_TAG_AXES`): a quiet `.rbc-tags` row on The Look panel (climate · agenda · vibe; Daylight stays silent, Twilight earns its chip; empty = "Untagged —" invitation, never a required step) opening the shared `__rbTagSheet` (one modal for every surface; a third Wear pick retires the oldest; Vibe is the only group allowed to stay empty). Rendered via `cfg.tagsHtml` on all five surfaces: Daily (`data.look_tags`), Weekly (day or `evening_look` holder by reference — `_wkTagHolder`), Travel (per look, `__tvTagsEdit(li)`), Look detail, composer.
- **Generated looks arrive tagged** (spec F3 auto-tag): `LOOK_TAGS_SCHEMA` + `LOOK_TAGS_RULE` + `normLookTags` in server.js; `look_tags` added to DAILY_SCHEMA (top level), WEEKLY day objects + WEEKLY_DAY_SCHEMA, and both travel look schemas — carried through every normaliser, refreshed on day restyle (`_wkApplyDay`) and evening dress, persisted in the lookbook blobs (`_dlPatchSaved` gained `look_tags`).
- **Hand-built looks inherit** (spec F3): `_rbInheritLookTags(pieceIds)` maps the pieces' coarse tags (dominant season → thermal range, occasions → agenda vocabulary; vibe is never inherited — only Robes suggests a vibe). The composer shows the inherited row live (`_lkNewTags` null = derived each paint) and stores at save; passive accrual and variant promotion seed tags too (the variant carries its ancestor's).
- **NO MIGRATION: saved-Look tags ride the existing `looks.tags text[]`** (migration 14 created it unused) — the four vocabularies are deliberately disjoint, so `_rbTagsParse` recovers the axes from a flat array. `_lkPatch`/`_lkCreate`/`_lkPushCloud` carry `tags`; an emptied sheet stores null, never `[]`. **HANDOFF RULE: keep the four tag vocabularies disjoint across axes** — overlap would corrupt the flat-array round-trip.
- **Deliberately NOT in this tranche** (next passes of the spec, in priority order): F1 piece-tag vocabulary refinement (two season ranges; Occasion folds into Evening & Event), A2's greyed "Shop similar via affiliate · Not ready yet" swap source ~~+ open-slots-first category chooser~~ (chooser shipped 2026-08-07, composer), D activity presets seeding composer slots, ~~E mobile share badge on the mosaic~~ (E shipped 2026-08-08).

### 2026-08-05 — Wardrobe add/edit + Refine redesign (fast add, one confirm screen)
From the Claude Design "Wardrobe add-edit" handoff (11 screens) + the locked product decisions. Cataloguing is one confirm screen with progressive disclosure, not a form to survive. Verified with the REWRITTEN `scripts/addflow_harness.mjs` (**102 checks green** — supersedes the 41-check reveal harness; the reveal itself survives) + regression runs: `ftue_harness` 139/139, `looks_harness` 144/144, `travel_console_smoke` 44/44.
- **One form, four doors** (`_waFormPaint` in the `_waSetupAutoTag` closure, state `_waForm {mode, view, photo, readable, revealed}`): the photo add, add-without-a-photo, batch pieces and the **edit modal** all render the same `.fm-step` form — the bundle form survives only as the hidden save bridge (`__waSawSubmit` → `#wa-hidden-form` → `WA.submit`, unchanged contract). Three views: **summary** (read-only "Here's what Robes saw" — photo with tag pops incl. L2/L3, label banner, a FOUR-row ledger The piece / Brand / Category / Colour-with-dot; tapping a row opens the editor on that field; the staged reveal still plays once per piece) ⇄ **details** ("Edit the details" — compact 64px-thumb header so nothing shows twice, fields The piece / Brand / Category / Subcategory / Item type / Colour) ⇄ **tags** ("Add tags and notes" — see the axes below). Manual/unreadable adds open on details with the "Add a piece." headline + photo slot; edit opens on details with `Update piece →` + a quiet "Remove from wardrobe" row (`_waOpenEdit` is now 4 lines — the patched `WA.open`'s edit branch calls `_showEditForm(it)` at 150ms).
- **Step 1 per screens 02-revised/10**: the dropzone is breakpoint-split (beta feedback 2026-08-05 — you can't take a photo from a desktop): **desktop is drop-first** ("Drop an image here" + one ink **Choose files** button, `.rb-wf-dt`), **≤767px keeps the snap/attach pair** ("Snap or attach the piece" + Take a photo / Attach photos, `.rb-wf-mb`) — **Take a photo goes STRAIGHT to the camera** via its own `#wa-rb-cam` input carrying `capture="environment"` (the one legitimate exception to the never-capture rule: a dedicated camera button, never the picker), **Attach-photos rides its own photos-only `#wa-rb-lib`** (`accept="image/*" multiple`, no extensions), and desktop Choose-files + the drop zone + every poke-the-input flow keep the capture-less `#wa-rb-file` (whose extension list fixes the macOS-Safari multi-select quirk). **iOS ALWAYS interposes its source sheet on non-capture file inputs — verified against Apple's forums 2026-08-06: `capture` is the ONLY bypass (camera), there is no library equivalent, and no accept/multiple combination suppresses the sheet. Do not burn time re-attempting a direct-to-library jump; Photo Library is the sheet's first row and that is the platform ceiling.** — plus a **From-a-link field** (coming-soon door) and **"Add without a photo"** (`__waManualStart`; its details view carries an "Add a photo" slot → `#rb-wf-photoin`, a straight attach with no re-scan). The `__waAddChooser` modal is deleted — step 1 IS the chooser; the name survives and now just opens `WA.open()` (wishlist branch kept).
- **No native OS dropdown for category** (locked decision 1): Category / Subcategory / Item type are custom pickers (`__waFieldPick` → `_waPopShell` — anchored popover on desktop, bottom sheet ≤767px) reading `_waTaxTree`; attributes are category-scoped (decision 2 — L2 list only ever holds the picked sheet L1's subcategories, the Sub/Type row doesn't render for tree-less categories or when the tree fetch degraded). **Colour is a single swatch + quiet Change** → `__waColourPop` palette popover (screen 07). `_buildSwatchRows`/`__rbPickSwatch`/`__rbInjectSwatches` are gone; `_waSwatchBg(name)` (wiring scope) is the one dot-renderer shared by the form, the popover and Refine.
- **Two tag axes, held apart** (decision 4): **Season** (sage tint) → `seasons[]`, **Wear it for** (rose tint; vocabulary **Work · Evening · Occasion · Travel · Active** + free "+ tag" chips) → `occasions[]`. `WA_OCCASIONS` holds that vocabulary. **The default state is DISPLAYED, never stored** (founder call 2026-08-06: the Refine rules must read back from the piece): an untagged piece shows **Year-round** and **Everyday** selected on the form, each default is exclusive with its axis's specific tags (picking one displaces it, emptying the axis re-selects it — an axis is never empty), and `WA.submit` strips a lone default back to **null** so the DB never stores it. A legacy stored 'Everyday' folds into the same default at prefill. State `window.__rbWaDetail = {seasons, wear, addingTag}`; Notes lives in the tags view.
- **The fast-add payload is the irreducible minimum** (decisions 3/6/7/9): `WA.submit`'s `_V2_KEYS` shrank to `['seasons','occasions']` — **price, fit_confidence, sentiment and hero_position are omitted from the payload entirely** (PATCH omission preserves old rows' values; the columns stay reserved), the grid-card **star is the one favouriting mechanic** (now ALWAYS visible: translucent ink circle off, solid ink on — hover-reveal dropped by design), and **Silhouette & fit is cut from the flow** — no pills anywhere in add/edit/Refine; the analyse still lands `silhouette_fit` in `item_dna` untouched. The taxonomy fold + bounded PGRST204 strip-and-retry loop are unchanged.
- **Browse cascade + trail row** (screens 01/09): a drillable category tab opens the **Subcategory › Item type cascade** (`__waCatTap` → `_waCascadeOpen/Paint` — two-column flyout under the tab on desktop, on mobile a bottom sheet where **every pick applies the filter and closes** — tap, filter, done (beta feedback 2026-08-05); reopening the tab's cascade lands on the drilled subcategory's item types with ‹ back. Both mobile sheets (cascade + Refine) size with `dvh` + `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` so iOS Safari's bottom bar can't swallow the list). Filtering runs live via **`_waDrill {l2, l3}`** in `_waFilteredItems`. Below the tabs sits **`#rb-wg-trail`**: breadcrumb "Robes › Bottoms › Jeans › Barrel-leg jeans ✕" (✕ = `__waTrailClear`, clears the whole category trail; Refine picks survive) + italic serif live count + right-aligned **+ Add piece / Refine** buttons at every width — the **mobile FAB and the wardrobe "Pack a trip" pill are retired** (`__wgPack*` stays defined-but-doorless; packing lives in the travel intake).
- **Refine holds four things** (same-day design follow-up: the Wear-it-for tag was settable but not filterable — no way to ask for "everything I wear for work"): Season / **Wear it for** (an **Everyday** chip leads, then the context vocabulary + any free tags her pieces carry; filters on `occasions[]` with the **Year-round posture** — founder call 2026-08-05: untagged = Everyday via `_waItemWear`, and an Everyday piece passes ANY pick, she wears it for anything. the same Everyday chip now leads the form's Wear-it-for row too, selected on untagged pieces — display of the null state, stripped at save) / Colour (full 21-swatch palette, selected-names italic note; the colour WHEEL is gone) / Brand, with Clear all + "Show N pieces". DOM order is the mobile-sheet order (Season, Wear, Colour, Brand); desktop re-places via grid areas — Season | Colour | Brand on the first row, Wear it for full-width above the footer. Both tag axes keep their form tints in the filter (`.rb-ref-chip.sea.on` sage / `.ctx.on` rose). Fits, worn/never (the card's Never-worn badge carries that signal) and the L2/L3 chip sections are removed — `_waRefine = {seasons, colors, wear, brand}`. ≤767px the drawer renders as a fixed bottom sheet with its own "Refine ✕" header (`.rb-ref-mhead`) and a stacked full-width CTA over a centred Clear all.
- **Gotcha shipped with this**: the bundle's `WA.open` focuses `#wa-label-in` on a **220ms timer** — every `_waFormPaint` appends a hidden dummy `#wa-label-in` (the pattern steps 1/2 already used) or the edit path throws; at submit the dummy simply shadows the hidden-host copy for the label (same node written and read).
- **Two live bugs fixed with the beta test (2026-08-05)**: (1) **stale session token** — the dashboard boot captured `window.__robes_session` ONCE; supabase-js auto-refreshes the JWT (~hourly) and fires TOKEN_REFRESHED, so a long-open tab's copy expired and every REST write 401'd ("Something went wrong" on wardrobe saves). The boot's `onAuthStateChange` listener now re-captures the session on every event, and `WA.submit`'s catch names the session case ("refresh the page") instead of the generic line. (2) **Coming Soon under the modal** — `.cs` sat at z-index 300, the same layer as `.fm` (wa-modal) but earlier in the DOM, so the paste-a-link door opened invisibly underneath; `.cs` is now z-index 1000 (above every surface it can be opened from: fm 300, swap/hero sheets 950, form pickers 990).
- CSS: form/picker/popover styles in `#rb-wa-form-style` (injected by `_waFormCss`); trail/cascade/refine-sheet/star rules in the existing `#rb-wa2-style`.

### 2026-07-31 — 3-level wardrobe taxonomy (migration 15) — LIVE on beta
Pushed to `beta` (auto-deploys beta.byrobes.com) 2026-07-31. **Migration 15 has NOT yet been run on Robes_p0** — until it is, the degrade paths carry the release: saves strip `category_l2`/`category_l3` on PGRST204 (`_waTaxCols`), analyse's L2/L3 read simply isn't persisted, and the Refine Type section doesn't render; run `supabase/wardrobe_taxonomy_migration.sql` once in the Supabase SQL editor to switch the feature on. Categorisation goes three-level per the taxonomy sheet (350 item types, 55 subcategories, 14 sheet-level categories, compiled from Zara + Editorialist + ICP gap fills). **Columns, not jsonb** (the queried-across-pieces promotion rule): `wardrobe_items.category_l2` / `category_l3`, both nullable text — **null is the NORMAL state for every pre-migration piece, no backfill**. `category` stays the back-compat single-level L1 every existing surface reads.
- **`wardrobe_taxonomy.js` is the source of truth** (new root module beside `style_dna.js`): the full tree as `TAXONOMY_GROUPS`, plus `resolveTaxonomy(l2, l3)` (case-insensitive validation; unknown l3 under a known l2 degrades to the l2 alone, unknown l2 → null) and `taxonomyPromptBlock()` (one line per group, L3s pipe-separated). **The sheet's 14 L1s are richer than the legacy enum, so each (l2, l3) node carries the legacy `l1` it folds into** — Knitwear → Tops (except coatigan → Outerwear, knit dress → Dresses, knit trousers/skirt → Bottoms), Jewellery → Accessories, Tailoring splits per garment (suit blazer/waistcoat → Outerwear, suit trousers/skirt → Bottoms, full suits → Other), Activewear splits (tops/bottoms/jackets), sleep/underwear/co-ords → Other, Swim & beach incl. cover-ups → Swimwear. L2 names are unique tree-wide, so an L2 alone recovers its sheet L1 and legacy L1; nothing is lost by the fold.
- **Migration 15** (`supabase/wardrobe_taxonomy_migration.sql`) is **GENERATED** by `scripts/gen_taxonomy_migration.mjs` from the module — edit the module and regenerate, never the SQL. Adds the two columns, the `wardrobe_taxonomy` lookup table (pk `(l2, l3)`, cols `l1`/`sheet_l1`/`l2`/`l3`, RLS authenticated-read, 350 idempotent upsert seed rows), a **composite FK** `wardrobe_items(category_l2, category_l3) → wardrobe_taxonomy` (MATCH SIMPLE — a null l3 passes, so "l2 known, l3 unsure" stays writable; the server validates first, the FK is the backstop against prompt drift), and a partial index on `category_l2` (the Refine Type filter is the first consuming surface).
- **`/api/wardrobe/analyse` emits the pair**: `ANALYSE_SCHEMA` gained required `category_l2`/`category_l3` (empty string = no fit), the prompt carries the full tree via `taxonomyPromptBlock()` (~64 lines; `maxOutputTokens` 600 → 700). Server-side, `resolveTaxonomy` validates the emitted pair and **a valid pair DECIDES the legacy `category`** (the deterministic fold — `category` and `category_l2` can never disagree on a surface that filters both); an invalid pair degrades to the model's own single-level `category` with null L2/L3. All three response shapes (ok / noItemDetected / analysisFailed) carry the two keys.
- **Save paths carry the pair with the retry-without degrade** (acceptance: no surface breaks when migration 15 hasn't run): `WA.submit`'s single retry became a **bounded strip-and-retry loop** — on PGRST204 it strips the tier the error names (taxonomy cols are named in the message; otherwise the v2 fields), flips `_waTaxCols`/`_waV2Cols`, and retries, so a fresh project missing both migrations still lands the core row. Adds read `window.__waSawL2/L3` (stashed by `_runStep3`); **edits carry the row's saved pair forward** and a manual category pick that contradicts the filed L2 **drops the pair** (`_WA_TAX_L2` client map, L2 → legacy categories it spans — split L2s like Cardigans span two). Onboarding's insert and the legacy handoff persist retry once without the columns the same way.
- **Sheet-L1 display layer (same day — Annie: show the sheet's categories, and give L2/L3 their own fields)**: everywhere the USER reads or files a category, L1 is now the sheet's 14 (Knitwear, Tailoring & suiting, Jewellery…); the STORED `category` column stays the legacy enum via the fold, so swap pools, `_dlSlot`, generation payloads and old rows are untouched. Client statics in personalize: `_WA_SHEET_CATS` / `_WA_TAB_CATS` / `_WA_L2_SHEET` (L2 → sheet L1) / `_WA_LEGACY_TO_SHEET` (legacy → sheet, bijective on the 9) / `_WA_SHEET_DEFAULT_LEGACY` (L1-only pick → stored legacy) + `_waSheetCatOf(it)` (L2 wins, legacy mapping for pre-migration pieces). **`GET /api/wardrobe/taxonomy`** serves the full tree (1h cache) so `wardrobe_taxonomy.js` stays the single source of truth — client fetches once at boot into `_waTaxTree`; fetch failure degrades every new surface to the legacy single-level UI.
- **Piece metadata is a three-select cascade** — add step 3 AND the edit modal: CATEGORY (sheet L1s) › SUBCATEGORY (`#wa-saw-l2`) › ITEM TYPE (`#wa-saw-l3`), state `window.__waSawTaxSel {sheet, l2, l3}` with shared handlers (`__waTaxCatChange/L2Change/L3Change`, painter `_waTaxPaintSelects`; L1 change resets the pair, L3 disabled until an L2 is picked; cleared by `WA.close`). The edit modal rewrites the bundle `#wa-cat` options to sheet L1s and injects the same row after it. **`WA.submit` derives the stored legacy `category` from the picks via `_waTaxLegacy`** (per-L3 fold for split L2s — a coatigan filed under Knitwear › Cardigans STORES Outerwear; harness-pinned), so the sheet names never reach the column and `category`/`category_l2` can't disagree. The reveal ledger's Category row reads "{Sheet L1} · {item type}" and live-tracks re-filing.
- **Wardrobe filtering is sheet-level**: the category tabs are `_WA_TAB_CATS` (All + the 14 + Other) and the grid filters via `_waSheetCatOf` — a new jumper lands under Knitwear while a pre-migration jumper stays under Tops until re-filed (honest, no backfill). Refine's L2 section is labelled **Subcategory**, and a new **Item type** (L3) section sits beside it — both scoped to the active tab (item types additionally narrow to any selected subcategories), selected-elsewhere chips stay visible so they're deselectable; `_waRefine.types`/`.itemTypes` in `_waMatchRefine`/count/clear. Pre-migration wardrobes carry no L2/L3 → neither section renders. **Every other category surface (swap pools, `_dlSlot`, composer slot-matching, generation payloads, the travel key-piece browser) still reads legacy L1 only** — upgrade each deliberately, per the brief.
- Verified: `addflow_harness` 41/41 (incl. 9 taxonomy checks: cascade prefill, L1-reset, split-L2 fold on save, sheet tabs) + `ftue_harness` 139/139 green, module unit checks (fold overrides, case-insensitive resolve, unknown-l3 degrade), live analyse-route degrade shape with a dead Gemini key.

### 2026-07-30 — Travel Edit restructure: looks-first (the day-card restructure the Look entity was briefed to unblock)
The stylist insight (docs/stylist-reference-editorialist.md: "I don't start with a suitcase — I start with outfits") + beta feedback, rebuilt end to end. **A trip is looks pinned to days**, not a day-by-day grid: `/api/travel` now returns flat, day-agnostic `looks[{occasion,title,how,formula}]` built around the trip's high-level plans (Night in / Night out / Beach day / Pilates…), and the user pins each look to the days it suits. Supersedes the plan-first flow — the day planner modal, the day console, `✎ The real plan`, the Day/Evening slot pairs + travel evening verbs, the deferred-outfits path, the capsule-repoint flick and the stale-bar batched restyle are all gone, with `/api/travel/day` + `/api/travel/outfits` replaced by **`/api/travel/looks`** (occasion re-mix, ≤1 gap piece). See the Travel Edit section below for the full architecture.
- **The four new rules**: (1) the result page opens on **Looks**; "The Edit" pane is now **The Travel Rack**. (2) A day has no plan title of its own — a day is just the looks pinned to it, and unfilled days read *Free*. (3) **One look, many days** — 📌 Pin to days, tap Day 3 and Day 5, done; unplaced looks stay in the row. (4) **Adjustments know their scope** — a swap from a day defaults to "Only Day N" (`look.overrides[di][fi]`, badged "Swapped for Day N only"), the sheet also offers "All pinned days" (`look.slotOverrides[fi]`); the Travel Rack's card swap stays identity-everywhere. Both scopes point at capsule indexes, and a swapped-in wardrobe piece joins the case (`_tvCapsuleIndexFor`) so packing stays truthful — both pieces travel.
- **Intake (travel) — the unfurl is SLIM** (Annie's wireframe feedback, same day: "prompt should only unfurl to capture location, date & vibe before a dedicated landing page"): the travel unfurl carries ONLY the where / when / **vibe** crumbs (vibe = new `/api/intent` field — the styling mood the prompt itself carries, never invented; editable inline via `_ikVibeEdit`/`_ikVibeDone`, provenance-tracked) plus a quiet read-only "Plans noted, yours to place" chip strip when the classifier heard `day_intents` — no plan editing, no looks row, no build-around rack in the unfurl (the rack is gated off travel). Commit (`Next · Build the outfits →`) validates where + start date, assembles `_tvBrief {dest, dateFrom, dateTo, brief, vibe, anchors, plans, packLookIds}` and opens the **build page**. A Look's **`Pack it`** seeds both the pieces (`_lkPackSeed`) and the look itself (`_lkPackSeedLook`) into the intake/modal. Day-scoped travel prompts style a fresh look via `/api/travel/looks` and pin it to that day (`__tvStyleLookForDay`) — no more single-day restyle.
- **The build page** (`_tvShowBuildPage` → `#tv-build-page`, a dedicated full-page stage — z-40 fixed overlay in the app's own tokens, NOT a modal; the wireframe was structural guidance only): "The travel edit · step 2 of 2" / "Build the outfits." over Where/When/Vibe pill crumbs (each taps back into the step-1 modal via `__tvOpen({restore:true})`), then **plan chips** (`day_intents` pre-selected; `_TV_PLAN_DEFAULTS` + custom add), the **plan→look mapping list** ("Your looks, your plans" — see the bullet below; this replaced the independent "Looks you already love" tick-row) and **"Key pieces for the case"** (the key-piece browser with category pills + weather filter + Snap new, OPTIONAL — the 2-piece floor is gone, looks lead). `__tvBuildGo` requires ≥1 plan → `_tvGenerate()`. The overlay closers (`__rbCloseResultOverlays`/`_rbHideResultPages`/`__tvGoBack`) all drop it.
- **Plans are covered, not duplicated** (UX feedback same day: 4 plans + 4 ticked looks generated 8 looks — coverage was never declared): each selected plan row in `_tvPlanMapPaint` is either **assigned one of her saved Looks** ("✨ Robes styles it · use a saved look" opens an inline LookTile picker; assigned shows mini mosaic + "travels as styled" + ✕, `_tvBrief.planMap {planLabel: lookId}`) or left with Robes. A look already used by another plan shows dimmed "with {plan}" and picking it MOVES it (one look, one plan on the build page — pin-to-days multiplies later). Deselecting a plan chip drops its mapping. `_tvGenerate` splits: mapped looks import with `occasion = plan` and their plans go up as **`coveredPlans`**; only the unmapped rest go as `plans`. Unmapped Pack-it seeds still travel whole (occasion "Packed whole", quiet strip with "map it to a plan above" + ✕ via `__tvLookPackToggle`).
- **Modal path** (concierge card / wardrobe pack-a-trip / moodboard): step 1 = trip fields (dest / dates / brief); `__tvSubmit` → the same **build page** — one build stage for every door.
- **`_pdRowsTv` is pins-aware**: first pinned look per date → slot `day`, second → slot `evening` (the index caps a date at two slots), unpinned dates → free rows; legacy day-blob and deferred branches kept for old saves. DayCard strip + home rail ride the same rows.
- **Migration on render** (`_tvMigrate`): each old day slot becomes a look pinned to its day (evening slots keep an "· evening" occasion); old deferred saves (`days: []`) become zero looks → a "Style the looks →" invite through `/api/travel/looks`. `_tvPatchSaved` persists `capsule + looks + tripDays`, clears legacy `days`, and re-syncs `planned_days` — **this also fixes a live bug: `_tvPatchSaved` was called 12× but never defined on this branch**, so travel mutations threw and never persisted or re-indexed.
- `_rbSwapModal` gained `cfg.headHtml` (the scope control + "Back to the styled piece" ride the shared sheet); look cards and the intake/build-modal look rows compose `window._rbLookTile` per the handoff rule. ~~Deliberately NOT the "The Look" console~~ — **superseded same day** by Annie's UX pass ("we do not want to lose this beautiful functionality"): the shared console came back, see the next bullet.
- **The console returns + the result re-tabs (UX feedback, same day)**: the result tabs are now the lookbook's Grid|Calendar segment — **Days | Looks | The Rack** (`.tvm-seg`, plain uppercase labels with counts; legacy `'outfits'`→`'days'`, `'edit'`→`'rack'`; opens on Looks per rule 1). **Tapping a day card unfolds the weekly-style console** — `_tvPaintDayConsole` → `_tvConsoleFor(li, di)` → the shared `_rbConsole` Look panel + Rack in a `.tv-con` grid (mirrors `#wk-day .wk-con`); a day holding several pinned looks tabs them inside the panel exactly like the Day/Evening switcher (`__tvDaySetLook`, occasion labels). **Tapping a look card unfolds the same console dayless** (`_tvPaintLookConsole`; pins line replaces the switcher). **The capsule flick is restored** (`_tvConFlip` — cycles the case's same-category members, applied at the current scope: from a day → `overrides[di][fi]`, from the Looks tab → `slotOverrides[fi]`; landing back on the look's own piece clears the override — harness-pinned). Rack rows: `_rbcProvenance` + the scope badge, formula ROW NOTE, Pack/Packed or `+ Add` as the third action, Swap → the scoped sheet (`__tvDayConSwap`/`__tvLookConSwap` → `__tvLookSwap`); head buttons Unpin-from-this-day / Pin-to-days + Pack this look. **Imported looks are fully interactive too** (follow-up feedback, same day: "it should all be exactly the same") — `_tvMigrate` resolves each imported look's pieces to REAL capsule formula entries (match by wardrobe id, then exact label; an unpacked piece joins the case with its `wardrobe_match`), so saved-Look imports ride the identical console, flick, scoped swap, Pack and 1:3 maths; `pieces` now carry `{id, name, image, category}` from `_tvGenerate`, and the "Packed whole from your looks" line survives as the panel quote when the look has no `how`. `_tvLookBlock` remains only as the formula-less fallback. Free days invite "✦ Style a look for this day →" (`__tvStyleLooks({pinTo})`). State: `_tvSelDayI`/`_tvSelLookI`/`_tvDayLookIdx` replaced `_tvSel`; `_tvPaintDetail` survives as a repaint shim over both consoles so every existing funnel (`__tvPinSync`, `__tvPackToggle`) keeps working. No anchor on travel (no consumer — the restyle verb is `/api/travel/looks`).
- **A look is always adjustable, and the adjustment knows its scope** (follow-up, same day — the worked example: Look 01 pinned to 1 Aug is Wear A `{a·b·c·d}`; pinned to 10 Aug with the shoes swapped and a blazer added is Wear B `{a·b·c·e, f}` — ONE look, worn twice; neither pure immutability (version explosion, kills "the Thursday one, worn 11×") nor pure mutation (editing rewrites five days of history)): the console racks gained **remove** (row ✕ / swipe) and **"+ Add a piece"** (the shared `__rbcAddMenu` chooser — wardrobe pick joins the case via `_tvCapsuleIndexFor`, photo/upload arms `_waAfterAdd`). **Day scope** writes `look.dayAdds[di] = [ci…]` (extra case pieces, badged "Added for Day N only" — flick/swap repoint the add in place, the sheet hides the scope segment) and `look.dayDrops[di] = [fi…]` (slot hidden that day alone); **look scope** mutates `formula` (push / splice — the splice REMAPS every fi-keyed map: `slotOverrides`, each day's `overrides`, `dayDrops`, or later slots point at the wrong piece). `_tvLookEntries` is the one resolver (drops filtered, adds appended with synthetic `fi = formula.length + k`); console handlers are ROW-indexed and re-resolve entries at call time. Unpinning a day deletes its overrides + dayAdds + dayDrops; `_pdRowsTv` mirrors the day's effective pieces so the rail/wear log carries Wear B's blazer. Two-piece floor at the current scope. **Promotion stays explicit** — a travel variant never mints a Look entity; "Save as a new look" on the Look detail is the only door (offered-not-applied, entity-level).
- Travel harness rewrite: first tranche shipped as **`scripts/travel_console_smoke.mjs`** (44 checks green — seg tabs, day/look consoles, multi-look switcher, scoped flick + badge isolation, the worked example (day add/drop isolation, look-scope add, remove-with-remap), free day, imported-look console parity + case-join, rack pane, legacy-save migration); the remaining Stage 3/5 sections are still superseded and flagged.

### 2026-07-30 — Look as a saved entity (migration 14; brief `docs/look-entity-brief.md`, Claude Design handoff `Looks.dc.html`)
Wardrobe was a catalogue of pieces with no catalogue of **looks** — a generated outfit was a position inside a plan blob, with no identity, no persistence and no way to wear it again. This lands Phase 1 (the entity, accruing from wears she already confirms) + Phase 2 (manual build). Verified with `scripts/looks_harness.mjs` (**111 checks green** at 1280px + 390px: primitive, tab, detail, wear/undo write shapes, promotion, composer, degradation) + regression runs of `ftue_harness` (139) and `addflow_harness` (32).
- **Three objects, never one** (brief §2): `Look` is mutable and keeps its identity through small edits; a `Wear` is `{look, these exact pieces, this date}` and is **immutable**; the DayCard is presentation. `supabase/looks_migration.sql` has **no update policy on `wears`** and a unique `(user_id, look_id, worn_on)` — corrections are delete-and-recreate, and confirmation is idempotent so a double-tap or a re-synced blob can't inflate the count "wear more, buy less" is measured on. `wears.piece_ids` is a **snapshot**: the detail's history keeps showing the sandals a look was worn in after the look moved to slides (harness-pinned).
- **`LookTile` is the shared primitive, extracted BEFORE the tab shipped** (brief B2 sequencing): `_ltTile` / `_ltStripHtml` / `_ltMosaicHtml` / `_ltCells` / `_ltToneOf`, exposed as `window._rbLookTile`. **`_dcCard` now composes it** for the title and the thumb strip and owns only day context (eyebrow, evening line, membership chip, states, ring). The class PREFIX is a parameter — `dc` on day surfaces, `lt` on look surfaces — so DayCard's markup is **byte-identical** to its pre-extraction output and the DayCard harness's reference strings still hold. **HANDOFF RULE: any surface that draws a look composes `window._rbLookTile`. Do not build a second look card** — two components rendering the same content on different surfaces is the `planned_days` divergence in another costume.
- **Wardrobe gains a tab, not a destination** (A1): the existing `#rb-wsub` row is now **All pieces | Looks | Wishlist** (`__waSetView('looks')`, `/looks` route + deep link, crumb `Wardrobe › Looks`). The panel header still reads *Your wardrobe*; nav stays Wardrobe / Lookbook. Grid is `_ltTile` mosaics — **title only**, count on hover (printed at ≤767px where there is no hover). One control: a **Last worn ↓ / First worn ↑** toggle (never-worn falls to the END descending, the FRONT ascending — the never-worn question in one tap). `_waV2Sync` builds the surface BEFORE its visibility pass reads it; without that the first switch left the tab blank behind its own CSS default.
- **Look detail is the live console's components end to end** (UX-review pass, 2026-07-30 — the first cut drew the mock's own components; Annie: this was meant to be an enhancement of what exists): The Look renders through `_rbConsole` as the **standing 4:5 composition** (`rb-lookv2` board, note in the quote slot, palette, canonical ownership line) or the look's photograph at the same ratio; **"The Rack" is `_rbcRow` rack rows** — flick cycles her same-category pieces, **Swap opens `_rbSwapModal`** — and BOTH routes go through the promotion gate when the look has history (a flick on a worn look asks Update / Save-as-new, never applies silently). **Streamline pass (same day, Annie: Look/Daily parity + mobile)**: the **name leads the page** — `.rb-lk-mast` (eyebrow + serif title + the app's `rb-rename-tbtn` **pencil** → inline input via `__lkTitleEdit`/`_lkTitleEditing`; a standing input read as a form and buried the name below the stacked Look panel on mobile) sits ABOVE the `.rb-lk-con` grid with the action row; the card list is titled **The Rack** (was "The pieces"; Daily's `rackTitleHtml` occasion h2 was dropped — the masthead already names the look, never repeat it under the rack); **stats moved BELOW the Rack**; actions are THREE — `Wear it today` · `Pin to a day` · `Pack it` — **`Swap a piece` dropped** (redundant: every rack row swaps), and `.rb-lk-act` restyled to the console `rbc-hbtn` register. Stats are pieces / wears / last worn / **cost per wear, shown only when priced pieces and a wear both exist**. Titles are **offered, not applied** (A6). **Looks are deletable** — hover-✕ on grid tiles (the lookbook's `sn-rm` pattern) + a quiet "Delete this look" on detail, both through `_rbConfirmDelete`; FK cascade takes `look_pieces` + `wears`, `_pdDeleteSource` sweeps pins. **Tile metadata prints** (hover-reveal was reverted — in this app hover reveals actions, never information). **Notice panels are hairline + cream, never ink-bordered** — ink borders mean *selected* (anchored, active day).
- **A tap IS the wear, with a quiet undo on the card** (A4, C1 — undo-on-tap): no confirm dialog, no toast. Undo DELETEs the wear and decrements `times_worn`. **Passive accrual (B3) is wired into all three existing wear paths** — `_dcMarkWorn` (flagged), `__rbRailWear` (`?daycard=off`) and `__dlWear` (daily console): `window.__lkAccrue(pieceIds, …)` creates the Look if that exact composition isn't one yet, then writes the Wear, and OWNS `times_worn` for wears it records (each caller falls back to its own patch loop when accrual declines — already logged today, or fewer than two owned pieces). Identity is the **exact piece set**, never a similarity threshold.
- **Variant promotion is explicit or it doesn't happen** (A5/B5): swapping a piece on a look **with history** opens Update this look / Save as a new look / Leave it, and nothing is applied until she picks. Update mutates composition and says the wears stand; promote writes a new row carrying `origin_look_id` + `source:'variant'` and never touches the ancestor's `look_pieces` (harness-asserted). A look with no history just takes the edit.
- **The composer IS the live console** (Phase 2; rebuilt same day — Annie: *"the entire functionality should carry across, we are not reinventing the wheel"*, after a first cut shipped a bespoke card/rack at the wrong scale): `_rbConsole` draws The Look at the ratified 480px column (`body.rb-lookv2`, board tiles populating as pieces land), `_rbcRow` draws every filled rack card — the same flick cluster (cycling her same-category pieces), the same **Swap through `_rbSwapModal`** (wardrobe candidates by category + Snap mine, post-add hook landing the new piece in the row), the same corner ✕ (`onRemove`; a core slot returns to its dashed placeholder, an added row drops). The only bespoke markup is the dashed empty-slot row (`.rbc-row.rb-lk-rempty`, picker inside the row) — a state no generated console has. `+ Add a piece` is the shared `__rbcAddMenu` chooser; its apply (`__lkApplyNew`) fills the first empty slot that takes the piece's category, else appends. **Copy (Annie, 2026-07-30): rack eyebrow is "The Rack"; the name field is EMPTY with placeholder "Name your Look" (no "Robes suggests"/"Named by you" subtext)** — A6 still holds at save: an untouched field saves the offered name marked provisional. A dress in the Top slot quietly retires the Bottom row. **No extraction anywhere**: a photo is the look's image and fills the panel, hosted via `/api/wardrobe/upload` first so base64 never reaches a row. Handler idx→row mapping rides `_lkConMap`, rebuilt on every paint. **A slot never dead-ends** (Annie's shoe, same day): every inline picker — composer empty rows AND the detail's swap list — carries a dashed "New piece" tile, and an EMPTY category renders an invitation + "Add a piece" instead of "Nothing else in that category yet"; both arm `_waAfterAdd` and open the standard wardrobe add modal (`WA.open`, same poke-the-file-input pattern as `__rbcAddSnap`), so the freshly filed piece lands straight in the slot. The Looks grid ends with the same amplified `.rb-add-card` the pieces grid uses ("+ New look") — the CTA must not exist only in the empty state.
- **Pin writes `planned_days` with `source_type:'look'`** — the pin store is the index, so a pinned look shows on the home rail and month calendar through the same DayCard. `_pdTier` gives `'look'` the top tier (an explicit pin of a named look is the most deliberate statement about a day; it only ever wins on the index-fed surfaces, since the weekly/travel artifacts derive their strips from their own blob), `_rbOpenPlannedDay` branches to the Look detail (source_id is a uuid, never a numeric lookbook id), and `_dcPieceTotal` resolves the total from the look.
- **Migration 14 gotcha, hit on the first run**: Robes_p0 already carried an **unused, empty `public.looks`** table from an earlier experiment (nothing in the client, server or edge functions read it). `create table if not exists` skips silently against it, and the first policy then fails with a bare `42703: column "user_id" does not exist` — an error that names nothing useful. The migration now opens with a **preflight `do $$` block** that raises a sentence instead, and refuses to proceed if a foreign `looks` table exists. The guarded drop used to clear it (aborts rather than destroying data, so it is safe to paste):
  ```sql
  do $$
  declare n bigint;
  begin
    if to_regclass('public.looks') is not null then
      execute 'select count(*) from public.looks' into n;
      if n > 0 then raise exception 'public.looks holds % row(s) — not dropping.', n; end if;
    end if;
    drop table if exists public.wears cascade;
    drop table if exists public.look_pieces cascade;
    drop table if exists public.looks cascade;
  end $$;
  ```
- **Deliberately NOT built**: garment extraction from a photo (Phase 3 — must not gate 1 and 2); the Travel Edit day-card restructure (blocked on this, briefed separately) — `Pack it` instead routes to the trip intake with the look's pieces as the shortlist (`window._lkPackSeed`), per the standing rule that every route lands on the home prompt. **The wireframes' explanatory copy was not carried over** (founder instruction, 2026-07-30): the design doc's object-model cards, the 01/02/03 empty-state columns, "A wear is a fact, so it needs a date", the immutability footnotes — all annotation, not product copy. The empty state is a headline, ONE line and two ways forward.

### 2026-07-29 — Wardrobe logging: the onboarding reveal, every time
**Partially superseded 2026-08-05 (add/edit redesign above)**: the reveal survives as the read-only summary view (4-row ledger, no Cut row), but the collapsed full form, `__rbSawExpand`, the swatch wall and the fit pills are gone — see the 2026-08-05 delta for the current anatomy.
In-app cataloguing (step 3 of the add flow) was a full review form — 21-swatch picker, silhouette pills, notes, all expanded — which read as info overload beside onboarding's filing "magic" (the scan + labelled tags that wow every demo). `_runStep3` (in `_waSetupAutoTag`, `dashboard-personalize.js`) now plays the ONBOARDING reveal on every add; the form survives intact but collapsed. Verified with `scripts/addflow_harness.mjs` (**30 checks green**: reveal anatomy, collapse/expand, live sync, save payload, unreadable path, 2-piece batch) + a 139-check FTUE regression run.
- **The reveal**: photo panel with staggered tag pops (category / editorial colour / brand / fit — same spots and pop curve as onboarding's `kpTagPop`, here `rbSawPop`), the serif label banner, then the 5-row "What Robes files" ledger (The piece / Category / Brand / Colour / Cut) filling row by row ("Reading" → value, 420ms steps) — pure theatre, the analysis already landed in step 2. CSS injected once as `#rb-wa-saw-style` (`.rb-saw-*`); Retake is now an overlay chip on the photo.
- **Details collapse behind one row**: "✎ Adjust the details" (`#rb-saw-toggle` → `window.__rbSawExpand`) expands `#rb-saw-edit`, which holds the ENTIRE old form — label/category/brand, the swatch rows, fit pills, notes, and the `#rb-wa-detail-host` progressive-capture expander. Every field exists in the DOM from first paint (just hidden), so the `window.__waSaw*` state + `__waSawSubmit` hidden-bundle-form bridge is byte-for-byte unchanged.
- **The ledger never lies**: edits in the expanded form live-sync their ledger row and the banner (`window.__rbSawSync`; swatch picks sync via a delegated click listener on `#rb-saw-edit` reading `__waSawColor` a tick later; pill removal syncs via `_renderFitPills(true)` — the initial render passes no flag so the Cut row can't pre-paint ahead of the reveal).
- **Unreadable piece = no theatre**: `!tag.label` skips tags + ledger and opens the editor expanded with the give-it-a-name copy; the empty-label submit guard calls `__rbSawExpand(true)` before focusing, since the name field now lives inside the collapsible.
- Untouched: steps 1–2, batch add (each piece replays the reveal, details re-collapse, "PIECE n OF m" tag carries), edit mode (the full bundle form on revisit is correct there — revisiting IS editing), and all of `WA.submit`.

### 2026-07-29 — Learning meter (Clodagh test: "15 read as the wardrobe's item limit")
The wardrobe section is reframed from a **completion meter to a learning meter** — Clodagh read "1 / 15" as a cap on how many pieces the wardrobe holds. All in the `_ms*` block of `dashboard-personalize.js` + the `#wtrk` markup/CSS in `dashboard.html`; the harness pins every rule below.
- **No denominator, anywhere.** `#wtrk-num` is a bare count with a small-caps "Piece(s) filed" beneath (`.wtrk-num-lbl`, block span — the old `.wtrk-num span` "/ 15" rule is gone). The wardrobe empty state's "0 / 15" is now "0 · Pieces filed"; the concierge Daily pill dropped its "· n/15" tail; the styled-card nudge dropped "of 15". **HANDOFF RULE: never render the count as a fraction (`n / 15`, `n of 15`, `n/15`) on any wardrobe surface — the denominator is what created the limit read.** `_WA_TARGET` (15) survives as the last milestone + card-retire threshold only, never as a rendered denominator.
- **The track no longer ends.** `_msBarHtml` is scaled to `_MS_SCALE = 20`: ticks sit at each unlock's explicit `pos` (15/25/50/78%), the fill interpolates piecewise through those positions (`_msFillPct` — so it meets each tick exactly at its count; a straight n/20 would underrun the nudged 78% last tick), and the track's last quarter fades to near-transparent via a CSS mask on `.rb-ms-track` (75% → 5% alpha). At 15 pieces the fill reaches 78% — **there is no state where the bar reads full. HANDOFF RULE: the track must never fill to 100% at the last milestone.**
