- **Milestones name capability, not features**: `_MS_UNLOCKS` entries gained `cap` — **Dresses today / Plans your week / Packs your trips / Knows your taste** — which is what the bar columns render; `label` (Daily look / Weekly planner / …) survives for the derived headline + gating copy. Section eyebrow is a static "Robes is learning" (the JS `Your wardrobe`/`Wardrobe completion` toggle is gone — "completion" was the frame being retired).
- **No lock language**: the headline's earned branch reads "{Feature} just opened." (was "unlocked"), the wardrobe empty-state header reads "What more pieces bring" (was "What pieces unlock"). **HANDOFF RULE: features are never locked and copy never says lock/unlock** — this restates the app-wide never-lock convention at the copy level so it can't regress in build.
- Harness updated in step (`scripts/ftue_harness.mjs`): capability labels, tick positions, piecewise fill, fade mask, bare-count + no-denominator + eyebrow assertions per count, pill no-fraction at 3–14, empty-state header/no-unlock checks — **139 checks green** (supersedes the 110 count below).

### 2026-07-28 — Onboarding was bypassed for a second signup in the same tab
`rb_onboarded` was a **single unscoped sessionStorage key**, set when any account finished onboarding and never cleared. `onboarding.html` checks it **before** the `onboarded_at` lookup, so a tab that had already onboarded one account sent the *next* account signed up in it straight to `/dashboard` — the flow skipped entirely, and the dashboard gate read the same flag so it never bounced back. The new user's `onboarded_at` was correctly `null` the whole time; nothing ever read it. Hit by anyone testing repeated signups in one browser tab.
- Same bug class as the lookbook's `__anon` keys — **an unscoped browser key leaking across accounts**. Fixed the same way: `rb_onboarded__<uid>` in both readers (`onboarding.html`, `dashboard.html`) and the writer, plus a `removeItem('rb_onboarded')` on the way past so a tab still carrying the legacy global recovers on next load.
- The flag keeps its real job: a loop guard **for that same user** when the `onboarded_at` write failed.
- **Open risk, not yet fixed:** `supabase/onboarding_migration.sql` ends with an unguarded `update … set onboarded_at = coalesce(created_at, now()) where onboarded_at is null;`. The migration list above invites re-running "so a fresh project (or a missed one) can be reconciled" — **re-running that file stamps every pending user as onboarded, permanently**. Guard it before the list is replayed again.

### 2026-07-28 — Intake rack thumbnails, Cancel label, desktop multi-select
- **Intake rack thumbnails were stretched**: `#rb-intake .ik-ri .im` is a `<span>` with no `display`, so it stayed **inline — and `aspect-ratio` does not apply to non-replaced inline elements**. Inside `.ik-rack` (a flex row, default `align-items:stretch`) each tile grew to the tallest sibling, so a long two-line label stretched every image with it (measured 82×108 instead of 82×82). Fixed with `display:block` on `.im` plus `align-items:flex-start` on the rack. **Any `aspect-ratio` box that is a `<span>` needs an explicit `display`** — this pattern recurs across the injected racks.
- **`Never mind` → `Cancel`** on both intake action rows (`.ik-cancel`): on a config panel the soft dismissal read as optional rather than as the way out.
- **Desktop multi-select hardening**: `#wa-rb-file` already carried `multiple`, and every add entry point (tracker CTA / fast-add / add tile / grid card / panel button / empty-state CTA) was verified to open a multi-select picker and queue a 3-file batch in Chromium — the reported failure could **not** be reproduced there, so this is browser-specific (Safari/macOS the likeliest). Two known Safari quirks pre-empted: the wildcard-only `accept="image/*"` gained explicit extensions (`.jpg,.jpeg,.png,.heic,.heif,.webp`), and the input moved off `display:none` to a visually-hidden clip rect, since Safari has historically not reliably activated fully-hidden file inputs inside a `<label>`. If it still fails, get the browser + version before changing more.

### 2026-07-28 — Homescreen FTUE (Claude Design handoff: "Guided homescreen, empty to unlocked")
**Partially superseded 2026-07-29 (Learning meter delta above)**: the bar's presentation — ticks/fill scale, "n / 15" numeral, milestone labels, "Wardrobe completion" eyebrow — changed; the thresholds, gating, module order and card-retire rules below still hold. The first-time run is now a single milestone ladder. **One config drives everything**: `_MS_UNLOCKS = [{at:3,daily},{at:5,weekly},{at:10,travel},{at:15,styleNotes}]` in `dashboard-personalize.js` — re-tune the thresholds there and the progress card, the wardrobe empty state and the concierge gate all follow. No other gating flag. Verified with `scripts/ftue_harness.mjs` (boots the real dashboard against a Supabase stub at n = 0/1/3/5/10/15/16 plus both empty states, the first-load race and 390px): **110 checks green**. Run it with `npm i --no-save playwright && node scripts/ftue_harness.mjs` (set `CHROME_PATH` if playwright's bundled browser build isn't installed).
- **The progress card is the hero** (`#wtrk`): the plain bar + kicker/copy are replaced by the shared milestone bar (`_msBarHtml`) — ticks at 3/5/10/15 and four labelled columns. **No per-tick status text** (2026-07-28, founder call): "Locked" would be a lie since every edit is built and open, "n away" repeats the headline, and earned is already carried by the tick + numeral going dark — the headline states, the bar shows. **The card is height-budgeted**: numeral and label share one line, the CTA sits inline in the top row, and the section holds ~365px at 1280px against ~353px for the pre-FTUE tracker. The harness asserts `<= 380px` so it can't silently balloon again. The headline is **derived, never authored** (`_msHeadline`): it names the next unlock and the distance to it, or what was just earned at a boundary. Fill is `itemCount/15` on a 650ms `cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)` (disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`). **"Just unlocked" is a one-session label** — `_msTrack` seeds `_msPrevCount` from `rb_ms_last__<uid>` so a threshold crossed between sessions still reads as earned on the first render back. **The card retires at ≥16, not ≥15** (changed): at 15 it still has a job — Style Notes has only just unlocked — and from 16 count + adding live in the Wardrobe tab, with nothing in its place.
- **Module order splits on the first unlock** (`_rbFtueOrder`, idempotent, runs on every `_waSyncCounts`): below 3 pieces the progress card leads directly under the greeting; from 3 the prompt leads again as it did before, preserving the 4.7 mobile above-the-fold call. `#rb-rail` travels with `.concierge` in both; `#rb-styled` always sits immediately above the tracker, which is where its own `mount()` puts it, so the two never fight.
- **The concierge is absent below 3 pieces** (`_rbGateConcierge`) — a brand-new user is never shown a shelf of edits she has nothing to feed. From 3 the section appears with **all three edits open**: Daily Look, Weekly Planner and Travel Edit are all built, and the app-wide never-lock convention holds (the design's 5/10-piece locks were deliberately NOT implemented — founder call).
- **Style Notes is introduced once, at 15** (`_rbSilPrompt`, now a re-runnable function rather than a one-shot timeout, since the count lands async): absent below the threshold, then a **dark card** — the only dark surface on home, so the introduction reads as an arrival rather than another nudge. Still gated on missing `style_dna` chapters and dismissible per user.
- **Weather is deliberately UNCHANGED.** The mock's strip was placeholder art, not a spec (founder call, 2026-07-28) — an FTUE pass that promoted it to every width was reverted. Desktop keeps the nav pill (`#nav-weather`); under 768px the nav pill hides and `.dash-wx-m` carries icon · day · city · temp under the greeting. Don't re-derive this from the mock.
- **Wardrobe empty state** loses its prose block (too text heavy on the emptiest screen — this supersedes the copy approved 2026-07-27) and gains the **same milestone bar**, `status:false`, under a "What pieces unlock" header, plus two decorative `.rb-ghost-card` tiles behind the live add card.
- **Lookbook empty state** gains "Ways to fill it" (`_snPaintWays`): it **clones the real `.svc` concierge cards** off the dashboard rather than hand-rolling a second set of rows — one design, one place to change it, imagery included. **Cloning is gated on `.svc-daily`** — the marker personalize leaves when it relabels/reorders the cards at 600ms — with a bounded 150ms retry and a repaint hook fired at the end of that transform. Keying on the transformed DOM rather than a timer is what makes it deterministic: any open that beat 600ms used to paint the legacy bundle trio ("Key piece, three ways", photo imagery, wrong order) and only corrected on refresh. Until the transform lands the bare empty state shows; it never paints legacy. Each clone is stripped of its inline `onclick` (cloneNode carries the bundle's modal-opener ATTRIBUTE even where personalize overrode the property) and its `.rb-lock-wrap` progress pill, then wired to `window.__snWay(intent)` → close the page → `_cbSetIntent(intent)`. **Every route lands on the home prompt box** — the empty Lookbook never opens a modal of its own (founder call 2026-07-28: the hand-rolled version "opens old paths to logging"). `_CHIP_DEFS` gained a `travel` scaffold purely so `_cbSetIntent('travel')` can arm the prompt; no chip renders it. Removed the moment anything is saved.
- **Deliberately not built** (the mock predates this branch): the mock's three-tile week rail — `#rb-rail` already IS that module, wired to `planned_days` and sharing the DayCard renderer, so it was left untouched; the guided anchor-chip prompt builder — the live box (textarea + `+` menu + chips) stays at every count, founder call.

### 2026-07-28 — Gender identity setting (migration 13)
"How do you identify?" lives in the Account details modal as a **collapsible field** (set once, then rarely revisited — collapsed by default, header shows the current value): Woman / Man / Prefer not to say. **Every new signup defaults to `woman`** via the `profiles.gender_identity` column default (`supabase/gender_identity_migration.sql`, migration 13 — ADD COLUMN backfills existing rows too), and the value normalises to `'woman'` at every read (`_rbGender()` client-side, `normGender()` server-side), so a pre-migration profile behaves exactly as before.
- **Server** (`server.js`): `genderDirective(g)` replaces the six hard-coded "Unless the brief clearly indicates a male wearer, style for a woman." system-prompt lines (`/api/daily`, `/api/weekly`, `/api/weekly/day`, `/api/travel`, `/api/travel/day`, `/api/travel/outfits`) and is injected into `/api/alternates` + `/api/moodboard`; `/api/style` keeps its richer woman-default sentence and swaps in the directive for the other two values. **`man` = menswear only, everywhere** — male cuts/sizing/brands, never womenswear; the directive also tells the model the prompts' generic "she"/"her" refers to this male client (the stylist prompts stay she-voiced rather than rewriting every pronoun per-request). **`unspecified` ("Prefer not to say") = model's best judgement from the brief**, gender-neutral when nothing points a direction. Imagery follows: `wearerNoun`/`wearerWears` drive the Daily anchor shot and Travel hero ("one man… He wears" / "one person… They wear"); `/api/style`'s `wearer` field is forced to `man` under the man setting, otherwise the model's judgement stands (so frames match the looks it wrote); the moodboard `hero_looks` rules name the model's gender. Weekly/other stills are garment-only still-lifes — no change needed.
- **Client** (`dashboard-personalize.js`): `_rbGender()` (by `_rbStyleDna`), sent as `gender` on all ten generation POSTs (style ×3 incl. the onboarding-handoff quietStyle, daily, weekly, weekly/day, travel, travel/day, alternates, moodboard). The account modal's save PATCHes `gender_identity` and **retries without the column when the migration hasn't run** (error body names the column), so the rest of the form still saves; the in-memory `__robes_profile` syncs on success. Selected pill = warm cream `#F3EFE6` + `#C9BCA6` border + ✓ per the app convention. The dashboard boot select gained `gender_identity` with its own retry tier (gender → onboarded_at → bare).
- Onboarding needs no change — the column default covers the `handle_new_user` insert. Style Notes analysis/try-on (her own photo, identity-preserving) is deliberately untouched.

### 2026-07-24 — DayCard convergence (design spec v2.0 + engineering brief; audit `docs/daycard-audit.md`)
One canonical day renderer across the four day surfaces — home rail, weekly strip, travel strip, lookbook calendar. All code in the **DayCard module** in `dashboard-personalize.js` (right after `_rbDayStrip`): `_dcCard(data, opts)` (the renderer, CSS `#rb-dc-style`, classes `.rb-dc`), `_dcMoments(dayM, eveM, o)` (the normaliser — planned_days-shaped moments → DayCardData `{eyebrow,title,evening,pieces,pieceTotal,chip,state,modifier,pinned}`), plus the peek overlay + `_rbSetFocus`. **Flag: default ON, `?daycard=off` is the per-device kill switch** (localStorage `rb_daycard`; every surface falls back to its exact pre-DayCard render — harness-verified parity). Verified with a Playwright harness (Supabase shim + REST stubs): **65 checks green** at 1280px + 390px covering all four surfaces, peek actions, ghost-band supersession, flag-off parity and the mobile treatments.
- **Live-pass polish (Annie, same day)**: (1) **No ring on the strips** — body-tap already lands the console on the day, so the "Focus day" sparkle read as a second, different action; the ring survives only on the home rail where it genuinely differs from the body (peek vs prompt-scope). `_rbSetFocus` keeps its weekly/travel branches for programmatic use. (2) **Mobile shows 2 thumbs** — 3 + the `+N` chip bled off narrow cards; the 3rd thumb carries `.t3` (hidden ≤767px) and a mobile-only `.ov.m` chip re-counts against 2 shown (desktop keeps 3 + `.ov.d`); `.rb-dc` also gained `overflow:hidden` as a belt. (3) **Mobile calendar compaction** — compact cells were 150px slivers with bleeding thumbs at 390px (the DayCard path had no <768px rules, unlike legacy `.rb-mc`): ≤1000px cell thumbs hide, ≤767px cells go square (aspect 1, 5px pad), the evening text line + footer status hide and a ☾ `.dc-evemark` carries the two-moment signal.
- **One data path (§6.4 decision)**: rail + calendar read `planned_days` as before; the **strips now derive DayCardData LIVE from the plan blob through the SAME row builders the index uses** (`_wkStripV2` → `_pdRowsWk`, `_tvWeekV2` → `_pdRowsTv`) — one shape, no freshness gap, the index stays persistence-only. Old saves that can't emit dated rows (no `week_iso`/`dateFrom`) fall back to the legacy strip automatically.
- **Action model (spec §6)**: body opens, ring sets focus, no "Open →" labels anywhere. Rail/calendar body-tap → **day peek overlay** (`__rbDayPeek`, `#rb-dpk` — Annie's §6.2 call: day + evening moments at a glance, "Open the day →" click-through to `_rbOpenPlannedDay`, and **"Wore it ✓"** for past days via `_dcMarkWorn`, which repaints via `_rbRailRepaint` — a soft in-place repaint, because a refetch would revert the worn stamp until the debounced upsert lands). Strip body-tap lands the console on the day (`__wkOpenDay`/`__tvOpenDay` — inside the artifact the console IS the day's open view); the top-right **ring** (`.dc-ring`, sparkle) scopes the prompt on home (`__rbRailFocus` → `_ikScopeDay`) and re-points the console on the strips (`_rbSetFocus`). The rail's old flag-dependent body branch (`__rbRailTap`) survives only on the `?daycard=off` path. Empty/free cards fold into ONE empty state ("Nothing yet. / Tap to dress it." — component rule) whose tap aims the prompt (`__rbRailScope`).
- **Title config point (audit D-02, Annie: user's words win)**: `_DC_TITLE_MODE = 'user'` — `_dcTitleOf` prefers `activity` (her words) over `headline` (the rewrite), sentence-cased via `_dcSentence` (first letter only — never lowercase user text, proper nouns). `_wkApplyDay`'s occasion overwrite is untouched; her words surface anyway because the row builders prefer `user_activity`. Flip to `'robes'` to invert everywhere.
- **Slots per spec v2.0**: eyebrow (full = uppercase Inter; compact = serif numeral; travel = `DAY N`, no calendar date), evening line `Evening · {occasion}` with word-boundary truncation (`_dcTruncate`; the `Evening · Evening look` placeholder renders as bare "Evening" — §11.3), **thumbnails 3 + `+N` overflow** (from the LEAD moment only so the denominator matches the visible look; `_dcPieceTotal` resolves the total from the parent blob via the lookbook cache — `item_ids` is owned-only and can't carry it, D-03; **the `{n} yours` ownership count is DROPPED by product decision — do not resurrect**), membership-only chip (`_dcChipOf`: trip/week; **a daily look carries NO chip**; suppressed on strips — the artifact masthead carries it), states `empty-past/empty/past/today/planned` (+ calendar-only `void` for out-of-month — now DISTINCT from past-empty, §7) with modifiers `worn`/`packed` (footer status only) + the `pinned` ring kept from the rail. `_DC_THUMB_MODE = 'swatch'` restores the palette whisper if §10.1 reopens. Weekly strip drops "your plan" + `{n} yours`; travel strip drops "2 looks" and gains the `Packed ✓` modifier.
- **Ghost bands fixed (audit D-08, unconditional — not behind the flag)**: `_pdSupersede` — two same-type sources with **overlapping date ranges** are the same plan re-planned → only the freshest survives WHOLESALE (per-date `_pdFreshest` couldn't drop a rescheduled trip's rows on dates the fresh trip no longer covers). Applied in `_pdSlots` (rail), `_mvFresh` (calendar) and `comingUp` (its select now carries `updated_at`). Daily looks exempt (day+evening siblings coexist by design). Band lane assignment now **sorts segments by start column** before the greedy pass (was source-discovery order). Post-fix, >2 bands per column is near-impossible, so the `+N more` overflow is defensive: under the flag it's a button → popover of the hidden bands (`__mvMore`, `#rb-mv-pop`).
- **Calendar rhythm (§11.2)**: under the flag every week row reserves the SAME two band lanes (`padTop` constant), so band-carrying rows no longer sit taller — the "blank hero slot" the audit dismissed was this reservation varying per row. Cells are compact DayCards (min-height 150, no aspect-ratio); `worn` renders on the calendar (the §10.4 proposal, shipped as proposed — flip by not passing the modifier if it reopens).
- **Dead CSS removed (audit D-12)**: the never-emitted `.tvm-day` rules (a fossil from before the strips were unified onto `_rbDayStrip`).
- Rollback: `?daycard=off` per device, or set `_DC_FLAG_KEY` default; the legacy renders are all intact behind the branch points (`cardHtml`, `_wkPaintStrip`, `_tvPaintWeek`, `_mvPaint`, `__mvCell`).

### 2026-07-24 — The Diary Stage 6: the continuous spine (handoff Phase 6 — month view)
Month view lives INSIDE the Lookbook (no `/calendar` route — out of scope §9): a **Grid | Calendar** segmented toggle (`#sn-viewseg`) in the lookbook header; Calendar swaps the tabs/grid for `#sn-cal` (month serif title + ‹ › nav, `__mvNav`) via a `rb-cal-on` class on `#sn-page` (class, not inline styles — `snRenderPage` after `_lbCloudPull` re-sets the grid's inline display underneath). The lookbook always reopens on Grid. All code in the `_rbMonthView` IIFE (after `_rbRail`); CSS `#rb-mv-style`. Verified with a dedicated Playwright harness: 19 checks green covering all seven §6.5 acceptance items, in a timezone-shifted (America/Los_Angeles) context, plus Stage 2 (31) + Stage 3 (57) regression runs.
- **Bands — a trip is ONE event**: `_mvBands` groups `_pdMonth` rows by source (weekly + travel only; **daily looks never band** — decision 7, the cell is their whole expression), extent = the source's row dates, segments computed per Monday-start week row from REAL weekday offsets (never a hardcoded col). First visible segment carries the title ONLY when the band genuinely starts there (`is_start`); every other segment is "↳ {title}, continued" (italic, reduced). A band clipped by the grid boundary still renders its partial segment — the fetch runs grid±7d so the band knows its true start. **Lanes**: up to two per week row (greedy by start column), overlapping weekly+trip stack without collision, row `padding-top` derives from lane count, beyond two lanes a quiet "+N more" indicator. Sage = weekly, mauve = trip (the rail's dot colours as washes). Tap a band → `__snOpenItem(source)` + `month_band_opened {source_type}`.
- **Cells** (`.rb-mc`, aspect 1.25 → 1 on mobile): serif numeral, 2-line-clamped activity line, ≤3-thumb strip (hidden <1000px, onerror-hidden), states per §6.3 — past settled cream fill / today ink ring / future outlined dashed / free dashed + italic "left free" / pinned mauve ring (same accent as rail + intake) / bare (out of month, or in-month with nothing). **A date with two moments shows the daytime line + a compact ☾ marker** — never two stacked lines. A standalone daily look is full cell content, visually equal to a planned day. **Same-type supersession (`_pdFreshest`) applies per date before anything renders** — a re-planned trip never paints twice and a superseded plan's evening can't ghost a ☾.
- **One spine (§6.4)**: `window._rbOpenPlannedDay(m)` is THE opener — `__rbRailOpen`, `__mvCell` and the deep link all route through it (same `__snOpenItem` + select-day landing), harness-asserted: cell tap and rail tap land on the identical day console. **`?d=YYYY-MM-DD` deep link** on `/dashboard` resolves the date's precedence winner via `_pdRail(d,d)` (daily > travel > weekly) and opens its console from boot.
- NOT built (spec'd but deliberately deferred): the full shared card component with a density prop across rail/cell/artifact (the three renders share data + opener but not one template yet); month rows render only the current fetch — no infinite scroll, ‹ › nav only.

### 2026-07-24 — The Diary Stage 5: the capsule constraint + weekly evening looks (handoff Phase 4 + Phase 3 remainder)
The one place travel is DELIBERATELY different, now enforced: **on a trip you dress from a case, not a wardrobe** — plus the weekly console's day/evening switcher (the §3.3 remainder). Verified in the Stage 3 harness (52 checks green incl. the same-day evening-rules additions below) + Stage 2 rerun (30).
- **Trip day console is capsule-scoped**: `__tvFlip` cycles the slot through the trip's OTHER capsule members in the category (`_tvCapsulePool`) by **re-pointing this day's formula `item_index`** (`_tvRepointDay`) — day-scoped, membership untouched, other days untouched (the pre-Phase-4 flick offered owned wardrobe pieces and applied trip-wide). `__tvDaySwap` opens the shared modal with `cfg.pool` (capsule members, "From your case") and `cfg.adopt` — the **adoption section** ("From the rest of your wardrobe", one-line inline confirm: "Picking one adds it to your case and restyles this day only") → `__tvAdoptApply` appends the piece to membership (wardrobe_match, tier inherited), re-points the current day, fires `capsule_adopted {from:'console'}`. The rack hint (`cfg.rackHintHtml`, new `_rbConsole` slot) names MEMBERSHIP, not packing. Travel's Build-2 alternates prefetch/fetch retired (capsule candidates need no AI alternates — tokens saved).
- **Capsule-level swap (Edit tab) = membership change**: `__tvSwap`/`__tvSwapApply` keep the identity-swap-everywhere behaviour, then report the blast radius — `#tv-stale-bar` ("{piece} now packs — N days wear this slot · ↻ Restyle those days") → `__tvRestyleAllDays` = ONE batched `/api/travel/outfits` call (`__tvOutfitsGo` now accepts a ready-made plan array carrying the days' `user_activity`), firing `capsule_swap_restyle {days_affected}`. Packed toggles drive NO generation (decision 4, harness-asserted).
- **Weekly evenings get their own look, lazily**: when `wkData.evenings` covers the active day the weekly console shows a **Day/Evening switcher** (`__wkSetSlot`, `_wkActiveSlot` reset on render + day change). The evening's look generates on first open via the EXISTING `/api/weekly/day` (`__wkDressEvening` — activity framed as "the EVENING of this day only"; no schema change) into `d.evening_look {occasion, note, items}`, riding the blob. **Every console mutation targets the selected moment only** — flick/anchor/swap/remove/add/save all resolve their item array through `_wkConItems()`. `_pdRowsWk` evening rows enrich from `evening_look` (headline/thumbs/item_ids) so the rail's evening line gets real content.
- **Evening rules, consistent across all three tracks (same-day fix — Annie's bug report)**: *by default the evening is LEFT FREE; every dressed day carries a Day/Evening switcher; an evening ask is an ADDITION, never a restyle of the day.* (1) **Server**: the `/api/travel`, `/api/travel/outfits` and `/api/travel/day` prompts now add an Evening slot ONLY when the day's plan names an evening moment (was "exactly 2 slots" — the itinerary block's "both slots answer to it" softened to match); the client renders 1-slot days cleanly. (2) **Consoles**: the weekly switcher renders on EVERY dressed day (inline `segBtn` segmented pair, "Evening · free" until dressed — no longer gated on `wkData.evenings` coverage); travel's occ control is the fixed `['Day', 'Evening · free']` pair — `_tvLookState` returns `{s: eveS, eveFree: !eveS}` for `_tvActiveOcc === 1` instead of clamping back to Day. Both evening-empty states are an invitation card with an inline input (`#wk-eve-act` / `#tv-eve-act`) + "✨ Dress the evening →". (3) **The addition verbs**: `__wkDressEvening(activity)` creates/updates the `wkData.evenings` moment then generates with a the-day-is-NOT-to-be-changed brief; NEW `__tvDressEvening(activity)` does the trip equivalent via `/api/travel/day` and takes ONLY the Evening slot from the response — the existing Day slot is never replaced (a returned `new_item` still joins the capsule). Both fire `day_planned`. (4) **The scoped prompt routes evening asks as additions** (`_ikRestyleDay` → `_ikEveningIntent` — explicit framings only: "add an evening look" / "evening look|outfit|plan" / "for|in the evening" / "tonight" / "date night" / "after dark"; bare "dinner"/"drinks" still restyle the day): weekly/travel winners open the artifact at the day and call the DressEvening verb; a daily/empty date submits `__dlSubmit(text, {anchorDate, slot: 'evening'})` — `dlData.slot` stamps and `_pdRowsDl` emits an `evening` row, so the evening daily look COEXISTS with the day's look at the same date (restyles keep the saved slot). (5) `_wkPatchSaved` now persists `wkData.evenings` alongside days (a prompt-created evening moment used to vanish on reopen). (6) **The daily console carries the pair too** (Annie's second report, same day): a date's day + evening are two SEPARATE saved daily looks — the switcher (`__dlSetSlot`, rendered whenever `dlData.anchor_date` exists) navigates between siblings (`_dlSibling` — newest daily look at the same anchor date wearing the other slot; exclusion comes from `opts.savedId`, never `_dlActiveSaveId`, which still points at the previous look mid-render), the empty side renders the invitation in place of the console (`#dl-eve-act` + `__dlDressSlot` → `__dlSubmit({anchorDate, slot})` — an addition, never a restyle), and the tab NEVER leaves the daily context — a first cut bounced to the artifact of another track owning the date's moment (a trip's dinner), which read as being dumped into the travel edit (Annie's report, same day); the rail stays the cross-track surface, and a daily evening she creates simply outranks the trip's at read time (daily > travel > weekly). Console head/rack labels read "{weekday} evening" on the evening look. (7) **Ghost moments are dropped at read time** (`_pdFreshest`, applied in `_pdSlots` + the daily tab's cross-track lookup): re-planning the same dates coexists in `planned_days` by design, but a superseded plan of the SAME type is now dropped WHOLESALE per date — without this an old trip's evening row (no contender in the fresh trip, which left its evening free) won the evening slot and painted a rail moment that was empty on click-through. Cross-type complements still resolve per slot. Harness: Stage 3 57 checks (daily-pair + evening-ask sections), Stage 2 31 (ghost-supersession check).
- Config consumption completed: `artifact.hasCapsuleTab`/`hasPackProgress` now gate the travel tabs + masthead pack progress (weekly's shell would simply omit them). The full single-artifact-shell merge remains the one undelivered Phase 3 item — two render functions, config-fed strings, documented trade-off (regression surface vs. hygiene).

### 2026-07-23 — The Diary Stage 4: track config (handoff Phase 3, first tranche)
A week and a trip are the same object with different constraints — **`_RB_TRACKS`** (`dashboard-personalize.js`, right before the `_ik*` section; also `window._RB_TRACKS`) is the one config object with three consumers, resolved via `_rbTrackCfg(kind)` (unknown kinds fall back to weekly). Verified inside the Stage 3 harness: 42 checks green incl. reference-string assertions on both artifacts (zero visual drift) and a throwaway fourth-track fixture.
- **Shape**: per track (`weekly`/`travel`/`daily`) — `engine` (which generator commit routes to), `intake` (`defaultDays`/`maxDays`/`requiresDates`, `rackLabel`, `quickAdd` chips, `primaryLabel(st)`, `maxDaysError`), `artifact` (`eyebrow` — strings deliberately verbatim from the pre-refactor renders: "Your week, planned"/"The travel edit"/"Your daily look" — `hasCapsuleTab`, `hasPackProgress`), `console` (`rackScope: wardrobe|capsule`, `addVerb`, `rackHint` reserved). `rackScope: 'capsule'` is declared but NOT yet enforced — that's Phase 4's one deliberate structural difference (Stage 5); travel flick-through still offers owned wardrobe pieces until then.
- **Consumers wired**: the intake's per-kind ternaries (chips, range caps + errors, rack label, commit CTA, date-requirement, generic date-clamp) all read config; `_ikCommit` routes by `engine` so a fourth track rides an existing generator; the three `_rbConsole` adapters take `addChipLabel` from `console.addVerb`; the three artifact masthead eyebrows read `artifact.eyebrow`.
- **Fourth-track proof** (acceptance §3.5.3): the harness injects `_RB_TRACKS.retreat` (3-day default, own chips/labels, weekly engine) and the intake renders + commits through `/api/weekly` with no component changes.
- NOT yet unified (honest scope — the remaining Phase 3 work, sitting behind this because its regression surface is the working artifacts): the `__wkRenderResult`/`__tvRenderResult` shells are still two functions (config feeds their strings but the trees are separate), `hasCapsuleTab`/`hasPackProgress` are declared-not-consumed, and the weekly console has no day/evening switcher (weekly evenings still fold into the day's plan line).

### 2026-07-23 — The Diary Stage 3: the single prompt + unfurl intake (handoff Phases 1–2, FLAGGED)
The prompt becomes the one field that generates, scopes and restyles — the `diary_prompt_intake` flag: **default ON for everyone since 2026-07-24 (Stage 6 merge day); `?diary=off` is the per-device kill switch (persisted in localStorage `rb_diary_prompt`), `?diary=on` clears it.** (The stage shipped default-OFF/opt-in; Annie flipped it once Stages 1–6 were live-tested.) Flag off = the concierge behaves exactly as before (verified — the Stage 3 harness runs its parity test through the kill switch, and Stage 2 pins `rb_diary_prompt='off'` since it asserts the pre-diary rail tap semantics). All client code lives in the `_ik*` section of `dashboard-personalize.js` (before `_rbOnboardHandoff`); 31 harness checks green incl. flag-off parity, plus a 29-check Stage 2 regression run.
- **`POST /api/intent`** (`server.js`, INTENT_SCHEMA): gemini-2.5-flash, temp 0, thinking 0, 500 tokens, 5s server race. Returns `{intent: daily|weekly|travel|unclear, destination, date_start/end, day_intents[], confidence}`. Hard rules in-prompt: NEVER invent a destination or date ("somewhere warm" → destination null); relative dates resolve against the CLIENT's local calendar date (`clientDate` in the POST body — never server now()); confidence <0.6 → the app asks instead of acting. Captured in generation_log via the wrapped client. Client aborts at 4s.
- **Scope chip** (`#rb-scopechip` in `.cb-input`, created on demand by `_ikChipPaint`): `_ikScope {kind: none|plan|day}`. A weekly plan covering today AUTO-scopes (`_ikComputePlanScope` — label is the plan's real title, fallback "Week of {date}", never a placeholder); **trips never auto-scope** (decision 3 — a week is ambient, a trip is an occasion). Day scope = `.sel` (mauve, destructive-adjacent). Esc or chip-tap from a day returns to the PLAN scope, never straight to none. Rail integration: under the flag a populated card's BODY tap scopes the date (`__rbRailTap`), the card CTA opens (`__rbRailOpenCta`); flag off keeps tap=open.
- **Routing (`_ikSubmit`, intercepts `_cbSubmit` under the flag)**: photo → style track (P0 rule, classifier never sees it); day scope → `_ikRestyleDay` (winning moment from the planned_days cache: daily/empty → `__dlSubmit {anchorDate}`; weekly/travel → opens the artifact AT that day and drives the existing single-day modal + `/api/weekly/day`|`/api/travel/day` with her words as the activity); plan scope + no date/place signal → `_ikRestylePlan` (full `/api/weekly` re-gen from the saved blob's dayPlan/week_iso — a NEW row that coexists and wins at read time); else → classifier → confident daily submits directly, weekly/travel open the unfurl, unclear/failure → clarify state with the prompt preserved (never a dead end). Re-entry guarded (the bundle's send pipeline can double-fire).
- **The unfurl** (`#rb-intake`, attached — `.concierge-box.rb-attached` drops its bottom radius so the panel continues the field): reading state (skeleton, min 400ms) → ready; failure → clarify. **Crumbs**: where (editable; empty = legitimate invitation "add a place"), when (native date inputs; re-deriving preserves activity/pin/evening BY DATE; >21 days refused inline; end<start swaps), provenance note ("read from your prompt" / "edited by you" / "our suggestion"). **Build-around**: category chips from HER wardrobe only, multi-select rack → `anchorItemIds` on `/api/weekly` (BUILD-AROUND prompt block: woven where they serve, never forced); empty wardrobe → section absent. **Day list (sparse-primary)**: pin control (SVG pin, mauve wash `.ik-row.pinned` — pinned = SHE fixed it; classifier `day_intents` arrive pre-filled AND pinned, provenance "your prompt"), moment cell → tray (input + track-specific quick-add chips + Leave free), **+ Add an evening** (only when the row has one moment — never fourteen rows of placeholders). **Commit**: weekly → `_wkGenerate(…, {anchorItemIds, pinnedDays, evenings})` — evening activity folds into that day's authoritative plan line ("Office — evening: date night", one look reads the whole day until Phase 3 parity) AND lands as its own `evening` index row via `wkData.evenings`; `wkData.pinned_days` → rows `pinned=true` (`_pdRowsWk` extended). Travel → **generates DIRECTLY from the unfurl** (bug-fix same day — Annie: "everything should now happen in prompt, not modal"): the crumbs are the brief, the day list is the day planner, the rack is the shortlist (travel heading "What's tempting you?"), commit assembles `_tvBrief` + calls `_tvGenerate(plan)` — the old `#tv-brief-modal`/`#tv-plan-modal` never appear on this path (they survive for the concierge card / wardrobe pack-a-trip entries, and `__tvOpen` kept its dest/dateFrom/dateTo/plan prefill opts). The modal's guardrails moved inline: destination + dates required, shortlist floor `_TV_MIN_ANCHORS` (waived when the wardrobe has <2 pieces), trips cap at 10 days (the engine ceiling — never show rows the server would silently cut). Cancel/abandon writes NOTHING (verified).
- **Suggestion pills** (`#rb-sugg`, max 3, state-derived — NEVER a place/date the user hasn't given): unscoped → add-pieces (<15), plan-my-week (only when no weekly rows cover the coming week; city name only from `__rbCtx`), finish-deferred-trip (real title), dress-today (only when today has no rows); day scope → three tone modifiers (never dated/placed); plan scope → two plan-wide modifiers + dress-today. Telemetry `source: 'pill'` vs `'typed'` — the honest read on whether the field works.
- **Capture**: `prompt_submitted {intent, scope, source, latency_ms, ok}` on every submit incl. failures; `intake_opened/dates_edited/day_edited/day_pinned/evening_added/anchor_selected/committed/abandoned` per the §7.1 vocabulary.
- Deviations flagged for Phase 3: confident `daily` skips the intake (momentum > ceremony — the intake's single-day mode arrives with parity); weekly evening moments get no separate look yet (folded into the day's plan line).
- Same-day polish: the daily generation overlay title is the hero tagline "One prompt. Dressed for anything." on both the `/api/daily` and legacy dress-me overlays (was "Dressing you for today…" — wrong on any scoped day); the anchored-restyle overlay keeps "Restyling around your anchors…". The Daily console headers + lookbook subtitle derive their weekday from `dlData.anchor_date`, never render-time now().

### 2026-07-23 — The Diary Stage 2: the home calendar rail (handoff Phase 5)
First read surface over `planned_days` — seven cards across the top of home (yesterday, today, next five), mounted directly under the concierge prompt (`#rb-rail`, CSS `#rb-rail-style`, built by the `_rbRail` IIFE before `_rbOnboardHandoff`). Verified with a Playwright harness: 25 checks green at 1280px + 390px. **DATE-driven, one card per date, never one per moment** — a date holding two moments shows the daytime activity as the headline and the evening as a quiet second line. **"Palette whisper" card anatomy (Annie's design 1c, same day — supersedes the earlier warm-frame pass)**: text-led calendar cells with NO imagery. Stack: eyebrow "TODAY · THU 23" (relative label + short date, month only when it differs), serif activity ONCE (never repeated, never a truncated parent-title description), quiet evening line, a **dot-coded source tag** (`tagHtml`/`srcDot`: cream dot = Daily look, sage = week "WEEK OF 22 JUL" from `week_iso[0]`, mauve = trip "IBIZA TRIP" — the free day carries the tag of the plan it belongs to), then **up to three colour swatches** (`dayColors`: the day's `item_ids` → wardrobe `item_dna.display.primary_color_hex`, falling back to the `_ALL_SWATCHES` name map; no resolvable colours → no row, never placeholder dots), CTA. **Geometry is LOCKED**: 7 equal grid columns — today the SAME width (color only, per the 1c mock; the earlier wider-today grid is gone), body `min-height:172px`, title zone reserves TWO lines (`min-height:2.3em`), row heights equalise via grid stretch; mobile all cards 158px. The rail is the most compact module on home — **height ladder**: week-ahead compact < wardrobe tracker < concierge medium (`.svc-img` 16:9) < lookbook tall hero (`.rb-sn-img` 3:4). Judge changes on the FULL homepage with every module mounted, never the rail alone. Cards resolve through `_pdRail` (precedence pre-applied); repaints on boot (lazy-uid poll), after `_lbCloudPull`, ~700ms after every `_pdSync` write, and on `visibilitychange` (exposed as `window._rbRailPaint`).
- **States**: `past` (dimmed cream body, **"Wore it?"** CTA → "Worn ✓"), `today` (image over inverted-ink stack, wider desktop column — grid `1fr 1.5fr ×5`), `planned` (Open →), `free` (dashed + diagonal hatch, italic "Left free", **"Dress it →"** — a free day has nothing to open, so its tap SCOPES like an empty card), `pinned` (mauve ring — render support only, no write path until the intake), `empty-today` (ink dashed, "Dress today →"), `empty-future` (dashed invite), `empty-past` (ghost, inert). Cold start = six invitations, never a blank strip. Below 1000px the grid becomes a scroll-snap strip (166px cards, today 206px) that **centres today on mount**.
- **Tap semantics (flag-dependent since Stage 3)**: FLAG OFF — a populated card's tap OPENS the day (`__rbRailTap` → `__rbRailOpen`: `__snOpenItem` + `__wkSelectDay`/`__tvSetTab('outfits')`+`__tvSelectDay` land on the tapped day, `day_opened_from_rail` fires); an EMPTY card aims the prompt at the date (`__rbRailScope` arms `_cbAnchorDate` + `_cbIntent='dress-me'`, injects "An outfit for {Friday 25 Jul} — [the plan]"; `_cbResolve`'s dress-me branch passes the armed date as `__dlSubmit(prompt, {anchorDate})`, cleared by `_cbReset`). FLAG ON — EVERY card's body tap scopes the date via the chip (`window._ikScopeDay`), and opening lives on the card CTA (`__rbRailOpenCta`). Both paths fire `rail_day_scoped {state, offset_from_today}`; a scoped daily look lands on the tapped calendar date, not today, and its console/subtitle read the ANCHOR date's weekday (fixed 2026-07-23 — render-time now() said Thursday on a Friday look).
- **Mark worn** (`__rbRailWear`, past cards): stamps `worn: true` **blob-first** into the day object (`wkData.days[i]`/`tvData.days[i]`/`dlData`) so a later resync can't revert it (row builders emit `status: 'worn'` from the stamp), `_pdSyncSaved` re-syncs, `times_worn` +1 on every owned piece in the day's `item_ids` (the write path that makes cost-per-wear real), card settles to "Worn ✓" without reload.
- **"Coming up" strip** (`.rb-upnext` in `#rb-rail-up`): the next plan starting AFTER today+5 — one cheap `planned_days` query; leads with the parent's editorial hero (the lookbook card `img` — for a trip that's the destination hero shot), real title, date range + **derived counts** (trip: capsule pieces + slot count as looks; week: dressed days — never stored), right-aligned "Open the trip →"/"Open the week →" CTA. Renders only when one exists. The mock's "From your lookbook" strip is deliberately NOT duplicated — the dashboard's existing Lookbook row already serves it.
- Frames hide themselves on image error (`onerror` — a dead Cloudinary URL must not paint a broken-image glyph). Copy strings are provisional (`// COPY: needs sign-off`). Degradation: migration missing → seven dated empty cards + the usual one-time warn.

### 2026-07-23 — The Diary Stage 1: `planned_days` index (migration 12)
First stage of the Diary/calendar architecture (22 Jul `planned_days` brief + 23 Jul Diary handoff rev B, §0.2 amendments folded in — the slot/pinned/anchor_item_ids columns ship in migration 12 itself since no write path predates it). **Data layer + write paths only; nothing user-visible consumes it yet** (the home rail is Stage 2). Verified with a Playwright harness (Supabase shim + REST stubs): 43 checks green at 1280px + 390px incl. precedence, degradation and a 23:30-US-timezone date check.
- **`supabase/planned_days_migration.sql` (migration 12, NOT yet run on Robes_p0)**: `planned_days` — an INDEX over the wkData/tvData/dlData blobs, never a replacement (blobs stay authoritative for rendering). **A moment is a row**: unique on `(user_id, source_id, day_index, slot)`, `slot in ('day','evening')` check-capped at two (a third slot is a product decision with a migration attached), `pinned` (a fixed moment she placed — a generation constraint, not "more looks"), `day_date` is `date` (local-calendar concept — client sends local ISO, never timestamptz), `source_id` is text with NO FK (lookbook PK is `(user_id,id)`, rows can be local-only), denormalised `status/activity/headline/thumb_urls/item_ids`, reserved `anchor_item_ids` (Phase 2 build-around) + `ootd_photo_url`/`worn_at` (OOTD capture phase, unwritten). Own-all + admin-read RLS.
- **Client module** (`dashboard-personalize.js`, after `_lbCloudPull`): `_pdSync(sourceType, sourceId, blob)` — per-source 600ms-debounced upsert (`on_conflict` + `Prefer: resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal`) followed by two sweep DELETEs (`day_index=gte.N` for shrunk ranges, `slot=eq.evening&day_index=not.in.(…)` for vanished evenings — a ghost evening on the rail is the same bug class as orphans on delete). `_pdSyncSaved(id)` re-derives from the lookbook cache — the one call every patch path shares. Row builders `_pdRowsWk/_pdRowsTv/_pdRowsDl`: weekly = 1 row/day slot `day`; travel = 1 row per MOMENT (Day+Evening slots per dressed day, 1 free row for rest days, deferred trips band as bare `planned` rows with her typed `trip_day_plan` as activity); daily = 1 row at `anchor_date`. Thumbs prefer truthful wardrobe photos, then generated stills, never a still on a flicked piece (`_dlAltered` gate). **Degradation**: table missing (PGRST205/42P01) → warn once, `_pdDown` stops further calls for the session, app behaves exactly as before.
- **Date emission (brief §5)**: `_wkWeekDays` now emits `iso` alongside its display strings; `__wkPlanGo` → `_wkGenerate(…, weekIso)` stamps `week_iso[]` into the render data/save (positional against `days` — the server generates one entry per calendar day). Travel already had `dateFrom` (server echoes it); dates derive `dateFrom + day_index` via UTC arithmetic. Daily: `__dlSubmit` accepts `opts.anchorDate`, defaults to the LOCAL today (`_pdLocalISO`, never `toISOString()` — the 23:00-US off-by-one), restyles keep the saved look's original `anchor_date`; stored as `dlData.anchor_date`.
- **Write-path wiring**: fresh saves (dl/wk/tv snAdd branches) + every mutation funnel — `_dlPatchSaved`/`_wkPatchSaved`/`_tvPatchSaved`, the `__dlSubmit` savedId restyle branch, and the three `_xxPersistImages` (stills landing late refresh `thumb_urls`). `_lbCloudDelete` also fires `_pdDeleteSource` (no FK cascade exists — orphaned index rows paint ghost days). `__dlWear` stamps `dlData.worn` → row `status: 'worn'` (blob-first so a later resync can't revert it; `worn_at` stays reserved). Old saves without `week_iso`/`anchor_date` emit nothing client-side — the backfill covers them. Rename needs NO index patch: parent titles resolve at read time.
- **Read helpers** (unconsumed until Stage 2): `window._pdRail(fromISO, toISO, cb?)` — 7 DATE-driven slots whether or not rows exist, cb fires from localStorage cache then fresh; precedence resolved per `(date, slot)`: `daily > travel > weekly` (auto-planned tier reserved), ties to latest `updated_at` — a trip's evening contends with a week's evening, never displaces its daytime. `window._pdMonth(from, to)` — ALL rows unresolved + a `sources` map (band extents need the losing rows). Parent title/type resolves from the local lookbook cache (`_pdParent`), never a per-card query.
- **Backfill (run once AFTER migration 12), two equivalent forms**: `supabase/planned_days_backfill.sql` — paste-and-run in the Supabase SQL editor, no service key needed (this is the form actually used on Robes_p0) — or `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=… node scripts/backfill_planned_days.mjs [--dry-run]` from a terminal. Both are re-runnable (upserts): weekly/travel blobs only, **daily looks skipped entirely** (decision log #2, no guessing at `created_at::date`); old weekly saves get dates parsed from "Monday · 14 Jul" labels with nearest-year-to-`created_at`; unparseable sources skipped and surfaced (SQL query 5 lists them).
- **Capture**: `day_planned {source_type, day_index}` fires on single-day plan edits (`_wkApplyDay` with activity, `_tvApplyDayResult` with user activity); `day_opened_from_rail` vocabulary ships with the rail.
- **Decided precedence answers brief §13 Q3**: re-planning a week over the same dates COEXISTS in the table and resolves at read time (tier, then latest `updated_at`) — write-time exclusion loses data.

### 2026-07-22 — Weekly imagery (closing the generation_log zero-row gap)
The image-row distribution in `generation_log` (Daily 29 / Travel 8 / Weekly 0) evidenced two things. (1) **Weekly made no image-generation calls at all** — the monogram tile on unowned pieces was the designed behaviour, not a failure rate. Fixed: `/api/weekly` now runs the same background still-life pipeline as Travel. (2) **Travel's 8 rows against a 15-piece capsule is the deliberate `frames < 8` cap** (`server.js`, hero shot + ≤7 stills, sized to the client's 5-minute polling ceiling) — NOT rate-limit timeouts; the imageless capsule cards are unowned pieces past the cap, working as designed. Travel is unchanged. Weekly specifics:
- **Server**: after coverage validation, one still-life per **distinct unowned** piece — deduped by lowercased name across the week, because the routing discipline re-wears pieces across days, so every appearance shares one frame; owned pieces are skipped (wardrobe photos are truthful and free). Capped at 8 stills (same polling-ceiling maths as Travel). Response gains `jobId` + `imageCount` (omitted entirely when every piece is owned — zero frames means no job). Background loop mirrors Travel exactly: 3s stagger, 50s race per frame, Cloudinary-hosted URLs only, `logAI` feature `'weekly'` stage `'image'` — and because it rides the wrapped `ai.models.generateContent`, weekly image rows now land in `generation_log` automatically.
- **Client** (`dashboard-personalize.js`): `_wkPollImages`/`_wkSetImage`/`_wkSettlePlaceholder`/`_wkPersistImages` — the Travel poller pattern verbatim (`data-wkimg="i"` wraps, `.wk-img-ph` placeholders, onerror → monogram settle, `image_fallback` track with `surface: 'weekly-plan'`, 300s ceiling, persists into `wkData.generatedImages` + backfills the lookbook card `img` when the save had no owned photo). `wkFrame` in `_wkPaintConsole` mirrors `_tvFrame` (wardrobe photo → generated still → monogram; a flicked piece never wears the original's still via `_dlAltered`; reopened saves with a missing frame settle straight to monogram since `jobId` is stripped on save). Day-strip thumbs include stills. `__wkRenderResult` calls `_wkStopPolling()` up front so a stale poller can't patch an earlier plan's frames into new data. `_wkApplyDay` re-attaches the week's existing stills by name onto a restyled day's fresh items (`/api/weekly/day` itself still generates no imagery, matching `/api/travel/day`). `publicSharePayload`'s weekly branch includes stills.
- **Travel hero resurfaced (same day)**: the hero shot had been generated-but-invisible in-app since the 2026-07-10 day-console "The mood" tile removal, surviving only as the lookbook thumbnail and the `/board` share lead/OG image (`addImg(row.img)` runs first in `publicSharePayload`). It now renders once at TRIP level — a 200px "The mood · {destination}" tile beside the Edit pane's stylist summary (flex row, wraps below the text on narrow screens, `tv-noprint`). The tile only mounts when the frame exists or a live job can still deliver it (`data-tvimg="0"` so `_tvSetImage` patches it; old saves with no stored hero show no tile); a failed hero settles to the destination-initial monogram (`_tvSettlePlaceholder` special-cases i=0 — no capsule item owns frame 0).
Tranche 2's Build 1 originally proposed one acquisition verb (`Save`) everywhere; the Build 1 amendment (Part A, same day) corrected this after re-reading Travel's `Pack`/`Add` as a matched trip-scoped pair (Keep / Worth adding), not the same slot as the new verb. Implemented per the amendment, not the original brief:
- **Travel unowned rack piece** (day console `thirdHtml` + the capsule card, both routed through `__tvAddOwn`): unchanged label/behaviour (`+ Add`, sets `it.added = true`, toast "added to the pack", trip-scoped) — **plus** a new silent, idempotent write to `wishlist_items` via `_wlSaveFromItem(it, {silent:true})`, fired async after the existing render/toast so it never delays or narrates the trip-scoped action. No second confirmation.
- **Daily/Weekly unowned rack piece**: new third rack action, `Save` (`.rbc-act.save`, filled ink — same weight as Travel's `+ Add`), wired to `__dlSaveWishlist`/`__wkSaveWishlist` → `_wlSaveFromItem(it)` → `wishlist_items` (`source_type: 'robes'`, "Robes suggests" chip), toast **"Saved to your wishlist."** Owned pieces get no third action on these two surfaces (no case to add to). Once saved, the button becomes an inert `<span class="rbc-act done">✓ Saved</span>` — same idempotency the shared helper also guards server-side-of-state (a stray second click no-ops on the `it.wishlisted` flag rather than double-inserting).
- **`_wlSaveFromItem(it, opts)`** (new shared helper, lives by `_WL_SRC`): the single write path for all three surfaces — reuses the `wishlist_items` table/pipeline rather than reopening the manual-capture modal (`__wlOpenAdd` stays the screenshot/photo flow only). `it.wishlisted` persists on the item object exactly like `packed`/`added`/`anchored` already do (no schema change — it rides inside each surface's saved JSON blob via `_dlPatchSaved`/`_wkPatchSaved`/`_tvPatchSaved`).
- **Tile chip label is surface-scoped**, not hard-coded: `_rbcTile` reads `cfg.addChipLabel` (falls back to `'Add'`); all three `_rbConsole` call sites set it explicitly — Travel `'Add'`, Daily/Weekly `'Save'`.
- Neither verb touches `wardrobe_items` — ownership capture is still only the swap modal's Snap-mine path and the pack-toggle own-modal (`__tvQuickOwn`/`__tvOwnSnap`), unchanged.

### 2026-07-22 — Build 3: look-contextual row notes + copy rules (Tranche 2)
Closes the F7/F20 copy defects the Look/Rack audit found — the row/panel notes were either unused (`description`) or generic stylist-copy tells (`X yet Y`, benefit justification). `server.js` now carries four shared prompt-rule constants, interpolated into every stylist system prompt rather than repeated per-endpoint: `ROW_NOTE_RULE` (per-garment, 6–12 words, verb-led participle, never repeats the item name), `PANEL_NOTE_RULE` (per-look, 30 words, describes balance/register, garment TYPE only), `WEEK_SUMMARY_RULE` (40 words, no specific garments), `BANNED_CONSTRUCTIONS_RULE` (the audited tells — "X yet Y", "perfect for"/"ensuring"/"effortlessly"/"elevated"/"versatile", virtue framing, quotation marks — appended once per prompt, covers every field in that call).
- **New field `how`** on `DAILY_SCHEMA` and `WEEKLY_SCHEMA` item properties (and inherited into `WEEKLY_DAY_SCHEMA`, which reuses `WEEKLY_SCHEMA.properties.days.items.properties.items`) — the row note, required in place of `description` (which stays in the schema, now optional, purely an internal cue for the still-life image prompt — never rendered). Travel's existing per-formula `note` field is aligned to the same `ROW_NOTE_RULE` text rather than given a new field (`server.js` pillar §3 in `travelSystem`, plus `TRAVEL_DAY_SCHEMA`/`TRAVEL_OUTFITS_SCHEMA`'s rule #2) — it already had the right shape, just no length/register discipline.
- **Panel note**: Daily's `stylist_summary` field rule is replaced wholesale (was "2–3 sentences… name the garments… golden-ratio thinking", no cap) — this is the F7 close Tranche 1 §4.3 set up (removed the framework-vocabulary instruction) but didn't finish. Weekly's per-day `note` (rendered as the console's mood quote, `quoteHtml: d.note`) is now explicitly the day's panel note; the week-level `stylist_summary` and `WEEKLY_DAY_SCHEMA`'s refreshed `stylist_summary` both point at `WEEK_SUMMARY_RULE` instead of a bespoke 2–3-sentence instruction.
- Client (`dashboard-personalize.js`): Daily's and Weekly's `_rbConsole` conItems both gained `noteHtml: it.how ? '<div class="rbc-hownote">'+esc(it.how)+'</div>' : ''` — the same `.rbc-hownote` class Travel already used for `x.f.note`. **Flicking/swapping a piece clears or replaces its row note** (`_dlApplyOption` now sets `it.how = o.how || ''`) — a row note describes a SPECIFIC piece, so it can't survive a flick to a different one without going stale; only the original served piece (`it.orig.how`, stashed the first time `_dlOptions` runs) and an on-demand alternate that carries its own `how` (Build 2) keep one, everything else renders no row note rather than a mismatched line.
- **F20 occasion casing**: removed the ALL CAPS prompt instructions for `occasion_label` (Daily) and `week_label` (Weekly) — both now sentence case at generation. Removed the `text-transform:uppercase` CSS that was forcing visual caps regardless (`.dlm-kws .kw`, the weekly `week_label` pill's inline style) and normalise both to sentence case at render (`cap1(...toLowerCase())` for occasion_label; an inline equivalent for week_label) — belt-and-braces against any pre-existing saved lookbook rows that still carry the old ALL CAPS strings. `trip_label` (Travel) and the moodboard's `aesthetic_tags` keep their ALL CAPS treatment — out of scope, not named in the audit.
- Server-side defence in depth (not a substitute for the prompt rule): `how` is truncated to 160 chars wherever an item is normalised (`weeklyNormaliseItem`, the Daily per-item loop) — mirrors the existing pattern for `note`/other fields.

### 2026-07-22 — Build 2: on-demand AI alternates for Weekly + Travel (Tranche 2)
Weekly and Travel's flick-through carousel (`_dlOptions`) only ever offered the served original plus owned same-category pieces — no AI-suggested alternates, unlike Daily (whose `DAILY_SCHEMA` generates 2 per item upfront). Generating alternates upfront for Weekly (up to 84 items across a 14-day plan) would be a large schema/token cost for options mostly never viewed, so this is fetched lazily, one piece at a time.
- **`POST /api/alternates`** (new, `server.js`): takes `{item: {name, category, brand}, context: [other piece names in the look], styleDna, styleIcons}`, returns `{alternates: [{name, brand, retailer_hint, price_point, how}, …]}` (exactly 2, same shape as Daily's existing `alternates` plus the new `how` row note from Build 3). `gemini-2.5-flash`, thinking off, 500 tokens — deliberately the cheapest model already validated on this API (see the Gemini model chain note in Common gotchas), not a frontier model, per the brief. Rides the same `ai.models.generateContent` wrapper as every other endpoint, so it's automatically captured in `generation_log` with zero extra wiring.
- **Ordering guard (mandatory, not conditional — the generation_log gate was dropped, see the addendum below)**: `_dlOptions(it, opts)` gained `opts.aiFirst` — Daily keeps its established order (original, then AI alternates, then owned — alternates were always generated upfront ahead of owned); Weekly and Travel now pass `{aiFirst: false}` at both their `_rbConsole` conItems mapping and their `__wkFlip`/`__tvFlip` handlers, so owned wardrobe matches always list before any on-demand AI alternate. This is what stops Build 2 from quietly making new-to-buy easier to reach on the surface (Weekly) already suspected of over-weighting it.
- **Fetch + cache** (`dashboard-personalize.js`, by `_dlApplyOption`): `_rbFetchAlternates(it, surface, otherNames)` — a session-lifetime `Map` keyed on `surface|category|name` de-dupes in-flight and completed calls, so a second Swap on the same-named piece never re-fetches (`_rbAltKey`). `_rbPrefetchAlternates(items, surface, repaintFn)` fires the fetch for every not-yet-fetched piece in the **visible day/slot only** (never the whole week or trip) right after that day/slot paints (`_wkPaintConsole`/`_tvPaintConsole` each call it on their own last line — naturally covers every render path: initial mount, `__wkSelectDay`/`__tvSelectDay`/`__tvSetOcc`, restyle, anchor, flick, remove), then repaints once if anything genuinely new landed (a no-op if everything was already cached, so it can't loop). **Swap is still the explicit named trigger** (`__wkSwap`/`__tvSwap` each fire `_rbFetchAlternates` directly when the modal opens) — usually redundant with the prefetch, but the fallback if the background fetch hadn't resolved yet or failed transiently.
- **Accepted cost**: a beat of latency on the first flick where Daily is instant — the visible-day/slot prefetch covers most of it, per the brief. Not solved by moving generation upfront (that's the token-cost problem this build exists to avoid).

### 2026-07-22 — Build 2 addendum: instrumentation (Tranche 2)
The pre-ship `generation_log` owned-vs-total query (above) turned out to be unanswerable — at the volume in the table (five Daily generations, one Weekly, one Travel) and with `detail` carrying no ownership fields, there was nothing to query. Two separate problems, both fixed:
- **The gate is removed, not conditional.** The ordering guard is mandatory on every surface regardless of what any future query shows — it was already correct under every branch of the original decision table; the query was only ever going to set urgency, never permission.
- **`detail` now carries `owned_count`/`item_count`.** `logAI(...)` (the console-only structured logger) already computed these on all three surfaces, but never reached the queryable table — only the auto-wrapped `ai.models.generateContent` writes to `generation_log`, and its `detail` only ever carried `{input_images, gen_id, user_prompt}`. Each of `/api/daily` (top of the file), `/api/weekly`, `/api/weekly/day` and `/api/travel`'s text-generation success path now fires one extra manual `glog()` call (`genCtx.getStore()` for `user_id`/`gen_id`, same precedent `weekly_coverage_uncorrected` already used) with `detail: {stage: 'composition', owned_count, item_count}` — no schema change, `detail` is jsonb.
- **Weekly logs a per-day breakdown, not just the week average.** A week-level ratio hides a single day that's almost entirely new-to-buy — the exact failure the query exists to catch. `/api/weekly`'s composition row carries `days: [{label, owned_count, item_count}, …]` alongside the week totals, in the same row (one row, not one per day). `/api/weekly/day` (the single-day restyle) logs its own flat `owned_count`/`item_count` — no per-day array needed since it only ever describes one day.
- **Logging-path audit (done before writing any of the above — an incomplete table would just produce a more detailed wrong answer)**: every Gemini call across every surface — text and background image loops alike — runs through the single wrapped `ai.models.generateContent` (one `GoogleGenAI` instance in the whole codebase, wrapped once at module load, `if (SUPA_SERVICE_KEY)`, no other reassignment). The background image IIFEs (`/api/style`, `/api/daily`, `/api/travel`, `/api/stylenotes/tryon`, `/api/moodboard`) are invoked synchronously inside their route handler *before* `res.json()` returns, so the request's `AsyncLocalStorage` context (`genCtx`) survives into every staggered `setTimeout`-delimited iteration after the response is sent — verified with a standalone repro script, not just reasoning about it, since this is exactly the kind of thing that's easy to get backwards. The apparent oddity in the original query (`/api/daily` showing `has_image` on far more rows than rows with a full JSON payload) isn't a missing-logs bug: only text-model calls get a parsed payload in `response` (image-model calls deliberately reduce to `{has_image}` so base64 never lands in the table), and one Daily generation is exactly one text call plus several image calls — the ratio is the design working as intended, not evidence of a gap. `endpoint`/`status`/`environment` are already consistent by construction: `endpoint` comes from the `/api` middleware's `req.baseUrl + req.path` (or is set explicitly to match on every manual `glog()` call), `status` is drawn from the same small enum everywhere (`ok`/`partial`/`error`/`timeout`), and `environment` is one process-wide constant (`APP_ENV`, derived from `PUBLIC_URL` at boot) — a single deployed instance can never log a mixed environment value.

### 2026-07-21 — Hero Rack woven into the wardrobe (integration pass, parallel session)
Supersedes the presentation half of the 2026-07-14 Hero Rack bullet — the mechanics (star toggle, picker, season-awareness, `hero_position`, cap 10) are unchanged; only how it surfaces:
- **No titled section, no empty-state card, no separate rail**: the `.rb-hero-head` ("The Hero Rack" + echo + count note), the "Which five pieces…" `.rb-hero-empty` invite AND the `#rb-hero` rail itself are gone (design feedback same day: the rail read as separated from the wardrobe by the filter tabs). Heroes live **IN the wardrobe grid**: `_waRender` sorts in-season starred pieces to the front (by `hero_position`); off-season heroes and everything else keep newest-first order — an off-season hero just sits in its normal grid slot with its filled star. Zero heroes = a plain grid, no chrome at all. The grid-card `.rb-star` (hover-reveal desktop, always visible on touch) is the whole feature surface; starring toasts **"Saved to your Hero Rack"** (un-star: "Removed from your Hero Rack") and re-sorts the grid. `__waHeroPicker` is kept but door-less (reachable only via code / the edit-modal expander toggle); the v2 setup/sync guards now key on `#rb-wsub`, not `#rb-hero`.
- **Dashboard tracker strip leads with heroes** (`_waSyncCounts` `#wtrk-items`, so only while the tracker is on the page, <15 items): tiles sort hero-first (by `hero_position`, non-heroes keep newest-first) with a small ink `.wtrk-star` badge on hero tiles.
- **Generation priority**: the daily / weekly (+`/weekly/day`) / travel `wardrobeItems` payloads now carry `hero: true` + `seasons[]` on starred pieces (omitted otherwise — JSON.stringify drops the undefineds). Server: `heroMark(i)` appends `★ HERO (Summer/…)` (untagged → `Year-round`) to the closet lines, and `heroDirective(closetItems)` injects a prioritise-when-occasion-AND-season-match block — wired into `/api/daily` directly, into `weeklyClosetBlocks`' `stateDirective` (covers `/api/weekly` + `/api/weekly/day`), and into `travelSystem` beside its stateDirective (trip heroes answer to the destination's micro-climate, not the home season). `/api/travel/outfits` + `/api/travel/day` are capsule-driven and untouched.
- Verified 2026-07-21 with a Playwright harness (Supabase shim + REST stubs): 18 checks green at 1280px + 390px/touch, incl. the `/api/daily` payload capture.

Built from the admin architecture doc (record-browsing over analytics, custom in-repo page over SaaS). Full reference in the "Admin panel + capture plane" section; this is the day's delta:
- **Migration 11** (`supabase/admin_capture_migration.sql`, run on Robes_p0 2026-07-21): `profiles.is_admin` + `is_admin()`, `events` / `generation_log` / `feedback` tables, admin-read RLS on the existing tables, `admin_user_overview()` RPC (drop-and-recreate — the function gained `p_sort`/`p_dir` the same day, and the old 4-arg signature must be dropped or named-arg RPC calls turn ambiguous). Admin granted to slatteryannie@gmail.com.

Beta/production env filter.
