### 2026-07-12 — LLM experience fixes (key-piece imagery + styling quality)
Beta feedback (mixed genders in generated looks, prompt not reflected in imagery, heads/feet cropped, style icons ignored, missing images, contrived styling moves). All in `server.js` `/api/style` + the kp render paths unless noted:
- **The wearer is decided once, by the text model** — `STYLE_SCHEMA` gained a required `wearer` (`'woman'|'man'`) with the rule that a menswear/unisex *piece* (sportswear, boyfriend jeans) NEVER makes the wearer male; only the user's words do. All three look-image prompts use `one ${wearer}` (they previously said "one person" with no gender, so each of the 3 independently-generated frames could flip gender on a menswear-coded piece). Missing/invalid → defaults to woman.
- **Shared image-prompt fragments** (top of the style section in `server.js`): `FULL_BODY_FRAME` — head-to-toe framing, full head/hair + both shoes visible with margin, never crop face/head/hands/feet (replaces "tall portrait crop", which invited the cropping) — and `styleIconsImageLine(styleIcons)` — injects the user's style icons into the IMAGE prompts (previously DNA/icons steered only the text model). Both applied to: kp look images, Daily anchor shot, Travel hero shot, moodboard `hero_look` frames.
- **The image prompt now carries the user's brief** (first 200 chars) and, when a photo is attached, an explicit role line: reproduce the piece faithfully but compose a new scene — never copy the photo's framing/background/crop (generated frames were inheriting the piece snapshot's composition).
- **Key-piece image generation is strictly sequential** — one generation in flight, 3s between frames, 40s per attempt, ONE retry per frame after an 8s pause (long enough to clear a rate-limit window); a slow call that outlives its race timeout still lands its image on the job (slot taken only if still empty). The original `Promise.all` burst of 3 simultaneous calls with a single 50s race and no retry was the cause of the recurring "2 of 3 images" failures — concurrent calls contend for the image model's rate limit (a mere start-stagger is NOT enough; this is now consistent with daily/travel/moodboard, which were always serial). `_kpPollImages` ceiling raised 90s → 300s to match. NOTE: all beta users share one Gemini key — under multi-user load a frame can still occasionally drop both attempts; the next lever is Gemini quota tier, not code.
- **STYLING SANITY CHECK block** in the `/api/style` brief (after the layer formula): every styling move must honour the key piece's natural register — never belt knitwear/cardigans over athletic shorts, never force waist-cinching/hourglass tricks onto sporty silhouettes, never layer formal tailoring over gym wear. Silhouette/body-architecture DNA rules govern WHAT is selected, never a licence to contort HOW a piece is worn (fixes the "belt the cardigan over Umbro shorts to emphasize the hourglass" class of output — the DNA maxims were being demonstrated on every look).
- **Mobile crop fixes (CSS, not the model)**: (1) kp result card — under 700px the one-column layout put the cover-fit portrait image in a `min-height:300px` landscape box, slicing head + feet; the image now flows at its natural ratio (`position:static;height:auto` !important overrides in `kp-img-style`), min-height kept only as the placeholder backstop. (2) `/board` share page (`board.html`) — the lead image's forced `16/10` box and the `3/4` tiles did the same on phones; under 640px all grid images now render natural-ratio (mobile is a single-column feed), desktop keeps the 3/4 tile grid + spanning lead.

### 2026-07-12 — Look/Rack unification + interaction polish (parallel session)
- `docs/look-rack-template-audit.md` — per-context divergence audit of the Look/Rack templates; implemented as:
- **Shared components in `dashboard-personalize.js`**: `_rbConsole(cfg)` (one canonical Look-panel + Rack renderer, `.rbc-*` classes — Daily/Weekly/Travel day consoles are thin adapters; `data-dlimg`/`data-tvimg` poller contracts unchanged), `_rbDayStrip` (one calendar strip for Weekly + Travel), `_rbFeedbackBlock`/`_rbFeedbackArm` + one `__rbFbRate`/`__rbFbSubmit` pair, `_rbSwapModal` (shared swap modal — Weekly gained Swap; tile tap = swap everywhere, weekly tap-to-anchor retired), `_rbDayPost` (75s-abort POST guard behind `/api/weekly/day` + `/api/travel/day`).
- **Travel gained anchoring** (Anchor CTA per rack row, persisted in `tvData`, `/api/travel/day` holds anchored capsule pieces via an ANCHORED prompt block) and one-tap `__tvRestyleDay`; one restyle CTA everywhere ("↻ Restyle this day" in the rack header). Headers normalised ("The look · {Day} · {x} pieces", "The rack · {Day}"); Weekly console re-skinned to Daily parity + Rename + share flow (incl. weekly-plan branch in `publicSharePayload`); `transition_tip` added to weekly/travel schemas but the tip strip is retired from the render — **"The read" verdict block** closes the Look panel in all three consoles (`_rbcReadBlock`).
- **Rack cards are removable everywhere** — × badge + swipe-left-to-delete (shared delegated handler on `[data-rmfn]`); Daily/Weekly splice the item, Travel removes it from the look's formula only (capsule/packing keep it); two-piece minimum guard. Swipe-to-delete also covers lookbook cards (page grid + dashboard row) → existing `_rbConfirmDelete` modal.
- **"+ Add a piece" is a chooser** on every rack: From your wardrobe (grid picker → `__dlAddOwned`/`__wkAddOwned`/`__tvAddOwnedToLook`), Take a photo, Upload a photo — the camera/upload doors pop the OS picker in one click as soon as the wardrobe modal's step 1 mounts. Travel's day-rack add (`__tvAddPieceToLook`) files the snapped piece as an owned capsule Keep in this slot's formula.

### 2026-07-20 — Layout spec (held canvas, fluid content · Claude Design handoff)
One centred shell, two measures — the width follows the content, not one global rule. Tokens in `dashboard.html` `:root` (+ a mirrored copy in `stylenotes.html`): `--shell:1440px`, `--nav-h:64px` (was 60 — `sn-page`'s inline top now reads the var), and the stepped page gutter `--s6` (80px ≥1200 / 64px 1024–1199 / 40px 768–1023 / 24px <768).
- **Shell**: the nav bar bleeds full-width but its content aligns to the shell via the `.nav-in` wrapper (markup: `.nav > .nav-in > .nav-l + .nav-r` — keep the wrapper, the bar's hairline must span the viewport). `.dash`, `.wardrobe-panel`, the lookbook page, dl/tv/wk wrappers + payoff inners and `.rb-sfoot-in` all sit at `max-width:var(--shell)` with `--s6` padding (the old 900/960/1148/1180 caps are gone except kp's 900 content column, which is deliberate reading measure).
- **Grids hold their columns — never widen cells** (`minmax(0,1fr)` tracks everywhere; plain `1fr` = `minmax(auto,1fr)` and a card's min-content width silently widens its whole column — observed with the add-card at 3-col): wardrobe `.wg-grid` 5/4/3/2 (gap 16), lookbook `#sn-grid` 3/3/2/1 (gap 20), travel Keep/Worth-adding `.tvm-capgrid` 6/4/4/3 (gap 16), stylenotes palette `.g-6` 6 fixed → 3 <768 (gap 14).
- **Reading measure**: `.concierge` (prompt box) max 760px, `.dash-mast` (display headline) max 620px, travel `stylist_summary` max 900px; kp prose already ≤720.
- **Mobile shell is <768px, tablet band 768–1023 is desktop-shaped**: every nav-arch mobile rule (dock, FAB, back pill, header share, footer offsets, `_rbNavSync` matchMedia, stylenotes dock) moved 640 → 767. 768+ keeps the top bar + multi-column grids (portrait iPad never collapses to the phone shell). The ambient weather pill hides <768 and condenses to place·temp at 768–820 (`nth-child` hide of the day + its dot — the pill's span order is fixed, `_rbWeather` fills in place). Because the nav pill is gone <768px, a **mobile-only ambient strip** (`#dash-wx-m`, in the `.dash-mast` under `.dash-echo`) carries the "Monday · Dublin · 20°C" context there — `_rbSyncMobileWx()` (inside the `_rbWeather` IIFE) builds it from `window.__rbCtx` + the weekday and reveals it via a `.on` class only once geolocation + forecast land (never placeholder values); CSS shows `.dash-wx-m.on` at `max-width:767px` only. `.rb-tn-link` gets an invisible `::after` hit-area so nav links clear 44px on touch.

### 2026-07-20 — Nav architecture (anchored destinations · Claude Design handoff)
From the "Mobile Nav Architecture" + "Desktop Nav Architecture" mocks (Claude Design project 2c795d90…): one IA across breakpoints — three destinations (Home / Wardrobe / Lookbook; *four since 2026-09-08 — Lookbook · Diary · Wardrobe · Inspiration, Home kept on the dock*), detail screens keep their parent lit, Style notes sits off the nav (account area). Static markup + CSS in `dashboard.html` (NAV v2 block), behaviour in the `_rbNavArch` IIFE in `dashboard-personalize.js` (after `_rbInitBreadcrumb`), Style notes changes in `stylenotes.html`.
- **Desktop (>640px — ≥768px since the layout spec moved the mobile shell)**: Wardrobe + Lookbook are inline text links (`#rb-topnav`, `.rb-tn-link`) beside the wordmark (which carries Home); active = ink underline. The `#rb-crumb` breadcrumb and the "Wardrobe · N" `.nav-wbtn` pill are retired **in CSS only** (`display:none !important`): `rbSetCrumb` still runs hidden, and the pill MUST stay in the DOM — the wordmark/popstate close paths click it programmatically.
- **Mobile (≤640px at first — now ≤767px per the layout spec)**: fixed glass dock (`#rb-dock`, z-48 — under the nav's 50 and every modal, over the z-40/45 result overlays) with the three tabs and ink top-bar active state ("Ink underline" variant from the mock). **No count badge on the Wardrobe tab** — the final mock removed it (the design doc's closing footnote still claiming the count moves onto the tab is stale; the phones are authoritative). Detail overlays (kp/dl/tv/wk) swap the wordmark line for a **"‹ Lookbook" back pill** (`#rb-backpill`) that closes the overlay and opens the lookbook list. Result-page action bars (`.rb-sfoot`, `.dlm-payoff`, `.tvm-payoff`) get `bottom:86px !important` at ≤767px so genuine actions ride ABOVE the dock (the mock's actions tier); body/panel/sn-page get bottom padding for dock clearance.
- **State sync**: `_rbNavSync` (also `window._rbNavSync`) derives the active destination from DOM visibility — wardrobe panel `.visible` → Wardrobe; `#sn-page` or any kp/dl/tv/wk overlay → Lookbook (every result is a saved lookbook entry); else Home — and runs on a 350ms poll (views open/close through too many independent paths for event wiring). `window.__rbNavGo(dest)` is the single tap handler ('home' reuses the wordmark onclick; 'back'/'lookbook' close overlays then `__snOpen()`; 'wardrobe' opens the panel).
- **Closing the wardrobe panel programmatically: use `App.goHome()`** — `App.showWardrobe()` never toggles closed (verified: repeat calls keep `.visible`); goHome is the one call that reverses the bundle's view-switch without the wordmark handler's hard-navigation fallback. `_closeWardrobe` in `_rbNavArch` does this.
- **Style notes** (`stylenotes.html`): topbar-l is now `ROBES | ‹ Home` (`.sn-back`; wordmark + sep hidden ≤767px so mobile shows just the back pill), the `.sn-wbtn` Wardrobe button is removed, avatar stays dark (account-area marker); its own copy of the dock (nothing lit, real hrefs to /dashboard /wardrobe /lookbook) renders ≤767px.
- **Share + Rename relocation (third pass)**: on mobile detail screens Share rises into the header — `#rb-share-btn` (glass circle beside the avatar, same `window.__rbShare()` flow) shows under the same condition as the back pill (`_rbNavSync`), while the footer Share buttons carry `.rb-share-foot` and hide ≤767px (desktop keeps them). **Rename is a small pencil in the title row at both breakpoints** (`.rb-rename-tbtn` beside `#kp-headline` / `.dlm-title` / `#wk-headline` / `#tv-headline` → `__rbRename('kp'|'dl'|'wk'|'tv')`); the footer Rename buttons are removed everywhere. **All four result surfaces rename identically**: `__rbRenameSave` persists the name as the lookbook card `title` AND writes it onto the render data's `headline` (into `kpData`/`dlData`/`tvData`/`wkData`), and `applyLive` updates the visible headline — so a rename sticks on the page and on every reopen. Key piece has no dynamic `headline` of its own, so `__kpRenderResult` shows `data.headline` as the H1 when a custom name exists (else the editorial default). (Earlier the kp rename only touched the footer meta and dl had no pencil at all — both read as "the name won't stick".)
- **In-screen changes from the same handoff (second pass)**: (1) list headers are the small-caps eyebrow style matching "Your daily look"/"The travel edit" — wardrobe panel `.wg-title` restyled (Inter 500 uppercase `var(--rose)`, `.wg-sub` retired, count vertically centred beside via `.wg-header{align-items:center}`), lookbook page header is "Lookbook" + `#sn-count` "N kept" (filled in `snRenderPage`), and the wardrobe's All pieces/Wishlist sub-tabs (`#rb-wsub`) sit BELOW the title (mock order: title leads), hairlined like the category tabs. **The lookbook has type sub-filters** (`#sn-tabs`, `.sn-ftab` — All / Key pieces / Daily looks / Weekly plans / Travel edits): `_snFilter` + `_snSetFilter` drive `snRenderPage`'s `visible` slice, a filtered-empty state reads "No {type} yet." (`#sn-empty-t`/`-s`), legacy `'look'` rows only show under All, and `__snOpen` always resets to All (wardrobe convention). (2) Style notes chapter tabs lost the 01/02/03 numerals (`.num` spans removed, `.lbl` restyled to the 13px text-tab pattern) and the section eyebrows read plain "Style notes". (3) **Add-a-piece rework**: the `#rb-wg-cta-head` header CTA is hidden on All pieces (it survives as the **wishlist-only** add path — the wishlist hides the toolbar, so don't delete it); desktop adds via `#rb-add-pill` ("+ Add piece", primary ink, `margin-left:auto`) in the filters row with **Refine demoted to outline** (dark only via `.active`); mobile adds via the `#rb-wa-fab` ink FAB (fixed right 18px / bottom 96px, z-47, shown only ≤767px while the panel is `.visible` — a MutationObserver toggles `.on`; `#rb-add-pill` hidden ≤767px). Both add controls are view-aware (`__waAddChooser` / `__wlOpenAdd`). The dashed in-grid add card stays as the contextual slot.
- Verified 2026-07-20 with a Playwright harness (Supabase shim + REST stubs): 60 checks green across 1280px + 900px (tablet band) + 390px on dashboard home/wardrobe/lookbook/detail + stylenotes.

### 2026-07-14 — Wardrobe v2 (Hero Rack · Refine · Wishlist · richer capture)
From the Claude Design "Wardrobe Redesign Brief" handoff — **UX guide only, the mock's visuals were NOT mirrored**; everything renders with the beta's own tokens/classes inside the existing wardrobe panel. One contiguous "Wardrobe v2" block in `dashboard-personalize.js` (after `_waDelete`) + surgical edits to `_waCard`/`_waAddCard`/`_waBuildFilters`/`_waRender`/`WA.submit`/`_waOpenEdit`/step 3. Needs migration 10 (`wardrobe_v2_migration.sql`); every surface degrades gracefully until it runs (see migrations list). Details in the "Wardrobe v2" section under the dashboard wardrobe feature.
- **Hero Rack** (`#rb-hero`, above `#wg-filters`): horizontal rail of numbered larger cards (`.rb-hero-card`, Cormorant numerals) for the ≤10 pieces that define her wardrobe (`wardrobe_items.hero_position`). Star button on every grid card (`.rb-star`, hover-reveal on desktop / always on touch) + picker modal (`__waHeroPicker`) + "Feature in Hero Rack" toggle in the modal expander. **One rack, season-aware**: a hero piece whose `seasons` tags exclude the current season (derived from the month) rests off the rail until its season returns — untagged/Year-round heroes always show. *Presentation superseded 2026-07-21* (see that delta): the rail, title and empty-state card are gone entirely — in-season heroes simply lead the wardrobe grid itself (filled star on their cards), and heroes are prioritised in generation + lead the dashboard tracker strip.
- **Refine drawer** (`#rb-refine`, toggled by a right-aligned always-dark `Refine` pill with funnel icon + active-count badge): **categories are light text tabs** (`.wg-tab`, underline = active — replaced the 10 heavy pills, design feedback: they read cluttered on mobile; the active-class toggles now target `.wg-tab`, and `_waBuildFilters`' rebuild guard keys on `.wg-tab` presence). Drawer: season (standalone Spring/Summer/Autumn/Winter/Year-round multi-select), the **full `_ALL_SWATCHES` palette items are saved to** (now at wardrobe-wiring scope) closed by a **colour-wheel** swatch (`__waRefWheel` — native picker, maps a custom pick to the nearest palette colour), silhouette/fit chips (union of `item_dna.structural_dna.silhouette_fit`), worn/never segmented control, brand dropdown, live "Show N pieces" + Clear all. Untagged pieces count as Year-round in every season filter.
- **Wishlist** (`wishlist_items` table — deliberately separate so wardrobe counts/triggers/closet payloads stay untouched): "All pieces / Wishlist" sub-tabs (`#rb-wsub`) nest it UNDER Wardrobe (crumb `ROBES / Wardrobe / Wishlist`, URL `/wishlist` with deep-link + popstate support; panel always reopens on All pieces). Dashed cards with provenance chips (`source_type`: robes-suggests = sage chip + note block / instagram / substack / screenshot / url / photo). "I bought this" copies the row into `wardrobe_items`, deletes the wishlist row and fires `__rbAddFork`. Save flow (`__wlOpenAdd`): screenshot/photo upload → analyse → confirm (label/brand/price/source) → save; "Paste a link" is a visible Coming-soon door.
- **Richer capture** ("Add more detail" expander, `__rbWaDetailInject` — mounted in add step 3 AND edit mode, state on `window.__rbWaDetail`, wiped by WA.close): merged "When do you wear this" chips (seasons + occasions + custom "+ tag", split into `seasons[]`/`occasions[]` at save), private purchase price with live cost-per-wear line (price / times_worn), fit confidence, sentimental flag ("Irreplaceable" = never suggest letting it go), Hero Rack toggle. `WA.submit` extends the payload with the v2 columns and retries once WITHOUT them on PGRST204 (pre-migration), remembering via `_waV2Cols`.
- **Amplified add entry**: the bare `+` grid card became a weighted card (`.rb-add-card`: + circle, Cormorant "Add a piece", "Photograph · Link · Receipt") opening a method chooser (`__waAddChooser`) — Photograph is live (→ `WA.open()`), Paste a link + Import a receipt are dimmed Coming-soon doors (→ `KP.comingSoon`). The header CTA (`#rb-wg-cta-head`) is now **wishlist-only** ("+ Save a piece") — on All pieces, adding lives in the toolbar `#rb-add-pill` (desktop) and the `#rb-wa-fab` FAB (mobile); see the 2026-07-20 nav architecture section. Phase 2 = making the link + receipt doors real (copy-link import, receipt OCR).

## What this is
Fashion AI styling app. User inputs a key piece (photo, text, or link) → Gemini generates 3 editorial looks with outfit images. Landing page + waitlist live at **www.byrobes.com** (`main`); auth + dashboard closed beta live at **beta.byrobes.com** (`signup-flow`). Both deployed on Railway.

## Stack
- **Backend**: Node.js + Express, ES modules (`"type":"module"`), `server.js`
- **Frontend**: Vanilla HTML/CSS/JS SPA — no framework, no build step
- **AI (styling)**: Google Gemini via `@google/genai`
  - `gemini-2.5-flash` — JSON styling text (3 looks) + wardrobe item analysis
  - `gemini-3.1-flash-image` — editorial outfit images (1 per look)
- **Auth + DB**: Supabase (project: `Robes_p0`, URL: `https://ayowpaknssulsqqvwpqx.supabase.co`)
  - Google OAuth + email/password auth
  - `profiles` table — first_name, style_icons[], budget, wardrobe_description
  - `prompt_history` table — logs every Anthropic API call per user
  - `wardrobe_items` table — per-user clothing pieces with image, category, colour, brand, notes, item_dna
- **AI (wardrobe context)**: Anthropic `claude-sonnet-4-6` via Supabase Edge Function
- **Storage**: Cloudinary (photo uploads + generated images + wardrobe item photos), in-memory `lookStore` Map (48h TTL)
- **CRM**: Airtable — `Contacts` table (email/name), `Feedback` table (every prompt logged)

- `beta.byrobes.com` is a Railway custom domain on the staging service — with the service's source branch set to `beta`, pushing to `beta` deploys it

## Commit signing (agent sessions)
Commits **are** signed — `commit.gpgsign=true` with `gpg.ssh.program=/tmp/code-sign`, the managed environment signer that holds the key. Confirm with `git cat-file commit <sha> | grep gpgsig`, which is the source of truth.
- **`git log %G?` reporting `N` does not mean unsigned.** Fresh containers ship no `ssh-keygen` and no `gpg.ssh.allowedSignersFile`, so git cannot *verify* what it just signed, and tooling that reads `%G?` (e.g. a stop hook) raises a false "Unverified". Every commit on `beta` was signed the whole time.
- To make verification work in a session (`%G?` → `G`), three steps — the managed signer implements only `-Y sign`, so verification must fall through to a real `ssh-keygen`:
  1. `apt-get download openssh-client && dpkg-deb -x openssh-client*.deb /tmp/ssh-extract` → `install -m755 /tmp/ssh-extract/usr/bin/ssh-keygen ~/.local/bin/ssh-keygen` (a plain `apt-get install` fails — unrelated third-party PPAs 403).
  2. A dispatching wrapper at `~/.local/bin/git-ssh-sign`: `exec /tmp/code-sign "$@"` when an arg is `sign`, else `exec ssh-keygen "$@"`. Point `gpg.ssh.program` at it.
  3. Write `~/.config/git/allowed_signers` as `noreply@anthropic.com namespaces="git" <the ed25519 key>` and set `gpg.ssh.allowedSignersFile`. The key is recoverable from any existing signature — the SSHSIG blob embeds it after the 6-byte magic + 4-byte version.
- All of this is **container-local and ephemeral**; it does not change the commits, only whether this machine can verify them. GitHub's own Verified badge depends on the key being registered account-side, which is not settable from inside the container.

## Deploying
```bash
git add <files>
git commit -m "message"
git push -u origin main   # triggers Railway auto-deploy on www.byrobes.com
git push -u origin beta   # triggers Railway auto-deploy on beta.byrobes.com + staging URL (once the service points at beta)
```
No build step. Railway picks up `npm start` → `node server.js`.

## Environment variables (set in Railway)
- `GEMINI_API_KEY`
- `AIRTABLE_TOKEN`, `AIRTABLE_BASE_ID`
- `CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME`, `CLOUDINARY_API_KEY`, `CLOUDINARY_API_SECRET`
- `PUBLIC_URL` = `https://www.byrobes.com` on production, `https://beta.byrobes.com` on the beta/staging service
- `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` — enables the `generation_log` LLM call trail (admin capture plane). Server-only, never shipped to the client. Until it's set the trail is a silent no-op (`/api/health` reports `generation_log: false`)
- `PORT` (Railway sets automatically)

Note: Cloudinary vars must be set on **both** production and beta/staging Railway services for wardrobe photo uploads to work.

### Run-once migrations (Supabase SQL editor, in order)
1–8 are run on `Robes_p0`; 9 shipped with the share flow — verify it has been run (until then sharing degrades gracefully to "try again shortly"). Listed so a fresh project (or a missed one) can be reconciled:
1. `supabase/schema.sql` — profiles + prompt_history + trigger
2. `supabase/wardrobe_schema.sql` — wardrobe_items + RLS
3. `supabase/item_dna_migration.sql` — wardrobe_items.item_dna JSONB
4. `supabase/style_notes_migration.sql` — Style Notes scalar columns on profiles
5. `supabase/style_notes_analysis_migration.sql` — colour_analysis + silhouette_analysis JSONB
6. `supabase/style_dna_migration.sql` — style_dna JSONB + wardrobe_items_count triggers
7. `supabase/onboarding_migration.sql` — profiles.onboarded_at + backfill
8. `supabase/lookbook_migration.sql` — lookbook_items table + RLS
9. `supabase/share_migration.sql` — share_id/is_public on lookbook_items + anon RLS + profiles.instagram_handle
10. `supabase/wardrobe_v2_migration.sql` — Hero Rack + richer capture columns on wardrobe_items (hero_position, seasons[], occasions[], price, fit_confidence, sentiment) + wishlist_items table + RLS. Shipped with Wardrobe v2 (2026-07-14) — until it runs, saves silently retry without the new columns, the Hero Rack toasts "not ready yet" on feature attempts, and the wishlist shows its empty state with saves toasting "try again shortly"
11. `supabase/admin_capture_migration.sql` — admin capture plane (2026-07-21): `profiles.is_admin` + recursion-safe `is_admin()`, `events` + `generation_log` + `feedback` tables (own-insert + admin-read RLS), admin-read policies on profiles/wardrobe_items/wishlist_items/lookbook_items/prompt_history, `admin_user_overview()` RPC. Requires migration 10 first (references wishlist_items). After running, grant yourself admin: `update public.profiles set is_admin = true where id = '<your-auth-uid>';`. Until it runs every capture write fails silently and `/admin` bounces to byrobes.com
12. `supabase/planned_days_migration.sql` — the Diary's dated-day index (2026-07-23): `planned_days` table (one row per MOMENT — day/evening slots, pinned, denormalised rail/month summary, reserved OOTD + anchor columns), unique `(user_id, source_id, day_index, slot)`, own-all + admin-read RLS. Until it runs every planned_days write/read warns once per session and no-ops. After running it, run `scripts/backfill_planned_days.mjs` once with the service key (weekly/travel blobs → rows; daily looks deliberately skipped)
13. `supabase/gender_identity_migration.sql` — gender identity (2026-07-28): `profiles.gender_identity` text not null default `'woman'`, check `('woman','man','unspecified')` — the default covers every new signup and backfills existing rows. Until it runs the app behaves exactly as before ('woman' everywhere): the boot select falls back to a gender-less select, and the Account details save retries without the column
14. `supabase/looks_migration.sql` — the Look entity (2026-07-30): `looks` / `look_pieces` / `wears` tables (see the Look-entity delta section, incl. the preflight guard against the pre-existing empty `public.looks` table). Until it runs Looks degrade to the localStorage cache
15. `supabase/wardrobe_taxonomy_migration.sql` — 3-level taxonomy (2026-07-31), GENERATED by `scripts/gen_taxonomy_migration.mjs` from `wardrobe_taxonomy.js`: nullable `wardrobe_items.category_l2`/`category_l3`, the `wardrobe_taxonomy` lookup table (350 seed rows, pk `(l2, l3)`, authenticated-read RLS), the composite FK backstop and a partial index on `category_l2`. Until it runs saves strip the two columns on PGRST204 and retry (`_waTaxCols`), analyse output simply isn't persisted at L2/L3, and the Refine Type section doesn't render — L1 behaviour is untouched
16. `supabase/look_roles_migration.sql` — drag-cast formula roles (2026-08-07): nullable `look_pieces.role`. Until it runs `_lkPiecesCloud` strips the column on PGRST204 and retries (`_lkRoleCol`) — role casts on saved Looks live in the localStorage cache only; null = the client heuristic decides at render, no backfill
17. `supabase/season_tags_migration.sql` — season bands + shared tag namespace (ADR-002, 2026-08-12), **NOT yet run**: `wardrobe_items.season_band`/`season_source`, `looks.climate_band`/`climate_source`, the `tags` / `tag_pieces` / `tag_looks` namespace, `rb_tag_slug()`, and backfills off `seasons` / `occasions` / `looks.tags` (all three source columns survive read-only for one release). **Run `supabase/season_tags_audit.sql` first — it prints a pass/fail verdict against a pre-committed 25% cross-band threshold — and run migration 15 before Session B.** Schema only: nothing reads these columns yet, so it is revertible on its own (rollback block at the foot of the file). Verify with `DATABASE_URL=… ./scripts/season_tags_migration_test.sh` against a **throwaway** database (48 assertions)
18. `supabase/tag_prefill_migration.sql` — category → tag pre-fill (ADR-002 §6, Session B), **NOT yet run**, requires 15 and 17: GENERATED by `scripts/gen_tag_defaults_migration.mjs` from `CATEGORY_DEFAULTS` in `wardrobe_taxonomy.js`. Seeds `wardrobe_tag_defaults` + `wear_seed_labels`, adds `rb_piece_defaults()` / `rb_apply_tag_defaults()`, an AFTER INSERT trigger on `wardrobe_items`, and a one-shot backfill. **The pre-fill lives in the DB, not the client** — one implementation covers the backfill and every future add, so they cannot drift. It only ever writes `source: 'inferred'` and skips any row already marked `'user'`, so a correction is permanent. Verify with `DATABASE_URL=… ./scripts/tag_prefill_test.sh` (throwaway DB: replays the real chain 3→10→14→15→16→17→18, 16 assertions, trigger, idempotency)
19. `supabase/look_proposals_migration.sql` — proposals on a Robes-built look (2026-08-13), **NOT yet run**: nullable `looks.proposals jsonb` — the shop-row snapshot that lets a saved build reopen whole (mosaic + rack cards). One idempotent `add column if not exists`. Until it runs, `_lkPushCloud`/`_lkPatchCloud` strip the column on PGRST204 and retry (`_lkPropCol`), and proposals persist in the localStorage cache only (single-device)

## Supabase DB schema
**profiles**: `id` (FK → auth.users), `first_name`, `style_icons[]`, `budget`, `wardrobe_description`, `created_at`, `updated_at` (+ Style Notes / DNA / onboarding / share columns via the migrations above, e.g. `style_dna`, `onboarded_at`, `instagram_handle`)
- Auto-created on signup via `handle_new_user` trigger
- RLS: users can only read/write their own row

**prompt_history**: `id`, `user_id` (FK → profiles), `prompt`, `response`, `tokens_used`, `model`, `created_at`
- RLS: users can only read/write their own rows

**wardrobe_items**: `id`, `user_id` (FK → auth.users), `label`, `category`, `color`, `brand`, `notes`, `image_url`, `times_worn`, `item_dna`, `created_at`
- Schema in `supabase/wardrobe_schema.sql` — run once in Supabase SQL editor
- `item_dna` JSONB column added via `supabase/item_dna_migration.sql` — run once
- RLS: users can only read/write their own rows
- CRUD via direct Supabase REST API (not Edge Function) using user JWT

## Edge Function: wardrobe-context
- URL: `https://ayowpaknssulsqqvwpqx.supabase.co/functions/v1/wardrobe-context`
- Auth: pass user's JWT in `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header
- Body: `{ "prompt": "..." }`
- Reads user profile, assembles system prompt, calls `claude-sonnet-4-6`, writes to prompt_history
- Returns: `{ "response": "...", "tokens_used": N }`

## Style Notes page (`/stylenotes`, signup-flow branch)
`public/stylenotes.html` is a standalone protected page (NOT part of the dashboard bundle). Session check via `sbClient.auth.getSession()`; no session → `/signup.html?mode=signin`. **Honours `#silhouette`/`#taste` hashes on load** (`setTab`) — the deep-link target for the dashboard's silhouette prompt. Its analyse fetches carry the same AbortController + 100s stop as onboarding.

**Colour AND silhouette completion are now here, not in onboarding** (beta feedback 2026-07-10 removed the onboarding face scan too). A quiet **below-the-fold dashboard card (`_rbSilPrompt` in `__robes_personalize`)** invites her to finish whatever's missing: copy/CTA adapt to which chapters are absent (both → "Complete my style notes →" → `/stylenotes`; colour only → "Complete my colours →" → `/stylenotes`; silhouette only → "Complete my silhouette →" → `/stylenotes#silhouette`). Gated on `window.__robes_profile.style_dna.color_harmony` / `.silhouette_proportions` being absent, dismissible per-user (`rb_sil_prompt_off__<uid>` in localStorage), inserted before the concierge service cards (delayed 1200ms past `_rbApplyLayout` so the reshuffle can't displace it).

Entry point: the dashboard avatar dropdown — `__robes_personalize` appends an `#av-stylenotes` item (menu order: Account details / Style notes / Log out — Wardrobe + Lookbook were REMOVED from the dropdown once they became first-class nav destinations, and Moodboards is hidden in P0) that navigates to `/stylenotes`. The page's ROBES wordmark routes back to `/dashboard`, keeping it inside the dashboard experience.

The topbar carries the standard global header cluster on the right (`.topbar-r`): weather pill (`#sn-weather`, own lightweight geolocation + Open-Meteo fill), Wardrobe button → `/wardrobe`, and an avatar dropdown (`#sn-av-menu`: Dashboard / Wardrobe / Lookbook / Moodboards / Log out) fed by `profiles.first_name` in `init()` — the page never strips the app chrome.

- Three tabs: 01 Colour harmony, 02 Silhouette & proportions, 03 Taste & budget. Topbar breadcrumb is just `ROBES | Style notes` — the chapter segment was removed (beta feedback 2026-07-10: it duplicated the subnav tabs).
- Tabs 01 + 02 are **analysis-driven with empty states**: before a photo is uploaded the hero shows only the headline + invite copy; every section below is hidden. Presence of `profiles.colour_analysis` / `profiles.silhouette_analysis` (JSONB, via `supabase/style_notes_analysis_migration.sql` — run once) decides empty vs filled — never the scalar columns.
- Photo upload runs two parallel requests: `POST /api/stylenotes/analyse` (`{kind: 'colour'|'silhouette', data, mimeType}`) and `POST /api/wardrobe/upload` (Cloudinary). On success the analysis fills all sections and saves: render JSON to the `*_analysis` column, the DNA fragment merged into `profiles.style_dna`, derived scalars (`season`/`undertone`/`contrast` or `body_type`) + photo URL. On `no_face_detected`/`no_person_detected` an inline error shows under the slot and nothing is saved.

## Style DNA engine (`style_dna.js`, PRD: Style DNA Core Profile Generation)
The analyse endpoint runs **reasoning-first extraction + deterministic catalogs**: Gemini (`gemini-2.5-flash`, `temperature: 0`) writes stylist-grade observation fields FIRST (lighting cast, hair/skin/eye temperature evidence, pose distortions), then a **holistic archetype call** (`season` against the 12-season rubric embedded in the prompt / `body_shape` against the 5-archetype rubric) alongside the measurable primitives (undertone/contrast/chroma/lightness enums; shoulder/waist/hip ratios). Schemas use `propertyOrdering` so observations precede classification — the JSON doubles as chain-of-reasoning. Everything the user sees still comes from the `style_dna.js` catalogs:
- **Reconciliation (deliberate deviation from PRD §4.2/§5.1 primacy)**: `buildColorHarmony`/`buildSilhouette` prefer the model's holistic archetype call; the primitive mapping (`classifySeason`/`classifyBody`) is the deterministic fallback (schema-less retry, old payloads) and a cross-check recorded in `dna.classification` (`source`, `primitive_season`/`ratio_body_type`, `agreement`, `reasoning`). Rationale: the 4-enum mapping discards the holistic evidence a stylist uses — one wrong enum (Cool vs Warm) lands the opposite season family, the exact observed failure (warm muted blonde → True Summer instead of Soft Autumn). If the holistic season's family contradicts the extracted undertone, undertone is coerced to Neutral-Warm/Neutral-Cool so the rendered copy stays coherent.
- The silhouette prompt corrects for **mirror-selfie pose distortion** (a raised phone arm widens that shoulder — the old prompt read hourglass frames as Inverted Triangle) and camera angle before estimating ratios, and only allows Inverted Triangle on an unmistakably broader corrected shoulder line + undefined waist.
- **Extraction runs at `temperature: 0`** (the default 1.0 was sampling different undertone/contrast/chroma enums for the same photo, cascading into a different season on each retry — this is why colour results looked inconsistent). The colour prompt enforces a measurement protocol: neutralise ambient colour cast against the sclera/teeth first, ignore background/clothing/makeup, treat hair temperature (golden vs ash) as first-class undertone evidence. Accuracy ceiling is still the model perceiving colour semantically not colorimetrically — the parked next step is true PRD §4.1 colorimetry (Gemini locates skin/hair/iris/sclera regions, server samples pixels via `sharp`, LAB + sclera white-balance decide undertone/contrast).
- `classifySeason()` — PRD §4.2 mapping matrix → one of 12 seasonal archetypes (`SEASONS` catalog: 18-shade matrix, 6 neutrals, 4 stars + 4 accents, 7 avoid, metals rules, stylist voice from the Colour Harmony matrix sheet). Note: the sheet's Deep Winter/Cool Winter/Cool Summer/Warm Autumn/Deep Autumn names are mapped to the PRD enum's Dark Winter/True Winter/True Summer/True Autumn/Dark Autumn.
- `classifyBody()` — PRD §5.1 width-ratio thresholds → one of 5 body archetypes (`BODIES` catalog from the Master Archetype Registry: 4 dresses + notes, 4 necklines, 4 display tips, 4 full styling maxims). Loose clothing + flat ratios → Rectangle fallback with `fallback_applied: true`. Hourglass shoulder:hip band widened to 0.9–1.1 (photo-estimated ratios are noisy).
- `styleDnaPromptBlock` honours `style_dna.user_overrides` (`loved_colors`, `rejected_colors`, `measurements`) — user corrections outrank the photo-derived profile in every downstream prompt. No UI writes these yet; it's the landing slot for the planned refinement loop.
- `buildColorHarmony()` / `buildSilhouette()` return `{ render, dna }` — render feeds the page, dna is the PRD §3.3 `style_dna` payload (`color_harmony` / `silhouette_proportions` fragments). `low_confidence` lighting sets `refinement_needed: true` and falls back to Neutral-Warm.
- `styleDnaPromptBlock(styleDna, wardrobeCount, styleIcons)` — PRD §6 downstream injection: flattens the DNA into LLM prompts with three postures (audit 2026-07-08 softened the old binary cliff): **0 items** = pure editorial cold-start; **1–14 items** = hybrid — "weave in the pieces they already own by their exact labels wherever an owned piece genuinely serves the look — an owned piece always beats a hypothetical one" (no longer "do NOT over-constrain to their few uploaded items"); **≥15** = State B strict closet matching. `/api/style`'s own `closetDirective` mirrors this: it now fires for **any** owned pieces (`>0`, hybrid) rather than only `daily && >=15`. Wired into `/api/style` (accepts `styleDna`, `wardrobeCount`) and `/api/moodboard` (accepts `styleDna`); `__robes_personalize` passes `_rbStyleDna()` + `_waItems.length` from `window.__robes_profile.style_dna` (added to the dashboard boot select along with `wardrobe_items_count`).
- **Style Icons steer every surface**: the third arg (`profiles.style_icons`, client helper `_rbStyleIcons()`) renders a north-star directive — follow the icons' aesthetic, brands and styling codes without ever contradicting the DNA colour/silhouette rules. Works icons-only (before any photo analysis exists). Wired through `/api/style`, `/api/daily`, `/api/travel`, `/api/travel/day`, `/api/moodboard` (`styleIcons` in every client payload, incl. the onboarding prefire).
- Migration `supabase/style_dna_migration.sql` — run once: `style_dna JSONB` + GIN index, `wardrobe_items_count` + insert/delete trigger on `wardrobe_items` + backfill, vector extension. Deviation from PRD §3.1: existing text columns are NOT converted to Postgres enums (live RLS table); value sets are enforced by the engine.
- Tuning palettes/copy/rules = edit `style_dna.js` only; no prompt or client changes needed. **The seasonal `voice` strings speak as "Robes"** — the old "the AI / the engine / the system" phrasings were removed (no machine-language anywhere the user reads).
- The client downscales photos to max 1600px JPEG before sending (`createImageBitmap` → canvas, FileReader fallback) — keeps analyse + Cloudinary fast and clear of the 20mb body limit.
- Each photo slot shows `.slot-guide` microcopy on lighting/framing (headshot: soft daylight facing a window, no filters/direct sun/coloured light; full-length: straight-on head-to-toe, fitted clothing) — biggest cheap accuracy lever for the analysis.
- `/api/stylenotes/analyse` model chain: default `gemini-2.5-pro` (schema, `thinkingBudget: 1024` — pro can't disable thinking, so it's bounded; `maxOutputTokens` 4096) → `gemini-2.5-flash` (schema, thinking off, 2048) → flash **without** schema (JSON mode + the prompt's field spec — renderers validate shape anyway). **`req.body.fast` flips the order flash-first** (`flash schema → pro schema → flash no-schema`): **onboarding sends `fast: true`** so its once-per-session read lands in seconds (momentum > the marginal judgement gain); Style Notes keeps the careful pro-first chain for its considered re-reads. **Each attempt races a per-model timeout** (pro 45s, flash 25s) so a hung model falls through to the next instead of hanging the request — this was the root cause of the "stuck on Reading your colouring" freeze; the client also aborts at 100s. JSON parsing happens inside the attempt loop, so a truncated pro response falls through to flash instead of 502ing. All attempts failing → 502 `{ error: 'analysis_failed', reason }`; the client logs `reason` and Railway logs carry `[stylenotes/analyse] <model> failed` + the model that answered in `logAI`. **A Gemini account out of quota/credit** surfaces here as a fast `analysis_failed` — check the `reason` and the Gemini billing dashboard before suspecting code.
- Colour analysis JSON drives: 18-hex palette, undertone rows + note, 6 neutrals, 8 best colours, 7 avoid colours + note, proof captions, 3 seen-on-you label pairs, 3 metals (name + 3 gradient hexes) + note. Silhouette drives: body type, 4 traits, 4 dress cards, 5 necklines, 5 tips. All LLM strings pass through `esc()`, hexes through `hex()` validation.
- Loads/saves to `profiles`: `season`, `undertone`, `contrast`, `body_type`, `colour_analysis`, `silhouette_analysis`, `style_icons`, `budget` (tier name), `splurge_categories`, `annual_spend`, `headshot_url`, `full_length_url`. Base columns via `supabase/style_notes_migration.sql` — run once.
- Saves fire immediately on each interaction (`update … eq('id', uid)`); `#save-state` in the top bar shows Saving…/Saved/Couldn't save.
- `#ch-season-row`, `#ch-metrics`, `#sil-type-row` carry inline `display` styles — toggle via `style.display`, NOT the `hidden` attribute (inline display beats it).
- Only two upload affordances exist: headshot (tab 01, 300×380) and full-length photo (tab 02, 300×520). Never make the analysis/try-on frames uploadable.
- **Try-on imagery** (`POST /api/stylenotes/tryon`, `{kind, analysis, data+mimeType | photoUrl}`): after a successful analyse, the client fires a background job that renders the user (identity-preserving `gemini-3.1-flash-image` edit of their own photo) into the proof pair (best vs first avoid colour, colour tab) and the 4 dress silhouettes (silhouette tab, dresses coloured with the user's first star colour when the colour analysis exists). Images generate staggered 3s apart, upload to Cloudinary (URL-only — never base64 into the job), and the client polls `GET /api/images/:jobId` every 4s (`pollTryon`), patching frames as they land and persisting the URLs into `colour_analysis.proof_images` / `silhouette_analysis.dress_images` so they survive reload. `init()` backfills imagery for analyses saved before the feature existed via the stored `headshot_url`/`full_length_url` (Cloudinary-hosted only; once per session via `sessionStorage.rb_tryon_*` so a flaky job can't loop). Frames pulse (`rb-pulse`) while a job is live. Remaining `.ph` frames (seen-on-you, necklines, styling tips) are still editorial placeholders.
- No hex labels under clothing colours, no Accessories/Jewellery/Makeup sections, no "Read once"/privacy strings (explicit design exclusions).
- Selected states are warm cream `#F3EFE6` with `#C9BCA6` border + small dark dot/check — never heavy black fills.

## Onboarding flow (`/onboarding`, signup-flow branch)
`public/onboarding.html` is a standalone protected page (vanilla, no framework) ported from the Claude Design "Robes Onboarding Flow v2" bundle. One state machine (`st.stage`): dark splash (auto-advances 2s) → intro → name → 2 cream working steps (`STEPS = ['icons','keypiece']`) → dark done screen. **No photos of her in onboarding at all** (beta feedback 2026-07-10): the silhouette step went first (optimization 4.7 — a full-length body photo minutes into a first session was the likeliest bail point), then the colour face scan followed; both now live in Style Notes, invited by the dashboard's Style Note prompt card (`_rbSilPrompt`). `renderPhotoStep` remains in the file unused should a photo step return. Step count, progress bar and done-screen dots all derive from `STEPS.length`. All copy/tokens come from the mockup — Cormorant + Inter, `--primary` ink dark arc, cream `#FAF8F5` steps, hairline borders; **CTAs and pills are rounded (100px)** to match the core app (beta feedback — panels/inputs keep the 2px radii). **Typeahead dropdown taps commit via `pointerdown`** and hover only re-classes in place (rebuilding the list mid-tap detached the option under the finger — tapping "Add Zara" did nothing on mobile).

**Gating**: `profiles.onboarded_at` (via `supabase/onboarding_migration.sql` — run once; backfills existing users so only new signups see the flow). Dashboard boot selects it and redirects to `/onboarding` when the profile row loaded AND `onboarded_at` is null AND `sessionStorage.rb_onboarded` is unset (the session flag is a loop guard in case the DB write fails). If the select errors (migration not run), boot retries without the column and does NOT redirect. Email signup with an immediate session goes straight to `/onboarding`; Google OAuth + email-confirmation links still land on `/dashboard` and bounce via the boot check.

**Steps + persistence** (all saves fire immediately via `sbClient.from('profiles').update`):
- Name (dark screen) — prefilled from `profiles.first_name`, falling back to auth `user_metadata` (`first_name`/`given_name`/first token of `full_name`). Saves `first_name` (first word) only when changed.
- Step 01 Icons — typeahead + chips over the `ROBES_ICONS` pool, saves `style_icons` on continue.
- (Removed) Step Colour — the onboarding face scan was removed 2026-07-10 (beta feedback); the colour pipeline (fast-first analyse chain, ledger, Try again path) lives on in Style Notes tab 01.
- Step 02 First piece — wardrobe capture with **no exit gate** (wardrobe-first PRD): photo panel only — the optional "A note for the stylist" field was **removed** (optimization 4.7 — it confused users; the prompt now derives from the analysed label). CTA reads "Style my first piece" (step chrome now reads "Step 02 · First piece"). The "What Robes files" ledger reads The piece / Category / Brand / Colour / Cut. A photo auto-runs `catalogue()` — the same `/api/wardrobe/analyse` + `/api/wardrobe/upload` pair as the dashboard add flow (one retry on `analysisFailed`/missing label), then: row inserted into `wardrobe_items` via `sbClient` (background, promise kept in `st.piece.savePromise`), `prefireStyle()` fires `/api/style` immediately (result → `sessionStorage.rb_onboard_styled`), `writeHandoff()` writes `sessionStorage.rb_onboard_piece = {prompt, photo, cataloged}`, scan-line animation → extracted tags pop staggered (`kpTagPop`) → she continues via the "Continue to your dashboard" CTA. **Before continuing, the CTA awaits the wardrobe insert (capped 1.5s) so the handoff carries `cataloged: true`** — this closes the duplicate-piece-nº1 race (otherwise the dashboard legacy persist path could re-file the same piece). Skip is inert while a read is in flight (so the done screen can't lie). `noItemDetected` clears the photo with an inline error; a hard analysis failure keeps the photo so the CTA can be retried. No photo → nudge; Skip still goes to done.
- Done screen — sets `onboarded_at` + `sessionStorage.rb_onboarded`, CTA "Enter your dashboard" → `/dashboard`. The styled sub-copy reads "your looks are composing and will appear on your dashboard in a moment"; the CTA **holds ~4s** ("Composing your looks…") when the prefire hasn't landed yet, giving the killed-on-navigation `/api/style` a head start so the looks are usually waiting. The Add-to-Home-Screen nudge that once lived here was **removed** (optimization 4.7 — mis-rendered on Chrome/desktop; manifest + icons stay, we just don't prompt).
- **Skip discipline**: on the first-piece step, tapping Skip once with no piece filed arms a two-tap confirm ("Skip anyway — Robes styles only what you add"); a second tap (or Skip when a piece is already filed) goes to done.

**Dashboard handoff — the "Your piece, styled" card**: the landing is **fully silent** — the dashboard boot script must NOT paint a `kp-loading-overlay` for `rb_onboard_piece` (the old full-screen "Styling your piece three ways…" takeover was removed; the card carries the loading state). `_rbOnboardHandoff` IIFE at the end of `__robes_personalize` reads + clears `rb_onboard_piece`/`rb_onboard_styled` and renders an **inline card** (`#rb-styled`) glued under the wardrobe tracker — the relocated wow moment. Loading state (3 pulsing tiles) → ready state (3 look tiles + titles, images patched in by its own light poller on `data.jobId`). On ready the result is **saved straight into the lookbook** (`snAdd` type `key-piece`, id kept in `cardSaveId`, poller patches images via `persistCardImages`) so the Lookbook row shows the key piece populated without her opening anything. **Audit 2026-07-08 — the peak-moment cataloguing loop**: the ready card now leads with a benefit-led primary "Add your next piece →" (dark, `#rb-styled-addnext` → `_wtrkOpenAdd()`) plus a progress nudge ("That's piece N of 15. Add M more and Robes builds every look entirely from your own closet."), and demotes "See the full looks →" (`#rb-styled-open`) to a secondary outline link. The wow is already delivered, so the dominant next action is the next catalogued piece (the WAW driver), not more consumption. `shell()` grew an optional `footer` slot to carry it. "See the full looks →" still opens `window.__kpRenderResult(data, prompt, {intent:'style', skipSave: true, savedId: cardSaveId})` — skipSave prevents a duplicate row. Fresh prefire (<10 min) renders ready instantly; otherwise `quietStyle()` re-fires `/api/style` with no overlay; a hard failure paints a retry card that falls back to the classic `_cbStyleSubmit` overlay flow. The handoff's background wardrobe persist only runs when the payload lacks `cataloged: true` (legacy payloads or an onboarding insert that failed) — step 04 normally saves the item itself, so this guard is what prevents duplicate wardrobe rows.

Every working step has "Skip for now" — the flow never blocks. Only the name screen gates its Continue (disabled until non-empty).

## signup.html conventions
- Supabase client instantiated as `sbClient` (not `supabase` — conflicts with `window.supabase` global from CDN)
- Google OAuth redirects to `/dashboard` after auth
- Email signup: sends confirmation email; if `data.session` exists, redirects immediately
- `setLoading(true)` fires only **after** all client-side validation passes (email/password/firstName/password length) — putting it earlier leaves the submit button stuck on the loading label when validation fails
- **Copy (audit 2026-07-08)**: signup-mode CTA + loading label are "Start my style notes →" / "Creating your style notes…" (was "Reveal my dashboard →" / "Creating your wardrobe…" — the old label was literally false since the email-signup path routes to `/onboarding`, not the dashboard). The signup-mode `#form-subtext` now carries a value prop ("Snap your wardrobe once. Then one prompt gets you dressed for anything.") instead of shipping empty; sign-in mode still clears it.
- Supabase applies its own signup rate limit (~3/hour per email) independent of app code. On error, `rate limit`/`too many` in `error.message` swaps in a friendly message telling the user to wait an hour or use a different email — surfaces during repeated beta-testing with the same address, not a bug
- **Session-aware door**: on load `getSession()` redirects a live session straight to `/dashboard` (so a logged-in user never re-sees the form); the dashboard/onboarding/stylenotes no-session bounces all target `?mode=signin`, and `index.html` sends a logged-in visitor to `/dashboard` (localStorage token check).
- **Error mapping (`friendlyAuthError`)**: raw Supabase strings are humanised — "Email not confirmed" → tap-the-link copy, "Invalid login credentials" → "That email and password don't match", rate-limit/"For security purposes" → wait-a-while. A sign-up for an **already-registered email** (Supabase returns an obfuscated user with `identities: []`, no error) shows "Looks like you already have an account — sign in instead" instead of a false "check your email".
- **Sign-in mode** grows a "Forgotten your password? Reset it" link → `?mode=reset`.
- **`?mode=reset`** (password reset request): the form collapses to email-only (Google + password fields hidden), CTA "Email me a reset link", fires `resetPasswordForEmail(email, {redirectTo: origin + '/reset'})`. The emailed link lands on **`public/reset.html`** — picks up the recovery session (URL-hash tokens, 5s timeout → "link expired" state with a fresh-link path), one new-password field (8-char min, Show toggle) → `updateUser({password})` → `/dashboard`. Requires the Supabase redirect allowlist to include the reset URL (same allowlist as email confirmation).
- iOS suggests a strong password on signup (`autocomplete="new-password"`); sign-in mode swaps it to `current-password`.

## Conversational intent routing (PRD §2 — the inspiration tracks)
The concierge textarea (`#cb-ta`) routes every submit through `_cbSubmit` → `_cbResolve` in `dashboard-personalize.js`:
- **Chip = explicit override.** The three chips (Style a key piece / Dress me today / Create a moodboard) still exist as prompt scaffolds and manual overrides — a deliberate deviation from the PRD's "remove the pills": they double as the clarifying affordance. **Chip templates are short** (optimization 4.7 — the long ones bled off-screen on mobile; beta feedback 2026-07-10 cut Dress-me to ONE bracket): "Style my [cream blazer] three ways", "An outfit for [Sunday brunch]", "A moodboard for [a weekend in Ibiza]". On inject the caret lands on the first `[bracket]` for quick editing.
- **Auto-growing prompt box (`_cbAutoGrow`)**: `#cb-ta` grows to fit its content (capped 240px) on inject and on every keystroke, so template/typed text never clips off the top; `_cbReset` returns it to auto height.
- **Brackets run with their defaults (`_cbSubmit`)**: a submit whose text still contains `[…]` strips the brackets and runs with the suggestion inside them (beta feedback 2026-07-10 — the old hard block left users stuck on first load). The brackets are editable suggestions, never gates.
- **"Add from Wardrobe" in the prompt box (beta feedback)**: the `#cb-photo-zone` attach row (style intent) carries a `#cb-wa-btn` "From wardrobe" pill → `__cbWardrobePick()` (wardrobe grid modal) → `__cbWardrobeApply(id)` injects the piece's exact label into the prompt (replacing the first `[bracket]`, or "Style my {label} three ways" into an empty box) and fetches its Cloudinary photo into `_cbPhotoData` as a dataURL so `/api/style` sees it exactly like an upload.
- **No chip = NL intent extraction.** `_cbDetectIntent(text, hasPhoto)` classifies the free-typed prompt into `'style' | 'dress-me' | 'moodboard' | 'travel' | null`. Priority: a named piece beats the occasion around it ("my Prada shoes to the office today" → key piece, per PRD); a trip beats the occasions inside it ("dinners on my Ibiza trip" → travel), except an explicit "moodboard" ask. A photo attachment always means `'style'`.
- **null = clarifying loop.** `_cbShowClarify(prompt)` renders `#cb-clarify` (four tap options, incl. "Pack for a trip") under the chip row; the typed prompt is preserved and submitted with the chosen track via `_cbResolve`. Never clobber the textarea in this path — `_cbSetIntent` (chips) injects template text, `_cbResolve` does not.
- **Dress-me routes to `/api/daily` at every wardrobe count.** `_cbResolve('dress-me', …)` calls `window.__dlSubmit(prompt)` — the Daily Look track itself handles cold-start (fully aspirational at 0 items, hybrid below 15 with an explanatory toast, closet-first at ≥15). The chip is never locked; the old moodboard reroute is gone.
- **Travel routes to the brief modal, not straight to the API.** `_cbResolve('travel', …)` calls `window.__tvOpen({brief: prompt})` — destination + dates need structure, so the typed prompt lands in the modal's notes field (with a light destination-prefill heuristic).

## Daily Look context + closet injection (`/api/style`)
`/api/style` now accepts `intent` (`'style'` default | `'dress-me'`), `context` (`{city, month, tempRange, condition, hint}`) and `wardrobeItems` (label/category/color/times_worn, capped at 60 server-side):
- `_rbWeather` stores what it fetches in `window.__rbCtx` (`city`, `tempC`, `tempRange` from daily min/max, `condition`, `hint` via `layerHint()`), which `_cbStyleSubmit` snapshots into `context` for dress-me submits. Nav strip painting is unchanged.
- Server: `intent === 'dress-me'` swaps the system brief (three complete outfits for a real day, not three ways around one piece), relaxes the fallback rule (a plain occasion is a valid brief — only gibberish falls back), injects the real-time context line, and — at ≥15 closet items — directs Gemini to build primarily from the digitised wardrobe, referencing owned pieces by exact label.
- Both `/api/style` briefs carry the PRD §3.2 four-tier layer formula (Anchor / Canvas / Texture / Exclamation Point) + hyper-specificity directive.
- `__kpRenderResult(data, promptText, opts)` takes `opts.intent`/`opts.context`: dress-me gets the "Your day, dressed three ways" header, the contextual metadata pill (city · month | temp range | layer hint), and "Today's brief" instead of "Your piece". `intent`+`context` are persisted inside `kpData` so reopened lookbook entries keep the daily framing.

## Daily Look page (`/api/daily` + `__dlRenderResult`) — the Daily Match console
The dress-me track's own surface: ONE complete outfit for the real day. UX amendments 2026-07 restructured the render from the four numbered step sections into a **two-column console** (Context-to-Core still drives the LLM; the steps survive as data + the stylist note's bolded vocabulary). The `/api/style` dress-me path still exists for backward compat with saved lookbook entries.
- **Server `POST /api/daily`**: accepts `{prompt, name, styleDna, wardrobeItems (id/label/category/color/brand/image_url/times_worn, capped 60), context}`. The system prompt encodes the Context-to-Core Framework — 1) Context Filters (agenda/mobility from the brief, atmospheric reality from `context`, psychological goal), 2) Architectural Formula (exactly 4 steps: The Anchor ×1 / The Canvas ×1–2 / The Texture ×1 / The Accents ×2), 3) Golden Ratios (rule of thirds, volume balancing, textural contrast — surfaced in `stylist_summary` + item descriptions), 4) Transition Protocol (`transition_tip`: one subtractive-styling or hardware-swap move). Structured schema (`DAILY_SCHEMA`, gemini-2.5-flash, `thinkingBudget: 0`, 4800 tokens), fallback only on gibberish. Each item also returns **`alternates`** (exactly 2 similar-but-distinct same-slot options with brand/retailer/price — they power the flick-through rail) and the endpoint accepts **`locked`** (anchored pieces a restyle must keep: `{name, category, brand, wardrobe_id}[]`, injected as an ANCHORED PIECES block the model builds around).
- **Wardrobe-state directive** shifts the balance: 0 items = fully aspirational (every item `wardrobe_index: -1` + retailer_hint + price_point — a shopping brief), 1–14 = hybrid (owned pieces referenced by index + exact label wherever they serve, gaps filled aspirationally, owned always beats hypothetical), ≥15 = closet-first (new pieces only for true gaps / the exclamation point). Server resolves `wardrobe_index` → `wardrobe_match {id,label,image_url,color}` per item and assigns a flat `image_index`.
- **Imagery**: one frame per item via the shared `imageJobs` infra — anchor gets the full-look editorial shot (whole outfit, anchor leading), every other item a still-life; staggered 3s, Cloudinary-hosted URLs only, client polls `_dlPollImages` (5-min ceiling — staggered gen is slow) and persists via `_dlPersistImages`.
- **Client render** (`__dlRenderResult(data, promptText, opts)` in dashboard-personalize.js, page `#dl-result-page`, z-index 40 under the nav; classed CSS injected as `_DL_CSS` into `#dl-style` on every render). **Styled to the live platform design system** (amendments 2026-07 refinement): the masthead mirrors the Moodboard header exactly — rose eyebrow "Your daily look", short serif-italic `headline` H1, a keyword row (the occasion), then a meta row with the `.weather-strip`-style pill (🌤 location · month | temp | layer hint) + an occasion tag pill. All panels/cards are cream/white with hairline borders, `var(--rad*)` radii and 100px pill buttons — no dark panels, no sharp corners (the reference mock was an architectural reference, not a style spec). CSS uses the `:root` tokens (`--rose`/`--ink`/`--cream-*`/`--rad*`) so it always tracks the platform. The console grid is `360px | 1fr` — the rack gets the larger share.
- **Left — "The Look"**: a single dark stylist-moodboard panel (Instagram-stylist register): derived mood quote, 2-col tile board of the pieces in wardrobe order (first tile wide; slot label, owned ✓ / "Shop" tag, hover flick arrows, italic "the {piece}"; tap = swap modal), fabric swatch row (`_dlFabric`), palette dots + "N of M from your wardrobe". Beneath it the **interactive stylist note** — `stylist_summary` (plain prose — the framework step-name bolding was removed, optimization 4.7) + transition tip, re-read on every restyle ("re-reads on every restyle" hint in the header).
- **Right — "The Rack"**: product listing ordered top / bottom (or dress) / shoes / bag / accessories / layer (`_dlSlot` sort; `fi` stays the flat steps index so swap/persist/imagery keep working). Each card: image viewport (**garment-slot label** — Top / Bottom / Shoes / Bag, `_dlSlot(it).l`, NOT the architectural role — + "i / n" count), serif name, "✓ In your wardrobe" or brand + retailer · price, flip arrows + dots, and actions **Anchor** / Swap / Shop (affiliate coming-soon). **No jargon (optimization 4.7)**: the rack labels each piece by its slot, and the stylist summary renders as plain prose (the old "The Anchor / The Canvas" bolding was removed). The travel rack mirrors this (its `vslot` also uses `_dlSlot(it).l`, not the formula role). Card image cells are `align-self:start` + `width:100%` so `aspect-ratio` can't inflate their width and overlap the text (was a mobile bug).
- **Add a piece** (`__dlAddPiece`, dashed `+ Add a piece` under the rack): opens `WA.open()` over the page; the new owned piece is appended to the last step and joins the rack + the board on re-render (its wardrobe photo, no generated frame). The board tiles no longer carry a redundant "Shop" tag on unowned pieces — owned pieces still show the ✓.
- **Flick-through** (`__dlFlip(fi, dir)`): cycles `_dlOptions(it)` = original piece (snapshotted once into `it.orig`) → AI `alternates` → up to 4 owned same-category pieces. Applying an owned option sets `wardrobe_match`; an AI alternate clears it; cycling back to `orig` restores everything. Alternates have no imagery — the frame falls back to a serif-initial mono tile (`_dlAltered`), and `data-dlimg` is only emitted for the un-flicked original so the poller never paints the wrong piece. All frames use `data-dlimg="i"` (board + rack both show the same frame) — `_dlSetImage`/`_dlSettlePlaceholder` patch every instance.
- **Anchor + Restyle** (`__dlAnchor(fi)`, `__dlRestyle()`): anchoring locks a piece (dark row border + Anchored pill); "Restyle it" / the payoff's "Dress me again" re-posts `/api/daily` with `locked` = the anchored pieces and `savedId` = the live lookbook entry, so a restyle **evolves the same saved look** (fresh headline/summary/steps patched into it) instead of minting a new row. `__dlSubmit(prompt, opts)` re-marks anchors on the fresh data (by wardrobe id, then name) and restores a dropped `wardrobe_match` client-side.
- **Sticky payoff bar** (`.dlm-payoff`, `position:sticky;bottom:0` as the page's last child): headline + verdict/provenance, buttons Share / **Wear today** (`__dlWear` — PATCHes `times_worn`+1 on every owned piece, once per render) / **Dress me again** (primary). **The footer "Dress me again" (`__dlDressAgain`) now returns to the home concierge prompt** for a fresh brief (`__dlGoBack` → `_cbSetIntent('dress-me')`), NOT a silent re-mix (optimization 4.7). The in-context **"Restyle it"** (next to the stylist note) is still the re-mix (`__dlRestyle`, keeps anchors, evolves the same saved look).
- **Swap** (`__dlSwap(flatIdx)` → `__dlSwapApply`): same PRD 3.B modal as moodboards (category-filtered wardrobe grid, AI-alternative fallback, Snap mine → `WA.open()`, affiliate CTA). Apply overwrites the item's name/brand with the owned piece, clears retailer/price, sets `wardrobe_match`, then `_dlRerender()` (scroll-preserving re-render + `_dlPatchSaved`). Board tiles open the same modal.
- **Persistence**: auto-saved to the lookbook as `type: 'daily-look'` with `dlData` (jobId stripped so a reopened entry never polls a dead job); `__snOpenItem` re-opens via `__dlRenderResult(..., {skipSave: true, savedId})`. Card labels read "Daily look" in all three lookbook surfaces.
- **Reopen must carry `savedId`** — `__snOpenItem` passes `{skipSave: true, savedId: item.id}` and every render's skipSave branch resolves `_xxxActiveSaveId = (opts && opts.savedId) || data.id || null`. The render `data` (kpData/dlData/tvData) has no `id` of its own, so dropping `savedId` leaves the active id null; the next Share then hits `_shareFindOrMake`'s lazy-mint path and `snAdd`s a **duplicate** lookbook row on every share (the key-piece Share-duplication bug — the kp branch was the one missing `savedId`).
- Feedback block posts `surface: 'daily-look'` with occasion/ownership counts to `/api/feedback`.

## Travel Edit (`/api/travel` + `__tvRenderResult`) — Looks-First Capsule Packing
PRD: AI-Powered Capsule Packing & Lookbook Generator, **looks-first restructure 2026-07-30** (stylist insight + beta feedback — see the changelog entry above for the four rules and what it superseded). Looks are the unit: styled once, flat and day-agnostic, then **pinned to days** by the user. The Travel Edit card, the concierge `'travel'` intent, the wardrobe panel's "✈ Pack a trip" multi-select, the moodboard's pack CTA and a Look's `Pack it` all reach the same flow; the Diary intake is the prompt-first door.
- **Intake path (primary)**: `/api/intent` unfurls the prompt to a SLIM panel — where / when / **vibe** crumbs only (`INTENT_SCHEMA` gained `vibe`: the styling mood the prompt itself carries, never invented; editable via `_ikVibeEdit`/`_ikVibeDone`) + a read-only "Plans noted, yours to place" strip when `day_intents` landed; the build-around rack is gated off travel. Commit (`Next · Build the outfits →`, where + start date required) → `_tvBrief {dest, dateFrom, dateTo, brief, vibe, anchors, plans, packLookIds}` → `_tvShowBuildPage()`.
- **The build page** (`_tvShowBuildPage()` → `#tv-build-page`, full-page z-40 overlay, removed by `__rbCloseResultOverlays`/`_rbHideResultPages`/`__tvGoBack`): eyebrow "The travel edit · step 2 of 2", h1 "Build the outfits.", Where/When/Vibe pill crumbs (tap → `__tvOpen({restore:true})` reopens step 1), then plan chips (`__tvPlanToggle`/`__tvPlanAdd`, ≥1 plan gates `__tvBuildGo`), the **plan→look mapping list** (`_tvPlanMapPaint` in `#tv-plan-map` — each plan row assigns a saved Look via an inline LookTile picker (`__tvMapOpen`/`__tvMapPick`/`__tvMapClear`, state `_tvBrief.planMap {plan: lookId}`; one look per plan, picking moves it) or defaults to "✨ Robes styles it"; unmapped Pack-it seeds render a "Packed whole" strip, `__tvLookPackToggle` removes), key-piece browser (category pills + Open-Meteo weather filter + Snap new → `__tvAnchorSnap` reopens the build page with the new piece ticked). `__tvBuildGo` → `_tvBuildClose()` + `_tvGenerate()`; Cancel writes nothing. `_tvBrief` survives every round trip.
- **Modal path**: `__tvOpen({brief, dest, dateFrom, dateTo, anchors, plans, packLookId, suggested, restore})` — step 1 trip fields only (dest / dates / brief, +14/+21 defaults), CTA "Next · Build the outfits →"; `__tvSubmit` → the same build page.
- **`_tvGenerate()`** posts `{destination, dateFrom, dateTo, brief, vibe, plans (UNMAPPED plans only), coveredPlans (plans she mapped a look to), shortlistIds (anchors + packed looks' pieces), suggestedItems, importedLooks (title+occasion+pieces, context-only), userId, genId, name, styleDna, styleIcons, gender, wardrobeItems}` — `vibe` lands in the prompt as "THE VIBE, IN HER WORDS" and rides `data.vibe` into every later `/api/travel/looks` call. Her looks join `data.looks` client-side as `{imported: true, lookId, occasion (the mapped plan, or "Packed whole"), pieces}` — never re-styled.
- **Server engine**: `TRAVEL_SCHEMA` returns **`looks[{occasion,title,how,formula}]`** — no days, no slots. **Named plans are authoritative on count**: `looksTarget` = EXACTLY `plans.length` (unmapped plans, one look each, label verbatim as `occasion`, "never an extra she didn't ask for"); `coveredPlans` render an "OCCASIONS ALREADY COVERED BY HER OWN LOOKS — never style a look for these" block and all plans covered → `looksTarget` 0, prompt demands an empty `looks` array and normalise/validation accept it (the response `plans` echoes unmapped + covered so "+ Style another look" chips keep the full set); only a plan-less trip falls back to the trip-length heuristic (min(12, max(4, min(tripDays+2, 10)))). The 1:3 maths runs against `totalLooks` = generated + packed, and capsule pieces named in `importedLooks` are EXEMPT from the under-use corrective (their wears live in looks the server never sees). `how` is the look's PANEL NOTE, formula `note`s are ROW NOTEs. Every key piece KEPT (`unaccounted()` corrective pass), Worth Adding smallest (≤3 + moodboard picks), pack count derived, `travelUnderusedItems(capsule, looks)` 1:3 validation with one corrective regeneration. Weather (FR-101 fall-through) + imagery (hero + ≤7 stills, `data-tvimg` polling) unchanged. Response adds `looks`, `tripDays`, `plans`.
- **`POST /api/travel/looks`** (`TRAVEL_MORE_SCHEMA`, gemini-2.5-flash, thinking 0, 4000 tokens) replaced `/api/travel/day` + `/api/travel/outfits`: re-mix only from the lean capsule, one look per named occasion, ≤1 `new_item` gap piece (with `bridge`, `item_index = capsule.length`, dropped if unused). Powers "+ Style another look" (`__tvStyleLooks`, occasion chips modal), the intake's day-scoped travel prompts (`__tvStyleLookForDay` — styled then auto-pinned) and legacy deferred saves (zero looks → invite card).
- **Result page** (`__tvRenderResult`) — opens on **Looks**; tabs are the lookbook's Grid|Calendar segment: **Days | Looks | The Rack** (`.tvm-seg`, gated by `artifact.hasCapsuleTab`; `__tvSetTab` maps legacy `'outfits'`→`'days'`, `'edit'`→`'rack'`, scrolls only on a genuine change). `_tvMigrate(data)` converts legacy saves on render (day slot → look pinned to that day). **Days pane**: the calendar strip (`_tvPaintWeek` — DayCard via the pins-aware `_pdRowsTv` + `_dcMoments`/`_dcCard`, eyebrow DAY N, packed modifier; `?daycard=off` → `_rbDayStrip` fallback; unpinned days read *Free*) over the **day console** (`_tvPaintDayConsole` — the shared `_rbConsole` Look panel + Rack, weekly parity; several pinned looks tab like Day/Evening via `__tvDaySetLook`; free day = invitation → `__tvStyleLooks({pinTo})`). **Looks pane**: the looks row (`_tvPaintLooks`, cards composing `_rbLookTile.mosaic/title` + occasion eyebrow + pins line + 📌 Pin to days + trailing "+ Style another look") over the **look console** (`_tvPaintLookConsole` — same console dayless; changes reach every pinned day). Console rack rows carry Pack/+Add + Swap (scoped sheet) + the capsule flick (`_tvConFlip`); imported looks resolve to capsule formulas on render (`_tvMigrate`) and get the identical console — `_tvLookBlock` is only the formula-less fallback. State `_tvSelDayI`/`_tvSelLookI`/`_tvDayLookIdx`; `_tvPaintDetail` = repaint shim over both consoles. External openers (`__tvOpenDay`, rail, planned-day peek) land on the Days tab.
- **Pin to days** (`__tvPinOpen` sheet, `__tvPinToggle`, `__tvPinSync`): day buttons with real dates; unpinning a day deletes that day's scoped swaps; every change persists + re-syncs `planned_days`.
- **Swap, scoped** (`__tvLookSwap(li, fi, di)` through the shared `_rbSwapModal` + new `cfg.headHtml`): candidates = same-category capsule members ("From your case", `_tvCapsulePool`), adoption section for the rest of the wardrobe (joins the case via `_tvCapsuleIndexFor`), Snap mine armed via `__tvLookSnapMine`. Day context defaults **"Only Day N"** → `look.overrides[di][fi]` + "Swapped for Day N only" badge; **"All pinned days"** → `look.slotOverrides[fi]` (clears per-day overrides for that slot); "Back to the styled piece" removes the override at the current scope. Effective piece: `_tvEffCi` = day override → look override → formula. The Travel Rack's `__tvSwap`/`__tvSwapApply` stays identity-swap-everywhere (formula indexes are stable, so no restyle needed — the stale bar is gone).
- **The Travel Rack pane**: "The travel rack." heading + stylist summary + trip-level "The mood" hero tile + 1:3 matrix note (`__tvSelectItem` labels are look occasions) + Keep / Worth Adding `tvm-capgrid` cards (Pack/Packed, + Add with silent wishlist write, Swap) + "+ Add a piece to this trip".
- **Persistence**: `type: 'travel-edit'`, `tvData {capsule, looks (pins/overrides/slotOverrides), tripDays, …}`; `_tvPatchSaved` (now actually defined — it was a missing function on this branch) patches the save + `_pdSyncSaved`. `_pdRowsTv`: pins-aware (first pinned look per date → `day` row, second → `evening`, unpinned → free), legacy branches for old saves. Feedback via `_rbFeedbackBlock/_rbFeedbackArm` (payload adds `pinned`).

## Result-surface chrome — nav offset (amendments 2026-07)
All four generated result surfaces (moodboard / key piece / daily look / travel edit) are `position:fixed` overlays that must sit **below** the sticky nav (`z-index:50`, `--nav-h:64px` since the 2026-07-20 layout spec; the overlays' inline fallbacks still read 60px but the token wins). They render at `top:var(--nav-h)` (the moodboard panel already did; the kp/dl/tv pages were `top:0` and their mastheads hid under the nav — fixed here).
- **Sticky footers removed (2026-07-25).** The shared result-surface action bars — `.rb-sfoot` (moodboard / key piece / weekly), `.dlm-payoff` (daily), `.tvm-payoff` (travel), and the legacy `.look-bar` on the mock `#gd-result` surface — were all deleted (markup + CSS + the `bottom:86px` mobile-dock offsets + `.rb-share-foot`). Most of their CTAs are reachable elsewhere: **Share** on daily/weekly/travel via the inline "Share this look" in The Look, and the header `#rb-share-btn` still covers key-piece on mobile; **Rename** on kp/dl/wk/tv via the header pencil `rb-rename-tbtn` (`__rbRename`); the "start-a-new-X" CTAs via the home service cards / concierge intents. **Deliberately dropped with the footers (no surviving entry point):** moodboard Share/Rename/Pack-a-trip (`App.packFromBoard` now uncalled), key-piece **desktop** Share, and Daily/Weekly **"Wear today"** (`__dlWear`/`__wkWear` now uncalled — one-tap wear logging is gone from those surfaces). The handler functions remain defined (harmless dead code); `App.packFromBoard`, `__dlWear`, `__wkWear`, `__wkPlanAgain`, `__dlDressAgain`, `__tvPackAll` have no remaining callers. The bundle's `renderOutfit` was null-guarded around `#gd-piececount` (that span lived in the removed `.look-bar`).
- **Rename** (`window.__rbRename(kind)` for `'kp'`/`'dl'`/`'wk'`/`'tv'`): the header pencil opens a small modal that renames the active saved lookbook entry (`snUpdate` → title + `_lbCloudPatch`) and patches the live headline (`#tv-headline` / `#kp-headline` / `#wk-headline` / `.dlm-title`). Moodboard keeps the bundle's own rename sheet (`App.openRename` → patched `App.saveRename`, which also drives the slug/URL) — note that sheet was previously only surfaced via the removed footer, so moodboard rename now has no on-surface trigger.

## Feedback loop (PRD §4 — every output)
All three generated surfaces carry the inline 👍/👎 + comment block posting to `/api/feedback` → Airtable `Feedback`:
- Key piece / daily look: block inside `__kpRenderResult`; payload includes `email` (Supabase session), `prompt`, and `looksOutput` JSON (`surface`, `intent`, `context`, look titles, timestamp).
- Moodboard: `_mbInjectFeedback(panel, data)` appends `#mb-fb` to `#moodboard-panel` on every `_mbShowResult` (including saved-board reopens); payload includes `email`, `prompt`, and board metadata in `looksOutput`.
- Since 2026-07-21 every submit path ALSO writes to the Supabase `feedback` table via `_rbFbCloud` (rating + note + `lookbook_item_id`, surfaced in `/admin`) — the Airtable post is continuity, the Supabase row is the source of truth.

## Generation overlays — abort + cancel (`_rbOverlayGuard`)
The full-screen `kp-loading-overlay` used by `_cbStyleSubmit` (`/api/style`) and `__dlSubmit` (`/api/daily`) is guarded so a hung fetch can never trap the user behind the blur: `_rbOverlayGuard(overlay)` returns a `{signal, done, userCancelled, timedOut}` — a **90s hard AbortController**, plus a quiet **"Cancel" link that appears after 15s**. On failure the toast is always product-voice ("Robes couldn't finish those looks…" / timeout copy), never raw server text. The moodboard path (`__mbRunGeneration`) has its own 90s AbortController. Concierge/KP photo attaches are downscaled to 1600px JPEG (`_rbDownscale`) before upload so a raw camera photo isn't a long cellular hang inside the overlay.

## Background image generation (`/api/style`, `/api/moodboard`)
Gemini image generation (`gemini-3.1-flash-image`) takes 20–40s per image — both endpoints respond immediately with text/layout data plus a `jobId`/`mb_job_id`, then generate images in a background `imageJobs` Map (in-memory, 10min TTL) that the client polls via `GET /api/images/:jobId`.
- `/api/style`: images upload to Cloudinary as they land (`cloudinaryUpload` before writing to the job) — the client only ever receives hosted URLs, never base64, so results can be persisted (lookbook) without blowing storage quotas. Falls back to a raw `data:` URL only if the Cloudinary upload itself fails.
- Client polling: `_kpPollImages` (dashboard-personalize.js) starts ~2.5s after render, ticks every 3.5s, swaps each placeholder for the arriving `<img>` with a fade-in, and calls `_kpPersistImages()` on every new URL so the saved lookbook entry gets the images even if the user navigates away before the job finishes.
- `/api/moodboard`: images generate staggered (3s apart) to stay under Gemini's rate limit; `_mbPollImages` ticks every 4s (first poll at 5s) and patches arriving images into the on-screen mosaic **and** the saved moodboard (`panel._savedId`) so a reopened board keeps its imagery.
- Empty mosaic cells are explicit loading states while `mb_job_id`/`jobId` is present: `.rb-tile-loading` adds a small spinner (`.rb-spin`) to every pending cell + a "Creating imagery…" caption on the first, on top of the shared `kpPhPulse` pulse — both mosaic render paths (`_mbShowResult` and the `_mbPollImages` re-render) must keep this markup, and the poll's `done` handler strips it.

## Lookbook + moodboard cloud persistence
